import SwiftUI
import SwiftData
import os

private let storageLogger = Logger(subsystem: Bundle.main.bundleIdentifier ?? "com.chiara.PurlLab", category: "storage")

// MARK: - Versioned Schema & Migration Plan (CRIT-04)

enum PurlLabSchemaV1: VersionedSchema {
    static let versionIdentifier = Schema.Version(1, 0, 0)
    
    static var models: [any PersistentModel.Type] {
        [
            KnittingProject.self,
            RowCounter.self,
            GuideStep.self,
            TimerSession.self
        ]
    }
    
    @Model
    final class KnittingProject {
        var id: UUID
        var title: String
        var designer: String
        var selectedSize: String
        var notes: String
        @Attribute(.externalStorage) var pdfData: Data?
        var createdAt: Date
        var updatedAt: Date
        var totalSecondsWorked: TimeInterval
        var completedAt: Date?
        
        // Gauge (Campione su 10x10 cm)
        var patternGaugeStitches: Double?
        var patternGaugeRows: Double?
        var userGaugeStitches: Double?
        var userGaugeRows: Double?
        var isGaugeRecalculated: Bool = false
        var gaugeRecalculationSummary: String?
        @Attribute(.externalStorage) var originalGuideJSON: Data? = nil
        
        @Relationship(deleteRule: .cascade) var counters: [RowCounter] = []
        @Relationship(deleteRule: .cascade) var guideSteps: [GuideStep] = []
        @Relationship(deleteRule: .cascade) var timerSessions: [TimerSession] = []
        
        init(
            title: String,
            designer: String = "",
            selectedSize: String = "",
            notes: String = "",
            pdfData: Data? = nil
        ) {
            self.id = UUID()
            self.title = title
            self.designer = designer
            self.selectedSize = selectedSize
            self.notes = notes
            self.pdfData = pdfData
            self.createdAt = Date()
            self.updatedAt = Date()
            self.totalSecondsWorked = 0
        }
    }
    
    @Model
    final class GuideStep {
        var id: UUID
        var sectionTitle: String
        var title: String = ""
        var stepType: String = "single_action"
        var instructionText: String
        var subSteps: [String] = []
        var repeatVariations: [String] = []
        var rowsPerRepeat: Int?
        var totalRepeats: Int?
        var stitchCountBefore: Int?
        var stitchCountAfter: Int?
        var targetMeasureCm: Double?
        var sourcePage: Int?
        var sourceQuote: String?
        var isCompleted: Bool
        var orderIndex: Int
        var hasCounter: Bool = false
        var counterLabel: String?
        var counterKind: String? = nil
        var currentStepCount: Int = 0
        var targetStepCount: Int?
        var extraCounterLabels: [String] = []
        var extraCounterValues: [Int] = []
        var completedAt: Date?
        
        var project: KnittingProject?
        
        init(
            sectionTitle: String,
            title: String = "",
            stepType: String = "single_action",
            instructionText: String,
            subSteps: [String] = [],
            repeatVariations: [String] = [],
            rowsPerRepeat: Int? = nil,
            totalRepeats: Int? = nil,
            stitchCountBefore: Int? = nil,
            stitchCountAfter: Int? = nil,
            targetMeasureCm: Double? = nil,
            sourcePage: Int? = nil,
            sourceQuote: String? = nil,
            isCompleted: Bool = false,
            orderIndex: Int = 0,
            hasCounter: Bool = false,
            counterLabel: String? = nil,
            counterKind: String? = nil,
            currentStepCount: Int = 0,
            targetStepCount: Int? = nil
        ) {
            self.id = UUID()
            self.sectionTitle = sectionTitle
            self.title = title
            self.stepType = stepType
            self.instructionText = instructionText
            self.subSteps = subSteps
            self.repeatVariations = repeatVariations
            self.rowsPerRepeat = rowsPerRepeat
            self.totalRepeats = totalRepeats
            self.stitchCountBefore = stitchCountBefore
            self.stitchCountAfter = stitchCountAfter
            self.targetMeasureCm = targetMeasureCm
            self.sourcePage = sourcePage
            self.sourceQuote = sourceQuote
            self.isCompleted = isCompleted
            self.orderIndex = orderIndex
            self.hasCounter = hasCounter
            self.counterLabel = counterLabel
            self.counterKind = counterKind
            self.currentStepCount = currentStepCount
            self.targetStepCount = targetStepCount
        }
    }
    
    @Model
    final class RowCounter {
        var id: UUID
        var title: String
        var currentCount: Int
        var targetCount: Int?
        var stepIncrement: Int
        var resetOnTarget: Bool
        var orderIndex: Int
        
        var project: KnittingProject?
        
        init(
            title: String,
            currentCount: Int = 0,
            targetCount: Int? = nil,
            stepIncrement: Int = 1,
            resetOnTarget: Bool = false,
            orderIndex: Int = 0
        ) {
            self.id = UUID()
            self.title = title
            self.currentCount = currentCount
            self.targetCount = targetCount
            self.stepIncrement = stepIncrement
            self.resetOnTarget = resetOnTarget
            self.orderIndex = orderIndex
        }
    }
    
    @Model
    final class TimerSession {
        var id: UUID
        var startDate: Date
        var endDate: Date?
        var durationSeconds: TimeInterval
        
        var project: KnittingProject?
        
        init(
            startDate: Date = Date(),
            endDate: Date? = nil,
            durationSeconds: TimeInterval = 0
        ) {
            self.id = UUID()
            self.startDate = startDate
            self.endDate = endDate
            self.durationSeconds = durationSeconds
        }
    }
}

typealias KnittingProject = PurlLabSchemaV1.KnittingProject
typealias GuideStep = PurlLabSchemaV1.GuideStep
typealias RowCounter = PurlLabSchemaV1.RowCounter
typealias TimerSession = PurlLabSchemaV1.TimerSession

enum PurlLabMigrationPlan: SchemaMigrationPlan {
    static var schemas: [any VersionedSchema.Type] {
        [PurlLabSchemaV1.self]
    }
    
    static var stages: [MigrationStage] {
        []
    }
}

@main
struct PurlLabApp: App {
    @Environment(\.scenePhase) private var scenePhase
    
    static func createPersistentContainer() throws -> ModelContainer {
        let schema = Schema(versionedSchema: PurlLabSchemaV1.self)
        let modelConfiguration = ModelConfiguration("PurlLabV4", schema: schema, isStoredInMemoryOnly: false)
        return try ModelContainer(for: schema, migrationPlan: PurlLabMigrationPlan.self, configurations: [modelConfiguration])
    }
    
    static func createTemporaryInMemoryContainer() throws -> ModelContainer {
        let schema = Schema(versionedSchema: PurlLabSchemaV1.self)
        let inMemoryConfig = ModelConfiguration("PurlLabV4_InMemory", schema: schema, isStoredInMemoryOnly: true)
        return try ModelContainer(for: schema, configurations: [inMemoryConfig])
    }
    
    static let initialStorageResult: Result<ModelContainer, Error> = {
        do {
            let container = try createPersistentContainer()
            storageLogger.info("ModelContainer persistente inizializzato con successo")
            return .success(container)
        } catch {
            storageLogger.error("Impossibile inizializzare ModelContainer persistente: \(error.localizedDescription)")
            return .failure(error)
        }
    }()
    
    static var sharedModelContainer: ModelContainer? {
        try? initialStorageResult.get()
    }
    
    @State private var storageResult: Result<ModelContainer, Error> = PurlLabApp.initialStorageResult
    @State private var showingTemporaryStoreAlert: Bool = false
    
    init() {
        if let container = PurlLabApp.sharedModelContainer {
            let context = container.mainContext
            WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.configure(with: context)
            TimerManager.shared.configure(with: context)
        }
    }
    
    var body: some Scene {
        WindowGroup {
            switch storageResult {
            case .success(let container):
                ProjectListView()
                    .modelContainer(container)
                
            case .failure(let error):
                StorageErrorBlockingView(
                    error: error,
                    onRetry: {
                        do {
                            let container = try Self.createPersistentContainer()
                            storageLogger.info("Riprova: ModelContainer persistente inizializzato con successo")
                            let context = container.mainContext
                            WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.configure(with: context)
                            TimerManager.shared.configure(with: context)
                            storageResult = .success(container)
                        } catch {
                            storageLogger.error("Riprova fallita: \(error.localizedDescription)")
                            storageResult = .failure(error)
                        }
                    },
                    onRequestTemporaryStore: {
                        showingTemporaryStoreAlert = true
                    }
                )
                .alert("Usa archivio temporaneo?", isPresented: $showingTemporaryStoreAlert) {
                    Button("Annulla", role: .cancel) { }
                    Button("Usa archivio temporaneo", role: .destructive) {
                        do {
                            let inMemoryContainer = try Self.createTemporaryInMemoryContainer()
                            storageLogger.warning("Avvio con archivio temporaneo in memoria confermato dall'utente")
                            let context = inMemoryContainer.mainContext
                            WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.configure(with: context)
                            TimerManager.shared.configure(with: context)
                            storageResult = .success(inMemoryContainer)
                        } catch {
                            storageLogger.error("Inizializzazione archivio temporaneo fallita: \(error.localizedDescription)")
                            storageResult = .failure(error)
                        }
                    }
                } message: {
                    Text("I dati creati in questa sessione NON verranno salvati su disco e andranno persi alla chiusura dell'applicazione. Nessun dato esistente verrà toccato o cancellato.")
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Schermata Bloccante Errore Archivio Dati
struct StorageErrorBlockingView: View {
    let error: Error
    let onRetry: () -> Void
    let onRequestTemporaryStore: () -> Void
    
    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            VStack(spacing: 24) {
                Spacer()
                
                Image(systemName: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
                    .font(.system(size: 64))
                    .foregroundColor(.orange)
                
                Text("Errore Archivio Dati")
                    .font(.title2.bold())
                
                Text("Non è stato possibile aprire l'archivio locale dei progetti. I tuoi dati su disco non sono stati modificati né cancellati.")
                    .font(.body)
                    .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    .padding(.horizontal, 24)
                
                ScrollView {
                    Text(error.localizedDescription)
                        .font(.caption.monospaced())
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                        .padding()
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                        .background(Color(.secondarySystemBackground))
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12))
                }
                .frame(maxHeight: 120)
                .padding(.horizontal, 24)
                
                Spacer()
                
                VStack(spacing: 12) {
                    Button(action: onRetry) {
                        HStack {
                            Image(systemName: "arrow.clockwise")
                            Text("Riprova")
                        }
                        .bold()
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                        .frame(height: 50)
                        .background(Color.accentColor)
                        .foregroundColor(.white)
                        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 14))
                    }
                    
                    Button(action: onRequestTemporaryStore) {
                        Text("Usa archivio temporaneo (i dati non verranno salvati)")
                            .font(.subheadline)
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                            .padding(.vertical, 8)
                    }
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 24)
                .padding(.bottom, 24)
            }
            .navigationBarHidden(true)
        }
    }
}

[2/30] FILE: PurlLab/Models/GuideStep.swift

import Foundation
import SwiftData

// MARK: - GuideStep Metodi e Proprietà Calcolate
extension GuideStep {
    var isDetachedOrDeleted: Bool {
        isDeleted || modelContext == nil
    }
    
    var safeSubSteps: [String] {
        guard !isDetachedOrDeleted else { return [] }
        return subSteps
    }
    
    var safeRepeatVariations: [String] {
        guard !isDetachedOrDeleted else { return [] }
        return repeatVariations
    }
    
    var safeExtraCounterLabels: [String] {
        guard !isDetachedOrDeleted else { return [] }
        return extraCounterLabels
    }
    
    var safeExtraCounterValues: [Int] {
        guard !isDetachedOrDeleted else { return [] }
        return extraCounterValues
    }
    
    /// Determina se questo contatore tiene traccia di maglie invece che di ferri/giri
    var isStitchCounter: Bool {
        if let kind = counterKind?.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).lowercased(), !kind.isEmpty {
            return kind == "stitch" || kind == "maglia" || kind == "maglie" || kind == "punto" || kind == "punti"
        }
        let label = (counterLabel ?? "").trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).lowercased()
        return label.contains("magli") || label.contains("punti") || label.contains("stitch")
    }
    
    /// Calcola l'obiettivo effettivo (se pattern ripetuto moltiplica ferri × ripetizioni)
    var effectiveTargetCount: Int? {
        if let rows = rowsPerRepeat, let reps = totalRepeats, rows > 0, reps > 0 {
            return rows * reps
        }
        return targetStepCount
    }
}

[3/30] FILE: PurlLab/Models/KnittingProject.swift

import Foundation
import SwiftData

// MARK: - Statistiche e Schede di Lavoro
extension KnittingProject {
    /// Calcola il numero totale di ferri lavorati attraverso tutte le schede della guida (o del contatore globale),
    /// escludendo esplicitamente i contatori di maglie (ALTO-08)
    var totalGuideRowsWorked: Int {
        let rowSteps = guideSteps.filter { !$0.isStitchCounter }
        if !rowSteps.isEmpty {
            return rowSteps.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.currentStepCount }
        }
        return counters.first(where: { $0.title.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains("globale") })?.currentCount ?? counters.first?.currentCount ?? 0
    }
    
    /// Calcola il numero totale di maglie lavorate nel progetto finora.
    /// Per ciascuna scheda con ferri lavorati, tiene conto delle maglie per ferro (anche in presenza di aumenti/diminuzioni).
    /// Per le schede che contano maglie (isStitchCounter, es. Avvio maglie), somma direttamente le maglie lavorate.
    var totalStitchesWorked: Int {
        var total = 0
        var lastKnownStitches = 0
        
        let sorted = guideSteps.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex })
        for step in sorted {
            if step.isStitchCounter {
                // Per le schede che contano maglie (es. Avvio maglie, aumenti singoli), il contatore È già il numero di maglie lavorate!
                let sts = step.currentStepCount
                total += sts
                if sts > 0 {
                    lastKnownStitches = max(lastKnownStitches, step.stitchCountAfter ?? sts)
                } else if let target = step.targetStepCount, target > 0 {
                    lastKnownStitches = max(lastKnownStitches, target)
                }
            } else {
                // Per le schede che contano ferri/giri
                let startSts = step.stitchCountBefore ?? (lastKnownStitches > 0 ? lastKnownStitches : 0)
                let endSts = step.stitchCountAfter ?? startSts
                let meaningfulStitches = max(startSts, endSts)
                if meaningfulStitches > 0 { lastKnownStitches = endSts }
                
                let workedRows = step.currentStepCount
                guard workedRows > 0 else { continue }
                guard meaningfulStitches > 0 else { continue }
                
                if startSts == endSts || startSts == 0 || endSts == 0 {
                    total += workedRows * meaningfulStitches
                } else {
                    let target = max(step.targetStepCount ?? workedRows, workedRows)
                    let diff = Double(endSts - startSts)
                    for r in 1...workedRows {
                        let progress = Double(r) / Double(target)
                        let stsInRow = Int(round(Double(startSts) + diff * progress))
                        total += max(1, stsInRow)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        // Includi anche eventuali contatori personalizzati esplicitamente dedicati alle maglie
        for counter in counters {
            let title = counter.title.lowercased()
            if title.contains("magli") || title.contains("punti") || title.contains("stitch") {
                total += counter.currentCount
            }
        }
        
        // Se non ci sono schede guida ma ci sono contatori manuali, stima dalle maglie del campione
        if total == 0 && guideSteps.isEmpty {
            let manualRows = counters.first?.currentCount ?? 0
            if manualRows > 0 {
                let gaugeSts = Int(round(userGaugeStitches ?? patternGaugeStitches ?? 20.0))
                total = manualRows * max(10, gaugeSts * 2)
            }
        }
        
        return total
    }
    
    /// Stima del numero totale di maglie del pattern completo (obiettivo al 100%)
    var targetTotalStitches: Int? {
        var total = 0
        var lastKnownStitches = 0
        let sorted = guideSteps.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex })
        var hasValidTargets = false
        
        for step in sorted {
            guard let targetRows = step.effectiveTargetCount, targetRows > 0 else { continue }
            
            if step.isStitchCounter {
                hasValidTargets = true
                total += targetRows
                lastKnownStitches = max(lastKnownStitches, step.stitchCountAfter ?? targetRows)
            } else {
                let startSts = step.stitchCountBefore ?? (lastKnownStitches > 0 ? lastKnownStitches : 0)
                let endSts = step.stitchCountAfter ?? startSts
                let meaningfulStitches = max(startSts, endSts)
                if meaningfulStitches > 0 { lastKnownStitches = endSts }
                guard meaningfulStitches > 0 else { continue }
                hasValidTargets = true
                
                if startSts == endSts || startSts == 0 || endSts == 0 {
                    total += targetRows * meaningfulStitches
                } else {
                    let diff = Double(endSts - startSts)
                    for r in 1...targetRows {
                        let progress = Double(r) / Double(targetRows)
                        let stsInRow = Int(round(Double(startSts) + diff * progress))
                        total += max(1, stsInRow)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        return hasValidTargets && total > 0 ? total : nil
    }
    
    /// Totale dei ferri obiettivo in tutto il pattern, escludendo i contatori di maglie (ALTO-08)
    var targetTotalRows: Int? {
        let total = guideSteps.filter { !$0.isStitchCounter }.compactMap { $0.effectiveTargetCount }.reduce(0, +)
        return total > 0 ? total : nil
    }
    
    /// Percentuale di avanzamento globale basata sulle maglie lavorate (o sui ferri)
    var globalProgress: Double {
        if let targetSts = targetTotalStitches, targetSts > 0 {
            return min(Double(totalStitchesWorked) / Double(targetSts), 1.0)
        }
        if let targetRows = targetTotalRows, targetRows > 0 {
            return min(Double(totalGuideRowsWorked) / Double(targetRows), 1.0)
        }
        return 0.0
    }
    
    /// Restituisce la scheda operativa attiva (privilegiando schede con contatore effettivo per il lavoro ai ferri)
    func activeWorkingStep(preferredStepId: UUID? = nil) -> GuideStep? {
        let sorted = guideSteps.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex })
        if let preferred = preferredStepId, let match = sorted.first(where: { $0.id == preferred }) {
            return match
        }
        
        // 1. Prima cerca la prima scheda non completata che possiede un contatore o obiettivo ferri
        if let working = sorted.first(where: { !$0.isCompleted && ($0.hasCounter || ($0.targetStepCount != nil && $0.targetStepCount! > 0)) }) {
            return working
        }
        
        // 2. Se tutte le schede con contatore sono completate, la prima non completata
        if let firstIncomplete = sorted.first(where: { !$0.isCompleted }) {
            return firstIncomplete
        }
        return sorted.first
    }
    
    /// Annulla tutti i progressi fatti nel progetto e lo riavvia da capo:
    /// azzera i contatori delle schede guida, i contatori manuali, le sessioni e il tempo lavorato,
    /// e riapre tutti i passaggi come non completati.
    @MainActor
    func resetAllProgress(modelContext: ModelContext? = nil) {
        completedAt = nil
        totalSecondsWorked = 0
        updatedAt = Date()
        
        // Azzera timer attivo se associato a questo progetto
        TimerManager.shared.cancelTimer(for: id)
        
        // Elimina le sessioni del timer registrate finora
        let context = modelContext ?? self.modelContext
        if let context = context {
            for session in timerSessions {
                context.delete(session)
            }
        }
        timerSessions.removeAll()
        
        // Azzera i progressi di ogni scheda della guida
        for step in guideSteps {
            step.currentStepCount = 0
            step.isCompleted = false
            step.completedAt = nil
            if !step.extraCounterLabels.isEmpty {
                step.extraCounterValues = Array(repeating: 0, count: step.extraCounterLabels.count)
            } else {
                step.extraCounterValues.removeAll()
            }
        }
        
        // Azzera tutti i contatori del progetto
        for counter in counters {
            counter.currentCount = 0
        }
        
        // Reset indice salvato in UserDefaults
        UserDefaults.standard.removeObject(forKey: "purl_lab_last_guide_step_\(id)")
        
        try? context?.save()
        
        // Aggiorna step attivo e sincronizza con Apple Watch
        let firstStep = guideSteps.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex }).first
        WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.setActiveStep(firstStep, for: self)
    }
}

[4/30] FILE: PurlLab/Models/RowCounter.swift
================================================================================
import Foundation
import SwiftData

// MARK: - RowCounter Metodi Comportamentali
extension RowCounter {
    func increment() {
        currentCount += stepIncrement
        if let target = targetCount, target > 0, currentCount >= target && resetOnTarget {
            currentCount = 0
        }
    }
    
    func decrement() {
        if currentCount >= stepIncrement {
            currentCount -= stepIncrement
        } else {
            currentCount = 0
        }
    }
    
    func reset() {
        currentCount = 0
    }
}

[6/30] FILE: PurlLab/Models/WatchModels.swift

import Foundation

// MARK: - Clock Abstraction (iniettabile per test deterministici e immediati)
public protocol AppClock: Sendable {
    var now: Date { get }
    func sleep(nanoseconds: UInt64) async throws
}

public struct SystemClock: AppClock {
    public init() {}
    public var now: Date { Date() }
    public func sleep(nanoseconds: UInt64) async throws {
        try await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: nanoseconds)
    }
}

// MARK: - Watch Message Payload
struct WatchMessagePayload: @unchecked Sendable {
    let message: [String: Any]
    init(message: [String: Any]) {
        self.message = message
    }
}

// MARK: - Watch Transport Protocol (per testing e disaccoppiamento)
protocol WatchTransport: AnyObject, Sendable {
    var isReachable: Bool { get }
    func sendMessage(_ message: [String: Any], replyHandler: (([String: Any]) -> Void)?, errorHandler: ((Error) -> Void)?)
    func transferUserInfoFallback(_ userInfo: [String: Any])
}

// MARK: - Watch Command Coordinator
/// Coordinatore puro (senza dipendenze da WatchKit o WCSession) della logica di invio comandi,
/// tracking comandi in volo, deduplicazione, timeout di 8s con fallback e crown debounce.
@MainActor
final class WatchCommandCoordinator {
    private(set) var inFlightCommandsCount: Int = 0
    var transport: (any WatchTransport)?
    var clock: any AppClock = SystemClock()
    var inFlightCommandTimeoutNanoseconds: UInt64 = 8_000_000_000
    
    private var inFlightTimeoutTasks: [String: Task<Void, Never>] = [:]
    private var activeInFlightCommandIds = Set<String>()
    
    var onFullSyncNeeded: (@MainActor () -> Void)?
    var onSyncDataReceived: (@MainActor (Data) -> Void)?
    
    init(transport: (any WatchTransport)? = nil, clock: any AppClock = SystemClock()) {
        self.transport = transport
        self.clock = clock
    }
    
    func beginCrownDebounce() {
        inFlightCommandsCount += 1
    }
    
    func endCrownDebounce() {
        inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
    }
    
    func completeInFlightCommand(commandId: String) {
        guard activeInFlightCommandIds.remove(commandId) != nil else { return }
        if let task = inFlightTimeoutTasks.removeValue(forKey: commandId) {
            task.cancel()
        }
        inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
    }
    
    func resetForTesting() {
        for (_, task) in inFlightTimeoutTasks {
            task.cancel()
        }
        inFlightTimeoutTasks.removeAll()
        activeInFlightCommandIds.removeAll()
        inFlightCommandsCount = 0
        inFlightCommandTimeoutNanoseconds = 8_000_000_000
        clock = SystemClock()
    }
    
    func sendCommand(
        _ command: WatchCommandType,
        projectId: String,
        data: [String: Any] = [:]
    ) {
        guard let transport = self.transport else { return }
        let commandId = UUID().uuidString
        
        var message: [String: Any] = [
            "commandId": commandId,
            "command": command.rawValue,
            "projectId": projectId,
            "timestamp": clock.now.timeIntervalSince1970
        ]
        for (k, v) in data { message[k] = v }
        
        inFlightCommandsCount += 1
        activeInFlightCommandIds.insert(commandId)
        
        let payload = WatchMessagePayload(message: message)
        if transport.isReachable {
            let timeoutTask = Task { @MainActor [weak self] in
                try? await (self?.clock ?? SystemClock()).sleep(nanoseconds: self?.inFlightCommandTimeoutNanoseconds ?? 8_000_000_000)
                guard !Task.isCancelled, let self = self else { return }
                if self.activeInFlightCommandIds.remove(commandId) != nil {
                    self.inFlightTimeoutTasks.removeValue(forKey: commandId)
                    self.inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, self.inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
                    // Fallback transferUserInfo allo scadere del timeout prima di requestFullSync
                    transport.transferUserInfoFallback(message)
                    if command != .requestFullSync {
                        self.onFullSyncNeeded?()
                    }
                }
            }
            inFlightTimeoutTasks[commandId] = timeoutTask
            Self.sendSessionMessageReachable(transport, payload: payload, coordinator: self)
        } else {
            activeInFlightCommandIds.remove(commandId)
            inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
            transport.transferUserInfoFallback(message)
        }
    }
    
    private nonisolated static func sendSessionMessageReachable(
        _ transport: any WatchTransport,
        payload: WatchMessagePayload,
        coordinator: WatchCommandCoordinator?
    ) {
        transport.sendMessage(payload.message, replyHandler: { reply in
            let syncData = reply["syncData"] as? Data
            let error = reply["error"] as? String
            let cmdId = payload.message["commandId"] as? String
            Task { @MainActor in
                guard let coordinator = coordinator else { return }
                if let cmdId = cmdId {
                    coordinator.completeInFlightCommand(commandId: cmdId)
                } else {
                    coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
                }
                
                if let _ = error {
                    // iPhone ha restituito errore (es. notReady): fallback in background per non perdere il comando
                    transport.transferUserInfoFallback(payload.message)
                } else if let syncData = syncData {
                    coordinator.onSyncDataReceived?(syncData)
                }
            }
        }, errorHandler: { _ in
            let cmdId = payload.message["commandId"] as? String
            Task { @MainActor in
                guard let coordinator = coordinator else { return }
                if let cmdId = cmdId {
                    coordinator.completeInFlightCommand(commandId: cmdId)
                } else {
                    coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
                }
            }
            transport.transferUserInfoFallback(payload.message)
        })
    }
}

/// Modello sintetico di un progetto da visualizzare e controllare su Apple Watch
struct WatchProjectSummary: Codable, Identifiable, Sendable {
    let id: String
    var title: String
    var designer: String
    var selectedSize: String
    var totalSecondsWorked: TimeInterval
    var isTimerRunning: Bool
    
    // Scheda e Sezione Operativa Attualmente Lavorata
    var activeStepId: String?
    var currentSectionTitle: String
    var currentStepTitle: String
    var currentStepIndex: Int
    var totalStepsCount: Int
    var isStepCompleted: Bool
    
    // Contatore specifico della sezione
    var counterLabel: String
    var currentCount: Int
    var targetCount: Int?
    
    // Maglie attuali sul ferro per questa sezione
    var currentStitches: Int?
    var stitchesBefore: Int?
    var stitchesAfter: Int?
    
    // Navigazione tra schede
    var canGoPrevious: Bool
    var canGoNext: Bool
    
    // Statistiche globali di progetto (maglie e ferri totali)
    var totalStitchesWorked: Int
    var totalRowsWorked: Int
    
    // Eventuali contatori aggiuntivi sulla scheda
    var extraCounterLabels: [String]
    var extraCounterValues: [Int]
    
    var isTargetReached: Bool {
        if let target = targetCount, target > 0 {
            return currentCount >= target
        }
        return false
    }
    
    var displaySubtitle: String {
        let size = selectedSize.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        return size.isEmpty ? designer : (designer.isEmpty ? size : "\(size) · \(designer)")
    }
    
    /// Testo sintetico sulle maglie (es. "114 maglie" o "4 → 8 maglie", evitando la pill "0 → X")
    var stitchesDescription: String? {
        if let before = stitchesBefore, let after = stitchesAfter, before > 0, before != after {
            let diff = after - before
            let diffStr = diff > 0 ? "+\(diff)" : "\(diff)"
            return "\(before) → \(after) m (\(diffStr))"
        } else if let sts = currentStitches ?? stitchesAfter, sts > 0 {
            return "\(sts) maglie"
        }
        return nil
    }
}

/// Contenitore di sincronizzazione trasmesso via WCSession
struct WatchSyncState: Codable, Sendable {
    var projects: [WatchProjectSummary]
    var activeProjectId: String?
    var timestamp: Date
}

/// Tipi di comandi inviati da Apple Watch a iPhone
enum WatchCommandType: String, Codable {
    case incrementMainCounter
    case decrementMainCounter
    case setMainCounter
    case nextStep
    case previousStep
    case completeStep
    case selectStep
    case incrementExtraCounter
    case decrementExtraCounter
    case startTimer
    case pauseTimer
    case selectProject
    case requestFullSync
}

          "instruction": "Riporta sui ferri da 4 mm le 83 maglie in sospeso della seconda manica e riprendi a diritto 4 maglie al sottomanica. Posiziona il MP di inizio giro al centro delle 4 maglie del sottomanica (87 maglie totali).",

          "repeatVariations": [],

          "totalRepeats": null,

          "totalRepeats": 18,

          "instruction": "Diminuisci 3 maglie nel giro successivo per arrivare a 48 maglie, passa ai ferri da 3 mm e lavora 7 cm a coste 1/1 (circa 20 giri). Intreccia le 48 maglie a coste e nascondi tutte le codine di filo.",
          "subSteps": [
            "Diminuisci 3 maglie uniformemente (da 51 a 48 maglie).",
            "Con i ferri da 3 mm lavora 20 giri (7 cm) a coste 1/1 (*1 dir, 1 rov*).",
            "Intreccia le 48 maglie e nascondi le codine."
