import SwiftUI
import SwiftData

struct GaugeRecalculationView: View {
    @Bindable var project: KnittingProject
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
    @Environment(\.modelContext) private var modelContext
    
    // Campione Pattern (default 21 m x 28 f su 10 cm se non presente)
    @State private var patternStitches: Double = 21.0
    @State private var patternRows: Double = 28.0
    
    // Campione Utente (effettivo)
    @State private var userStitches: Double = 19.0
    @State private var userRows: Double = 26.0
    
    // Stati UI
    @State private var isCalculating: Bool = false
    @State private var recalculationResult: GeneratedGuideResponse?
    @State private var errorMessage: String?
    @State private var showingErrorAlert: Bool = false
    @State private var appliedSuccessfully: Bool = false
    
    private let haptic = UIImpactFeedbackGenerator(style: .medium)
    private let successHaptic = UINotificationFeedbackGenerator()
    
    /// Verifica se il campione dell'utente è identico a quello del pattern
    private var isGaugeIdentical: Bool {
        abs(userStitches - patternStitches) < 0.05 && abs(userRows - patternRows) < 0.05
    }
    
    /// Rapporto maglie e ferri
    private var stitchRatio: Double { userStitches / max(1.0, patternStitches) }
    private var rowRatio: Double { userRows / max(1.0, patternRows) }
    
    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            ScrollView {
                VStack(spacing: 20) {
                    // Header con icona e spiegazione
                    headerCard
                    
                    // Card Campione Pattern vs Campione Utente
                    gaugeInputSection
                    
                    // Diagnosi & Valutazione in tempo reale
                    diagnosisCard
                    
                    // Risultato del ricalcolo / Anteprima
                    if let result = recalculationResult {
                        recalculationResultCard(result: result)
                    }
                    
                    // Ripristino se già ricalcolato in precedenza
                    if project.isGaugeRecalculated && recalculationResult == nil {
                        previouslyRecalculatedCard
                    }
                }
                .padding()
            }
            .background(Color(.systemGroupedBackground))
            .navigationTitle("Adattatore Campione AI")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .cancellationAction) {
                    Button("Chiudi") { dismiss() }
                }
            }
            .onAppear {
                initValuesFromProject()
            }
            .alert("Errore Ricalcolo", isPresented: $showingErrorAlert) {
                Button("OK", role: .cancel) {}
                Button("Usa Ricalcolo Locale") {
                    runLocalRecalculation()
                }
            } message: {
                Text(errorMessage ?? "Si è verificato un errore imprevisto.")
            }
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Inizializzazione Valori
    private func initValuesFromProject() {
        if let ps = project.patternGaugeStitches, ps > 0 {
            patternStitches = ps
        } else {
            patternStitches = 21.0
        }
        
        if let pr = project.patternGaugeRows, pr > 0 {
            patternRows = pr
        } else {
            patternRows = 28.0
        }
        
        if let us = project.userGaugeStitches, us > 0 {
            userStitches = us
        } else {
            userStitches = 19.0
        }
        
        if let ur = project.userGaugeRows, ur > 0 {
            userRows = ur
        } else {
            userRows = 26.0
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Header
    private var headerCard: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 8) {
            Image(systemName: "gauge.with.needle")
                .font(.system(size: 40))
                .foregroundColor(.accentColor)
                .padding(.top, 4)
            
            Text("Calibrazione Tensione & Campione")
                .font(.title3.bold())
                .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
            
            Text("Inserisci il campione ottenuto con il tuo filato. Se non coincide con quello del pattern, l'AI ricalcola maglie di avvio, aumenti raglan, ferri e ripetizioni per mantenere perfette le proporzioni del progetto\(project.selectedSize.isEmpty ? "" : " per la taglia **" + project.selectedSize + "**").")
                .font(.footnote)
                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
                .padding(.horizontal, 8)
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
        .padding()
        .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
        .cornerRadius(16)
    }
    
    // MARK: - Input Sezione Campioni
    private var gaugeInputSection: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 16) {
            // 1. Campione Pattern
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
                HStack {
                    Label("Campione del Pattern (Originale)", systemImage: "doc.text")
                        .font(.subheadline.bold())
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                    Spacer()
                    Text("10 × 10 cm")
                        .font(.caption2.bold())
                        .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                        .padding(.vertical, 4)
                        .background(Color(.systemGray5))
                        .cornerRadius(6)
                }
                
                HStack(spacing: 16) {
                    GaugeField(label: "Maglie", value: $patternStitches, unit: "m", step: 0.5)
                    GaugeField(label: "Ferri / Giri", value: $patternRows, unit: "f", step: 0.5)
                }
            }
            .padding()
            .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
            .cornerRadius(16)
            
            // 2. Il Mio Campione
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
                HStack {
                    Label("Il Mio Campione (Tensione Reale)", systemImage: "hand.raised")
                        .font(.subheadline.bold())
                        .foregroundColor(.accentColor)
                    Spacer()
                    Text("10 × 10 cm")
                        .font(.caption2.bold())
                        .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                        .padding(.vertical, 4)
                        .background(Color.accentColor.opacity(0.15))
                        .foregroundColor(.accentColor)
                        .cornerRadius(6)
                }
                
                HStack(spacing: 16) {
                    GaugeField(label: "Maglie", value: $userStitches, unit: "m", step: 0.5)
                    GaugeField(label: "Ferri / Giri", value: $userRows, unit: "f", step: 0.5)
                }
            }
            .padding()
            .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
            .cornerRadius(16)
        }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Diagnosi in Tempo Reale
    private var diagnosisCard: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 14) {
            if isGaugeIdentical {
                // Campione Identico
                VStack(spacing: 10) {
                    HStack(spacing: 10) {
                        Image(systemName: "checkmark.circle.fill")
                            .font(.title2)
                            .foregroundColor(.green)
                        
                        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                            Text("Campione Perfetto!")
                                .font(.headline)
                                .foregroundColor(.green)
                            Text("La tensione del tuo filato coincide al 100% con il pattern (\(formatNum(patternStitches)) m × \(formatNum(patternRows)) f).")
                                .font(.footnote)
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        }
                    }
                    
                    Text("Non è necessario alcun ricalcolo. Puoi lavorare il cartamodello originale con le indicazioni previste dal designer.")
                        .font(.caption)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
                        .padding(.top, 4)
                }
                .padding()
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                .background(Color.green.opacity(0.12))
                .cornerRadius(14)
            } else {
                // Campione Differente
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 12) {
                    HStack {
                        Image(systemName: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
                            .foregroundColor(.orange)
                        Text("Tensione Differente Rilevata")
                            .font(.headline)
                        Spacer()
                    }
                    
                    HStack(spacing: 12) {
                        DiffPill(
                            title: "Maglie",
                            original: patternStitches,
                            actual: userStitches,
                            diffPct: (stitchRatio - 1.0) * 100,
                            unit: "m"
                        )
                        
                        DiffPill(
                            title: "Ferri",
                            original: patternRows,
                            actual: userRows,
                            diffPct: (rowRatio - 1.0) * 100,
                            unit: "f"
                        )
                    }
                    
                    Text(diagnosticExplanation)
                        .font(.footnote)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        .padding(.vertical, 2)
                    
                    // Azione di Ricalcolo AI
                    Button {
                        runGeminiRecalculation()
                    } label: {
                        HStack(spacing: 8) {
                            if isCalculating {
                                ProgressView()
                                    .progressViewStyle(CircularProgressViewStyle(tint: .white))
                                Text("Ricalcolo con Gemini AI in corso...")
                                    .bold()
                            } else {
                                Image(systemName: "sparkles")
                                    .font(.headline)
                                Text("Ricalcola Tutto il Pattern con AI")
                                    .font(.headline)
                            }
                        }
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                        .padding(.vertical, 14)
                        .background(Color.accentColor)
                        .foregroundColor(.white)
                        .cornerRadius(12)
                    }
                    .disabled(isCalculating)
                    
                    // Opzione Ricalcolo Locale Istantaneo
                    Button {
                        runLocalRecalculation()
                    } label: {
                        HStack(spacing: 6) {
                            Image(systemName: "bolt.fill")
                            Text("Ricalcola con Motore Matematico Istantaneo (Locale)")
                        }
                        .font(.caption.bold())
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                        .padding(.top, 2)
                    }
                    .disabled(isCalculating)
                }
                .padding()
                .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
                .cornerRadius(16)
            }
        }
    }
    
    private var diagnosticExplanation: String {
        var parts: [String] = []
        if userStitches < patternStitches {
            parts.append("Il tuo filato produce meno maglie per 10 cm: senza modifiche il capo risulterebbe più largo. L'AI ridurrà le maglie avviate e le ripetizioni per preservare le misure sartoriali in cm.")
        } else if userStitches > patternStitches {
            parts.append("Il tuo filato produce più maglie per 10 cm: senza modifiche il capo risulterebbe più stretto. L'AI aumenterà proporzionalmente le maglie per garantire la vestibilità desiderata.")
        }
        if abs(userRows - patternRows) >= 0.5 {
            parts.append("I ferri verranno riadattati nelle sezioni a misura fissa e nei giri di sagomatura.")
        }
        return parts.joined(separator: " ")
    }
    
    // MARK: - Scheda Risultato Ricalcolo
    @ViewBuilder
    private func recalculationResultCard(result: GeneratedGuideResponse) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
            HStack {
                Image(systemName: "checkmark.seal.fill")
                    .font(.title2)
                    .foregroundColor(.green)
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                    Text("Pattern Ricalcolato con Successo!")
                        .font(.headline)
                        .foregroundColor(.green)
                    Text(project.selectedSize.isEmpty ? "Guida ricalibrata sul tuo campione" : "Taglia \(project.selectedSize) ricalibrata sul tuo campione")
                        .font(.caption)
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                }
                Spacer()
            }
            
            if let summary = result.recalculationSummary, !summary.isEmpty {
                Text(summary)
                    .font(.footnote)
                    .padding(10)
                    .background(Color(.systemGray6))
                    .cornerRadius(8)
            }
            
            // Dettagli sezioni generate
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                Text("Sezioni ricalcolate:")
                    .font(.caption.bold())
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                
                ForEach(result.sections, id: \.name) { sec in
                    HStack {
                        Image(systemName: "circle.fill")
                            .font(.system(size: 6))
                            .foregroundColor(.accentColor)
                        Text(GeminiService.cleanSectionName(sec.name))
                            .font(.footnote)
                        Spacer()
                        Text("\(sec.steps.count) schede")
                            .font(.caption2)
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                }
            }
            
            // Bottone Applica
            Button {
                applyRecalculatedGuide(result)
            } label: {
                HStack {
                    Image(systemName: "arrow.triangle.2.circlepath")
                    Text("Applica Guida Ricalcolata al Progetto")
                        .bold()
                }
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                .padding(.vertical, 14)
                .background(Color.green)
                .foregroundColor(.white)
                .cornerRadius(12)
            }
        }
        .padding()
        .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
        .cornerRadius(16)
        .overlay(
            RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16)
                .stroke(Color.green.opacity(0.4), lineWidth: 1.5)
        )
    }
    
    // MARK: - Scheda Già Ricalcolato
    private var previouslyRecalculatedCard: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
            HStack {
                Image(systemName: "sparkles")
                    .foregroundColor(.indigo)
                Text("Progetto Attualmente Ricalibrato")
                    .font(.subheadline.bold())
                Spacer()
            }
            
            if let summary = project.gaugeRecalculationSummary {
                Text(summary)
                    .font(.caption)
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
            
            Button(role: .destructive) {
                restoreOriginalGuide()
            } label: {
                HStack {
                    Image(systemName: "arrow.uturn.backward")
                    Text("Ripristina Pattern Originale (\(formatNum(patternStitches)) m × \(formatNum(patternRows)) f)")
                }
                .font(.footnote.bold())
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                .padding(.vertical, 8)
            }
        }
        .padding()
        .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
        .cornerRadius(16)
    }
    
    // MARK: - Logica Esecuzione Ricalcolo
    private func runGeminiRecalculation() {
        haptic.impactOccurred()
        isCalculating = true
        errorMessage = nil
        
        let pTitle = project.title
        let pDesigner = project.designer
        let pSize = project.selectedSize
        let sorted = project.guideSteps.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex })
        let snapshots: [GaugeStepSnapshot] = sorted.enumerated().map { idx, step in
            GaugeStepSnapshot(
                stepId: step.id.uuidString,
                index: idx,
                sectionTitle: step.sectionTitle,
                title: step.title,
                stepType: step.stepType,
                instruction: step.instructionText,
                subSteps: step.safeSubSteps,
                repeatVariations: step.safeRepeatVariations,
                rowsPerRepeat: step.rowsPerRepeat,
                totalRepeats: step.totalRepeats,
                stitchCountBefore: step.stitchCountBefore,
                stitchCountAfter: step.stitchCountAfter,
                targetMeasureCm: step.targetMeasureCm,
                hasCounter: step.hasCounter,
                counterLabel: step.counterLabel,
                counterKind: step.counterKind,
                targetStepCount: step.targetStepCount,
                sourcePage: step.sourcePage,
                sourceQuote: step.sourceQuote
            )
        }
        
        let pSts = patternStitches
        let pRws = patternRows
        let uSts = userStitches
        let uRws = userRows
        
        Task {
            do {
                let response = try await GeminiService.shared.recalculateGuideForGauge(
                    patternTitle: pTitle,
                    designer: pDesigner,
                    targetSize: pSize,
                    snapshots: snapshots,
                    patternStitches: pSts,
                    patternRows: pRws,
                    userStitches: uSts,
                    userRows: uRws
                )
                
                await MainActor.run {
                    self.recalculationResult = response
                    self.isCalculating = false
                    self.successHaptic.notificationOccurred(.success)
                }
            } catch {
                await MainActor.run {
                    self.isCalculating = false
                    self.errorMessage = error.localizedDescription
                    self.showingErrorAlert = true
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    private func runLocalRecalculation() {
        haptic.impactOccurred()
        guard let result = GeminiService.recalculateGuideLocally(
            project: project,
            patternStitches: patternStitches,
            patternRows: patternRows,
            userStitches: userStitches,
            userRows: userRows
        ) else {
            self.errorMessage = "Campione non valido: i valori del campione devono essere maggiori di zero."
            self.showingErrorAlert = true
            return
        }
        self.recalculationResult = result
        self.successHaptic.notificationOccurred(.success)
    }
    
    // MARK: - Applicazione al Progetto
    private func applyRecalculatedGuide(_ result: GeneratedGuideResponse) {
        do {
            try GaugeGuideManager.applyRecalculatedGuide(to: project, result: result, modelContext: modelContext)
            project.patternGaugeStitches = patternStitches
            project.patternGaugeRows = patternRows
            project.userGaugeStitches = userStitches
            project.userGaugeRows = userRows
            try? modelContext.save()
            WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.syncAllProjectsToWatch()
            haptic.impactOccurred()
            successHaptic.notificationOccurred(.success)
            dismiss()
        } catch {
            errorMessage = error.localizedDescription
            showingErrorAlert = true
        }
    }
    
    private func restoreOriginalGuide() {
        do {
            try GaugeGuideManager.restoreOriginalGuide(for: project, modelContext: modelContext)
            try? modelContext.save()
            WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.syncAllProjectsToWatch()
            haptic.impactOccurred()
            successHaptic.notificationOccurred(.success)
            dismiss()
        } catch {
            errorMessage = error.localizedDescription
            showingErrorAlert = true
        }
    }
    
    private func formatNum(_ val: Double) -> String {
        val.truncatingRemainder(dividingBy: 1) == 0 ? "\(Int(val))" : String(format: "%.1f", val)
    }
}

// MARK: - Componenti Grafici Secondari
private struct GaugeField: View {
    let label: String
    @Binding var value: Double
    let unit: String
    let step: Double
    
    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
            Text(label)
                .font(.caption)
                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
            
            HStack {
                Text("\(formatNum(value)) \(unit)")
                    .font(.title3.monospacedDigit().bold())
                Spacer()
                Stepper("", value: $value, in: 5...60, step: step)
                    .labelsHidden()
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, 10)
            .padding(.vertical, 6)
            .background(Color(.tertiarySystemGroupedBackground))
            .cornerRadius(10)
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
    }
    
    private func formatNum(_ val: Double) -> String {
        val.truncatingRemainder(dividingBy: 1) == 0 ? "\(Int(val))" : String(format: "%.1f", val)
    }
}

private struct DiffPill: View {
    let title: String
    let original: Double
    let actual: Double
    let diffPct: Double
    let unit: String
    
    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 4) {
            Text(title)
                .font(.caption2.bold())
                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                .textCase(.uppercase)
            
            HStack(spacing: 4) {
                Text("\(formatNum(actual))")
                    .font(.headline.bold())
                Text("vs \(formatNum(original)) \(unit)")
                    .font(.caption)
                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
            
            Text(String(format: "%+.1f%%", diffPct))
                .font(.caption2.bold())
                .padding(.horizontal, 6)
                .padding(.vertical, 2)
                .background(diffColor.opacity(0.15))
                .foregroundColor(diffColor)
                .cornerRadius(4)
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
        .padding(10)
        .background(Color(.tertiarySystemGroupedBackground))
        .cornerRadius(10)
    }
    
    private var diffColor: Color {
        abs(diffPct) < 0.1 ? .green : (diffPct > 0 ? .blue : .orange)
    }
    
    private func formatNum(_ val: Double) -> String {
        val.truncatingRemainder(dividingBy: 1) == 0 ? "\(Int(val))" : String(format: "%.1f", val)
    }
}

// MARK: - Errori e Gestione Snapshot / Ricalcolo Guida
enum GaugeRecalculationError: LocalizedError {
    case emptySections
    case snapshotCreationFailed
    case noSnapshotAvailable(String)
    
    var errorDescription: String? {
        switch self {
        case .emptySections:
            return "La guida ricalcolata non contiene sezioni valide."
        case .snapshotCreationFailed:
            return "Impossibile creare lo snapshot della guida originale. Operazione annullata."
        case .noSnapshotAvailable(let msg):
            return msg
        }
    }
}

struct GaugeGuideManager {
    static func isNoFrillsProject(_ project: KnittingProject) -> Bool {
        let titleLower = project.title.lowercased()
        return titleLower.contains("no frills") || titleLower.contains("nofrills")
    }
    
    static func makeOriginalGuideSnapshotData(for project: KnittingProject) -> Data? {
        let sortedSteps = project.guideSteps.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex })
        guard !sortedSteps.isEmpty else { return nil }
        
        var sectionsDict: [String: [GeneratedStep]] = [:]
        var sectionNames: [String] = []
        
        for step in sortedSteps {
            if sectionsDict[step.sectionTitle] == nil {
                sectionsDict[step.sectionTitle] = []
                sectionNames.append(step.sectionTitle)
            }
            let genStep = GeneratedStep(
                stepId: step.id.uuidString,
                title: step.title,
                stepType: step.stepType,
                instruction: step.instructionText,
                subSteps: step.safeSubSteps,
                repeatVariations: step.safeRepeatVariations,
                rowsPerRepeat: step.rowsPerRepeat,
                totalRepeats: step.totalRepeats,
                stitchCountBefore: step.stitchCountBefore,
                stitchCountAfter: step.stitchCountAfter,
                targetMeasureCm: step.targetMeasureCm,
                hasCounter: step.hasCounter,
                counterLabel: step.counterLabel,
                counterKind: step.counterKind,
                targetStepCount: step.targetStepCount,
                sourcePage: step.sourcePage,
                sourceQuote: step.sourceQuote
            )
            sectionsDict[step.sectionTitle]?.append(genStep)
        }
        
        let originalGuide = GeneratedGuideResponse(
            patternTitle: project.title,
            designer: project.designer,
            targetSize: project.selectedSize,
            gauge: nil,
            needles: nil,
            recalculationSummary: nil,
            changedStepTitles: nil,
            sections: sectionNames.map { GeneratedSection(name: $0, steps: sectionsDict[$0] ?? []) }
        )
        
        return try? JSONEncoder().encode(originalGuide)
    }
    
    static func updateGuideStepInPlace(_ existing: GuideStep, cleanSection: String, stepData: GeneratedStep) {
        existing.sectionTitle = cleanSection
        if let title = stepData.title { existing.title = title }
        if let type = stepData.stepType { existing.stepType = type }
        existing.instructionText = stepData.instruction
        if let sub = stepData.subSteps { existing.subSteps = sub }
        if let rep = stepData.repeatVariations { existing.repeatVariations = rep }
        existing.rowsPerRepeat = stepData.rowsPerRepeat
        existing.totalRepeats = stepData.totalRepeats
        existing.stitchCountBefore = stepData.stitchCountBefore
        existing.stitchCountAfter = stepData.stitchCountAfter
        existing.targetMeasureCm = stepData.targetMeasureCm
        if let hc = stepData.hasCounter { existing.hasCounter = hc }
        existing.counterLabel = stepData.counterLabel
        if let ck = stepData.counterKind { existing.counterKind = ck }
        existing.targetStepCount = stepData.targetStepCount
        if let sp = stepData.sourcePage { existing.sourcePage = sp }
        if let sq = stepData.sourceQuote { existing.sourceQuote = sq }
        
        // Riallinea progresso: se targetStepCount cambia, limita currentStepCount e ricalcola isCompleted
        if let target = existing.targetStepCount, target > 0 {
            if existing.currentStepCount > target {
                existing.currentStepCount = target
            }
            let completed = existing.currentStepCount >= target
            if completed != existing.isCompleted {
                existing.isCompleted = completed
                existing.completedAt = completed ? (existing.completedAt ?? Date()) : nil
            }
        }
    }
    
    static func applyRecalculatedGuide(
        to project: KnittingProject,
        result: GeneratedGuideResponse,
        modelContext: ModelContext? = nil
    ) throws {
        guard !result.sections.isEmpty else {
            throw GaugeRecalculationError.emptySections
        }
        
        // Salva uno snapshot della guida originale prima del primo ricalcolo
        if !project.isGaugeRecalculated && project.originalGuideJSON == nil {
            guard let snapshotData = makeOriginalGuideSnapshotData(for: project) else {
                throw GaugeRecalculationError.snapshotCreationFailed
            }
            project.originalGuideJSON = snapshotData
        }
        
        project.isGaugeRecalculated = true
        project.gaugeRecalculationSummary = result.recalculationSummary
        project.updatedAt = Date()
        
        let newStepsData = result.sections.flatMap { sec in
            sec.steps.map { (cleanSection: GeminiService.cleanSectionName(sec.name), stepData: $0) }
        }
        let existingSteps = project.guideSteps.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex })
        
        let hasIdentityMatch: Bool = {
            guard existingSteps.count == newStepsData.count else { return false }
            let existingIds = Set(existingSteps.map { $0.id.uuidString })
            let newIds = newStepsData.compactMap { $0.stepData.stepId }
            return newIds.count == existingSteps.count && Set(newIds) == existingIds
        }()
        
        let hasPositionalMatch: Bool = {
            guard existingSteps.count == newStepsData.count else { return false }
            for i in 0..<existingSteps.count {
                let existing = existingSteps[i]
                let stepData = newStepsData[i].stepData
                if existing.stepType != (stepData.stepType ?? "single_action") || existing.sourcePage != stepData.sourcePage {
                    return false
                }
            }
            return true
        }()
        
        if hasIdentityMatch {
            let dataById = Dictionary(uniqueKeysWithValues: newStepsData.compactMap { item -> (String, (cleanSection: String, stepData: GeneratedStep))? in
                guard let id = item.stepData.stepId else { return nil }
                return (id, item)
            })
            for existing in existingSteps {
                if let (cleanSection, stepData) = dataById[existing.id.uuidString] {
                    updateGuideStepInPlace(existing, cleanSection: cleanSection, stepData: stepData)
                }
            }
        } else if hasPositionalMatch {
            for i in 0..<existingSteps.count {
                let existing = existingSteps[i]
                let (cleanSection, stepData) = newStepsData[i]
                updateGuideStepInPlace(existing, cleanSection: cleanSection, stepData: stepData)
            }
        } else {
            for oldStep in project.guideSteps {
                modelContext?.delete(oldStep)
            }
            project.guideSteps.removeAll()
            
            var orderIndex = 0
            for (cleanSection, stepData) in newStepsData {
                let step = GuideStep(
                    sectionTitle: cleanSection,
                    title: stepData.title ?? "",
                    stepType: stepData.stepType ?? "single_action",
                    instructionText: stepData.instruction,
                    subSteps: stepData.subSteps ?? [],
                    repeatVariations: stepData.repeatVariations ?? [],
                    rowsPerRepeat: stepData.rowsPerRepeat,
                    totalRepeats: stepData.totalRepeats,
                    stitchCountBefore: stepData.stitchCountBefore,
                    stitchCountAfter: stepData.stitchCountAfter,
                    targetMeasureCm: stepData.targetMeasureCm,
                    sourcePage: stepData.sourcePage,
                    sourceQuote: stepData.sourceQuote,
                    orderIndex: orderIndex,
                    hasCounter: stepData.hasCounter ?? false,
                    counterLabel: stepData.counterLabel,
                    counterKind: stepData.counterKind,
                    currentStepCount: 0,
                    targetStepCount: stepData.targetStepCount
                )
                step.project = project
                project.guideSteps.append(step)
                orderIndex += 1
            }
        }
    }
    
    static func restoreOriginalGuide(
        for project: KnittingProject,
        modelContext: ModelContext? = nil
    ) throws {
        var originalGuide: GeneratedGuideResponse?
        if let data = project.originalGuideJSON,
           let decoded = try? JSONDecoder().decode(GeneratedGuideResponse.self, from: data) {
            originalGuide = decoded
        } else if isNoFrillsProject(project),
                  let guideUrl = Bundle.main.url(forResource: "NoFrillsGuide_M", withExtension: "json"),
                  let guideData = try? Data(contentsOf: guideUrl),
                  let decoded = try? JSONDecoder().decode(GeneratedGuideResponse.self, from: guideData) {
            originalGuide = decoded
        }
        
        guard let guide = originalGuide, !guide.sections.isEmpty else {
            throw GaugeRecalculationError.noSnapshotAvailable("Impossibile trovare lo snapshot della guida originale per questo progetto.")
        }
        
        let originalStepsData = guide.sections.flatMap { sec in
            sec.steps.map { (cleanSection: GeminiService.cleanSectionName(sec.name), stepData: $0) }
        }
        let existingSteps = project.guideSteps.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex })
        
        let hasIdentityMatch: Bool = {
            guard existingSteps.count == originalStepsData.count else { return false }
            let existingIds = Set(existingSteps.map { $0.id.uuidString })
            let origIds = originalStepsData.compactMap { $0.stepData.stepId }
            return origIds.count == existingSteps.count && Set(origIds) == existingIds
        }()
        
        let hasPositionalMatch: Bool = {
            guard existingSteps.count == originalStepsData.count else { return false }
            for i in 0..<existingSteps.count {
                let existing = existingSteps[i]
                let stepData = originalStepsData[i].stepData
                if existing.stepType != (stepData.stepType ?? "single_action") || existing.sourcePage != stepData.sourcePage {
                    return false
                }
            }
            return true
        }()
        
        if hasIdentityMatch {
            let dataById = Dictionary(uniqueKeysWithValues: originalStepsData.compactMap { item -> (String, (cleanSection: String, stepData: GeneratedStep))? in
                guard let id = item.stepData.stepId else { return nil }
                return (id, item)
            })
            for existing in existingSteps {
                if let (cleanSection, stepData) = dataById[existing.id.uuidString] {
                    updateGuideStepInPlace(existing, cleanSection: cleanSection, stepData: stepData)
                }
            }
        } else if hasPositionalMatch {
            for i in 0..<existingSteps.count {
                let existing = existingSteps[i]
                let (cleanSection, stepData) = originalStepsData[i]
                updateGuideStepInPlace(existing, cleanSection: cleanSection, stepData: stepData)
            }
        } else {
            for oldStep in project.guideSteps {
                modelContext?.delete(oldStep)
            }
            project.guideSteps.removeAll()
            
            var orderIndex = 0
            for (cleanSection, stepData) in originalStepsData {
                let step = GuideStep(
                    sectionTitle: cleanSection,
                    title: stepData.title ?? "",
                    stepType: stepData.stepType ?? "single_action",
                    instructionText: stepData.instruction,
                    subSteps: stepData.subSteps ?? [],
                    repeatVariations: stepData.repeatVariations ?? [],
                    rowsPerRepeat: stepData.rowsPerRepeat,
                    totalRepeats: stepData.totalRepeats,
                    stitchCountBefore: stepData.stitchCountBefore,
                    stitchCountAfter: stepData.stitchCountAfter,
                    targetMeasureCm: stepData.targetMeasureCm,
                    sourcePage: stepData.sourcePage,
                    sourceQuote: stepData.sourceQuote,
                    orderIndex: orderIndex,
                    hasCounter: stepData.hasCounter ?? false,
                    counterLabel: stepData.counterLabel,
                    counterKind: stepData.counterKind,
                    currentStepCount: 0,
                    targetStepCount: stepData.targetStepCount
                )
                step.project = project
                project.guideSteps.append(step)
                orderIndex += 1
            }
        }
        
        project.originalGuideJSON = nil
        project.isGaugeRecalculated = false
        project.gaugeRecalculationSummary = nil
        project.userGaugeStitches = nil
        project.userGaugeRows = nil
        project.updatedAt = Date()
    }
}

================================================================================

import SwiftUI
import SwiftData

/// Pagina mostrata al termine della guida: riepilogo del lavoro svolto e del tempo impiegato.
struct GuideCompletionView: View {
    @Bindable var project: KnittingProject
    var onReviewGuide: () -> Void
    var onExit: () -> Void
    var onRestartFromBeginning: (() -> Void)? = nil
    
    @Environment(\.modelContext) private var modelContext
    @State private var showingRestartAlert = false
    
    private var sortedSteps: [GuideStep] {
        project.guideSteps.sorted { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex }
    }
    
    private var completedCount: Int {
        sortedSteps.filter(\.isCompleted).count
    }
    
    /// Ferri/giri contati: schede con pattern ripetuto o lavorate a misura
    private func isRowCounter(_ step: GuideStep) -> Bool {
        step.rowsPerRepeat != nil || step.stepType == "work_to_measure" || step.stepType == "repeat_pattern"
    }
    
    private var rowsCounted: Int {
        sortedSteps.filter { $0.hasCounter && isRowCounter($0) }.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.currentStepCount }
    }
    
    private var stitchesCounted: Int {
        sortedSteps.filter { $0.hasCounter && !isRowCounter($0) }.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.currentStepCount }
    }
    
    private var startDate: Date {
        sortedSteps.compactMap(\.completedAt).min() ?? project.createdAt
    }
    
    private var endDate: Date {
        project.completedAt ?? Date()
    }
    
    private var daysSpan: Int {
        let cal = Calendar.current
        let d = cal.dateComponents([.day], from: cal.startOfDay(for: project.createdAt), to: cal.startOfDay(for: endDate)).day ?? 0
        return max(1, d + 1)
    }
    
    private struct SectionSummary: Identifiable {
        let id: String
        let title: String
        let total: Int
        let completed: Int
        let rows: Int
        let finalStitches: Int?
    }
    
    private var sectionSummaries: [SectionSummary] {
        var titles: [String] = []
        for s in sortedSteps where !titles.contains(s.sectionTitle) { titles.append(s.sectionTitle) }
        return titles.map { title in
            let steps = sortedSteps.filter { $0.sectionTitle == title }
            let rows = steps.filter { $0.hasCounter && isRowCounter($0) }.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.currentStepCount }
            let finalSts = steps.compactMap(\.stitchCountAfter).last(where: { $0 > 0 })
            return SectionSummary(
                id: title,
                title: title,
                total: steps.count,
                completed: steps.filter(\.isCompleted).count,
                rows: rows,
                finalStitches: finalSts
            )
        }
    }
    
    private var extraCountersSummary: [(String, Int)] {
        var result: [(String, Int)] = []
        for step in sortedSteps {
            let labels = step.safeExtraCounterLabels
            let values = step.safeExtraCounterValues
            for (i, label) in labels.enumerated() {
                let v = i < values.count ? values[i] : 0
                result.append((label, v))
            }
        }
        for counter in project.counters.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex }) where counter.currentCount > 0 {
            result.append((counter.title, counter.currentCount))
        }
        return result
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            ScrollView {
                VStack(spacing: 22) {
                    // Intestazione celebrativa
                    VStack(spacing: 10) {
                        Image(systemName: "party.popper.fill")
                            .font(.system(size: 56))
                            .foregroundStyle(.orange, .pink)
                            .padding(.top, 20)
                        Text("Hai finito!")
                            .font(.system(size: 34, weight: .bold, design: .rounded))
                        Text("\(project.title)\(project.selectedSize.isEmpty ? "" : " · Taglia \(project.selectedSize)")")
                            .font(.title3.weight(.semibold))
                            .foregroundColor(.accentColor)
                        if !project.designer.isEmpty {
                            Text("di \(project.designer)")
                                .font(.subheadline)
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        }
                    }
                    
                    // Statistiche principali
                    LazyVGrid(columns: [GridItem(.flexible()), GridItem(.flexible())], spacing: 12) {
                        StatTile(icon: "clock.fill", color: .orange,
                                 value: TimerManager.shared.formatTime(project.totalSecondsWorked),
                                 label: "Tempo di lavoro")
                        StatTile(icon: "checkmark.circle.fill", color: .green,
                                 value: "\(completedCount)/\(sortedSteps.count)",
                                 label: "Schede completate")
                        StatTile(icon: "line.3.horizontal", color: .indigo,
                                 value: "\(rowsCounted)",
                                 label: "Ferri/giri contati")
                        StatTile(icon: "number", color: .blue,
                                 value: "\(stitchesCounted)",
                                 label: "Maglie contate")
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                    
                    // Date
                    HStack(spacing: 0) {
                        DateBlock(title: "Iniziato", date: project.createdAt)
                        Divider().frame(height: 36)
                        DateBlock(title: "Finito", date: endDate)
                        Divider().frame(height: 36)
                        VStack(spacing: 2) {
                            Text("\(daysSpan)")
                                .font(.headline.monospacedDigit())
                            Text(daysSpan == 1 ? "giorno" : "giorni")
                                .font(.caption2)
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        }
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                    }
                    .padding(.vertical, 12)
                    .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16))
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                    
                    // Riepilogo per sezione
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 10) {
                        Text("Cosa hai lavorato")
                            .font(.headline)
                        
                        ForEach(sectionSummaries) { sec in
                            HStack(alignment: .top, spacing: 12) {
                                Image(systemName: sec.completed == sec.total ? "checkmark.circle.fill" : "circle.dashed")
                                    .foregroundColor(sec.completed == sec.total ? .green : .secondary)
                                    .font(.title3)
                                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 3) {
                                    Text(sec.title)
                                        .font(.subheadline.bold())
                                    HStack(spacing: 10) {
                                        Text("\(sec.completed)/\(sec.total) schede")
                                        if sec.rows > 0 {
                                            Text("• \(sec.rows) ferri/giri")
                                        }
                                        if let sts = sec.finalStitches {
                                            Text("• \(sts) maglie")
                                        }
                                    }
                                    .font(.caption)
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                }
                                Spacer()
                            }
                            .padding(12)
                            .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
                            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12))
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                    
                    if !extraCountersSummary.isEmpty {
                        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                            Text("Altri contatori")
                                .font(.headline)
                            ForEach(Array(extraCountersSummary.enumerated()), id: \.offset) { _, item in
                                HStack {
                                    Text(item.0)
                                    Spacer()
                                    Text("\(item.1)")
                                        .bold()
                                        .monospacedDigit()
                                }
                                .font(.subheadline)
                                .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                                .padding(.vertical, 8)
                                .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
                                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 10))
                            }
                        }
                        .padding(.horizontal)
                    }
                    
                    // Azioni
                    VStack(spacing: 10) {
                        Button(action: onExit) {
                            Label("Torna ai progetti", systemImage: "house.fill")
                                .font(.headline)
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                .frame(height: 50)
                                .background(Color.accentColor)
                                .foregroundColor(.white)
                                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16))
                        }
                        Button(action: onReviewGuide) {
                            Text("Rivedi la guida")
                                .font(.subheadline.bold())
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                .frame(height: 44)
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        
                        Button(role: .destructive) {
                            showingRestartAlert = true
                        } label: {
                            Label("Ricomincia da capo", systemImage: "arrow.counterclockwise")
                                .font(.subheadline.bold())
                                .foregroundColor(.red)
                                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                .frame(height: 44)
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal)
                    .padding(.bottom, 24)
                }
            }
            .background(Color(.systemGroupedBackground))
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarTrailing) {
                    Button {
                        onReviewGuide()
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "xmark.circle.fill")
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                }
            }
            .alert("Ricomincia da capo", isPresented: $showingRestartAlert) {
                Button("Annulla", role: .cancel) { }
                Button("Ricomincia da capo", role: .destructive) {
                    project.resetAllProgress(modelContext: modelContext)
                    if let onRestart = onRestartFromBeginning {
                        onRestart()
                    } else {
                        onReviewGuide()
                    }
                }
            } message: {
                Text("Sei sicuro di voler ricominciare \"\(project.title)\" da capo? Tutti i progressi, i contatori e il tempo registrato verranno azzerati.")
            }
        }
    }
}

private struct StatTile: View {
    let icon: String
    let color: Color
    let value: String
    let label: String
    
    var body: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
            Image(systemName: icon)
                .foregroundColor(color)
                .font(.title3)
            Text(value)
                .font(.system(.title2, design: .rounded, weight: .bold))
                .minimumScaleFactor(0.6)
                .lineLimit(1)
            Text(label)
                .font(.caption)
                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
        .padding(14)
        .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
        .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16))
    }
}

private struct DateBlock: View {
    let title: String
    let date: Date
    
    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: 2) {
            Text(date.formatted(.dateTime.day().month(.abbreviated).year()))
                .font(.subheadline.bold())
            Text(title)
                .font(.caption2)
                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
    }
}

================================================================================

import SwiftUI
import SwiftData

enum GuideDisplayMode: String, CaseIterable {
    case card = "Scheda"
    case list = "Elenco Sezioni"
}

struct GuideView: View {
    @Bindable var project: KnittingProject
    @Binding var currentStepIndex: Int
    @Environment(\.modelContext) private var modelContext
    var onSelectPdfPage: (Int) -> Void
    var onExitProject: () -> Void = {}
    
    @State private var displayMode: GuideDisplayMode = .card
    @State private var showingGenerateSheet = false
    @State private var showingAddStepSheet = false
    @State private var showingGaugeSheet = false
    @State private var sectionToDelete: String? = nil
    @State private var showingDeleteSectionAlert = false
    @State private var errorMessage: String?
    @State private var showingCompletion = false
    
    private var sortedSteps: [GuideStep] {
        project.guideSteps.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex })
    }
    
    struct SectionGroup: Identifiable {
        let id: String
        let title: String
        let steps: [GuideStep]
    }
    
    private var orderedSections: [SectionGroup] {
        var orderedTitles: [String] = []
        for step in sortedSteps {
            if !orderedTitles.contains(step.sectionTitle) {
                orderedTitles.append(step.sectionTitle)
            }
        }
        
        return orderedTitles.map { title in
            SectionGroup(
                id: title,
                title: title,
                steps: sortedSteps.filter { $0.sectionTitle == title }
            )
        }
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        Group {
            if project.isDeleted || project.modelContext == nil {
                Color.clear
            } else if project.guideSteps.isEmpty {
                    ContentUnavailableView {
                        Label("Nessuna Guida", systemImage: "sparkles")
                    } description: {
                        Text(project.pdfData != nil ? "Genera la guida interattiva passo-passo dal PDF con l'AI oppure crea le tue schede manualmente." : "Crea le tue schede operative e i contatori manualmente.")
                    } actions: {
                        VStack(spacing: 12) {
                            if project.pdfData != nil {
                                Button {
                                    showingGenerateSheet = true
                                } label: {
                                    Label("Sviluppa Guida da PDF con AI", systemImage: "sparkles")
                                        .bold()
                                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                        .padding(.vertical, 4)
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
                                
                                Button {
                                    showingAddStepSheet = true
                                } label: {
                                    Label("Aggiungi Scheda Manualmente", systemImage: "plus.circle")
                                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                        .padding(.vertical, 4)
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(.bordered)
                            } else {
                                Button {
                                    showingAddStepSheet = true
                                } label: {
                                    Label("Aggiungi Scheda Manualmente", systemImage: "plus.circle")
                                        .bold()
                                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                        .padding(.vertical, 4)
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
                            }
                        }
                        .padding(.horizontal, 24)
                    }
                } else {
                    VStack(spacing: 0) {
                        if project.completedAt != nil {
                            Button {
                                showingCompletion = true
                            } label: {
                                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                                    Image(systemName: "checkmark.seal.fill")
                                    Text("Progetto completato · Vedi riepilogo")
                                        .font(.subheadline.bold())
                                    Spacer()
                                    Image(systemName: "chevron.right")
                                        .font(.caption.bold())
                                }
                                .padding(.horizontal, 14)
                                .padding(.vertical, 10)
                                .background(Color.green.opacity(0.15))
                                .foregroundColor(.green)
                                .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12))
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            .padding(.horizontal)
                            .padding(.top, 8)
                        }
                        
                        // Banner Guida Ricalcolata per Campione Utente
                        if project.isGaugeRecalculated {
                            Button {
                                showingGaugeSheet = true
                            } label: {
                                HStack(spacing: 8) {
                                    Image(systemName: "sparkles")
                                        .foregroundColor(.indigo)
                                    let sts = project.userGaugeStitches ?? 0
                                    let rws = project.userGaugeRows ?? 0
                                    let stsStr = sts.truncatingRemainder(dividingBy: 1) == 0 ? "\(Int(sts))" : String(format: "%.1f", sts)
                                    let rwsStr = rws.truncatingRemainder(dividingBy: 1) == 0 ? "\(Int(rws))" : String(format: "%.1f", rws)
                                    Text("Guida ricalibrata sul tuo campione (\(stsStr) m × \(rwsStr) f)")
                                        .font(.caption.bold())
                                        .foregroundColor(.indigo)
                                    Spacer()
                                    Text("Dettagli")
                                        .font(.caption2.bold())
                                        .foregroundColor(.accentColor)
                                }
                                .padding(.horizontal, 14)
                                .padding(.vertical, 8)
                                .background(Color.indigo.opacity(0.12))
                                .cornerRadius(8)
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            .padding(.horizontal)
                            .padding(.top, 4)
                        }
                        
                        if displayMode == .card {
                            // Modalità Scheda Singola (Focus Mode)
                            GuideSingleCardView(
                                steps: sortedSteps,
                                orderedSections: orderedSections,
                                currentIndex: $currentStepIndex,
                                project: project,
                                onNavigateToPage: onSelectPdfPage,
                                onDeleteStep: { step in
                                    deleteStep(step)
                                },
                                onFinishGuide: {
                                    finishGuide()
                                },
                                onOpenGaugeSheet: {
                                    showingGaugeSheet = true
                                },
                                onToggleOutline: {
                                    withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.2)) {
                                        displayMode = .list
                                    }
                                }
                            )
                        } else {
                            // Modalità Elenco Completo con gestione Sezioni
                            VStack(spacing: 0) {
                                HStack {
                                    Text("Indice Sezioni e Schede")
                                        .font(.subheadline.bold())
                                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                    Spacer()
                                    Button {
                                        withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.2)) {
                                            displayMode = .card
                                        }
                                    } label: {
                                        HStack(spacing: 4) {
                                            Image(systemName: "rectangle.portrait.fill")
                                            Text("Torna alla Scheda")
                                        }
                                        .font(.caption.bold())
                                        .foregroundColor(.white)
                                        .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                                        .padding(.vertical, 6)
                                        .background(Color.accentColor)
                                        .clipShape(Capsule())
                                    }
                                }
                                .padding(.horizontal, 16)
                                .padding(.vertical, 8)
                                
                                List {
                                    ForEach(orderedSections) { group in
                                        Section {
                                            ForEach(group.steps) { step in
                                                GuideStepRow(
                                                    step: step,
                                                    onNavigateToPage: onSelectPdfPage,
                                                    onJumpToCard: {
                                                        if let idx = sortedSteps.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == step.id }) {
                                                            currentStepIndex = idx
                                                            withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.2)) {
                                                                displayMode = .card
                                                            }
                                                        }
                                                    },
                                                    onDelete: {
                                                        deleteStep(step)
                                                    }
                                                )
                                            }
                                            .onDelete { indexSet in
                                                deleteStepsFromGroup(indexSet, in: group)
                                            }
                                        } header: {
                                            HStack {
                                                Text(group.title)
                                                    .font(.headline)
                                                Spacer()
                                                Menu {
                                                    Button(role: .destructive) {
                                                        sectionToDelete = group.title
                                                        showingDeleteSectionAlert = true
                                                    } label: {
                                                        Label("Elimina Sezione '\(group.title)'", systemImage: "trash")
                                                    }
                                                } label: {
                                                    Image(systemName: "ellipsis")
                                                        .font(.caption)
                                                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                                        .padding(6)
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                .listStyle(.insetGrouped)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            .onAppear {
                if !sortedSteps.isEmpty {
                    if currentStepIndex < 0 || currentStepIndex >= sortedSteps.count {
                        let savedIdx = UserDefaults.standard.object(forKey: "purl_lab_last_guide_step_\(project.id)") as? Int
                        if let saved = savedIdx, saved >= 0 && saved < sortedSteps.count {
                            currentStepIndex = saved
                        } else if let active = project.activeWorkingStep(),
                                  let idx = sortedSteps.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == active.id }) {
                            currentStepIndex = idx
                        } else if let firstIncomplete = sortedSteps.firstIndex(where: { !$0.isCompleted }) {
                            currentStepIndex = firstIncomplete
                        } else {
                            currentStepIndex = 0
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            .onChange(of: currentStepIndex) { _, newIndex in
                if newIndex >= 0 && newIndex < sortedSteps.count {
                    UserDefaults.standard.set(newIndex, forKey: "purl_lab_last_guide_step_\(project.id)")
                }
            }
            .onReceive(NotificationCenter.default.publisher(for: .watchDidChangeActiveStep)) { notification in
                if let targetStepId = notification.userInfo?["stepId"] as? UUID,
                   let idx = sortedSteps.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == targetStepId }) {
                    withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.25)) {
                        currentStepIndex = idx
                    }
                }
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $showingAddStepSheet) {
                AddGuideStepSheet(project: project)
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $showingGenerateSheet) {
                GenerateGuideSheet(project: project)
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $showingGaugeSheet) {
                GaugeRecalculationView(project: project)
            }
            .fullScreenCover(isPresented: $showingCompletion) {
                GuideCompletionView(
                    project: project,
                    onReviewGuide: {
                        showingCompletion = false
                    },
                    onExit: {
                        showingCompletion = false
                        onExitProject()
                    },
                    onRestartFromBeginning: {
                        showingCompletion = false
                        currentStepIndex = 0
                    }
                )
            }
            .alert("Elimina Sezione", isPresented: $showingDeleteSectionAlert) {
                Button("Annulla", role: .cancel) { sectionToDelete = nil }
                Button("Elimina", role: .destructive) {
                    if let sec = sectionToDelete {
                        deleteEntireSection(sec)
                    }
                    sectionToDelete = nil
                }
            } message: {
                Text("Sei sicuro di voler eliminare l'intera sezione '\(sectionToDelete ?? "")' e tutti i suoi passaggi?")
            }
            .alert("Errore", isPresented: .constant(errorMessage != nil)) {
                Button("OK") { errorMessage = nil }
            } message: {
                Text(errorMessage ?? "")
            }
    }
    
    // MARK: - Fine Guida
    private func finishGuide() {
        // Salva la sessione di lavoro in corso nel tempo totale del progetto
        if TimerManager.shared.isRunning && TimerManager.shared.activeProjectId == project.id {
            _ = TimerManager.shared.pause()
        }
        project.completedAt = Date()
        project.updatedAt = Date()
        UINotificationFeedbackGenerator().notificationOccurred(.success)
        showingCompletion = true
    }
    
    // MARK: - Funzioni di Cancellazione e Gestione
    private func deleteStep(_ step: GuideStep) {
        if let idx = project.guideSteps.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == step.id }) {
            project.guideSteps.remove(at: idx)
            modelContext.delete(step)
            if currentStepIndex >= project.guideSteps.count {
                currentStepIndex = max(0, project.guideSteps.count - 1)
            }
        }
    }
    
    private func deleteStepsFromGroup(_ indexSet: IndexSet, in group: SectionGroup) {
        for index in indexSet {
            let step = group.steps[index]
            deleteStep(step)
        }
    }
    
    private func deleteEntireSection(_ sectionTitle: String) {
        let stepsToDelete = project.guideSteps.filter { $0.sectionTitle == sectionTitle }
        for step in stepsToDelete {
            deleteStep(step)
        }
    }
    
    private func clearAllSteps() {
        for step in project.guideSteps {
            modelContext.delete(step)
        }
        project.guideSteps.removeAll()
        currentStepIndex = 0
        project.completedAt = nil
    }
    
    private func loadPreloadedGuide() {
        guard let guideUrl = Bundle.main.url(forResource: "NoFrillsGuide_M", withExtension: "json"),
              let guideData = try? Data(contentsOf: guideUrl),
              let guide = try? JSONDecoder().decode(GeneratedGuideResponse.self, from: guideData) else {
            errorMessage = "Guida di test non trovata nel bundle."
            return
        }
        
        project.selectedSize = "M"
        project.completedAt = nil
        for step in project.guideSteps {
            modelContext.delete(step)
        }
        project.guideSteps.removeAll()
        
        var stepOrder = 0
        for section in guide.sections {
            for stepData in section.steps {
                let step = GuideStep(
                    sectionTitle: GeminiService.cleanSectionName(section.name),
                    title: stepData.title ?? "",
                    stepType: stepData.stepType ?? "single_action",
                    instructionText: stepData.instruction,
                    subSteps: stepData.subSteps ?? [],
                    repeatVariations: stepData.repeatVariations ?? [],
                    rowsPerRepeat: stepData.rowsPerRepeat,
                    totalRepeats: stepData.totalRepeats,
                    stitchCountBefore: stepData.stitchCountBefore,
                    stitchCountAfter: stepData.stitchCountAfter,
                    targetMeasureCm: stepData.targetMeasureCm,
                    sourcePage: stepData.sourcePage,
                    sourceQuote: stepData.sourceQuote,
                    orderIndex: stepOrder,
                    hasCounter: stepData.hasCounter ?? false,
                    counterLabel: stepData.counterLabel,
                    currentStepCount: 0,
                    targetStepCount: stepData.targetStepCount
                )
                step.project = project
                project.guideSteps.append(step)
                stepOrder += 1
            }
        }
        currentStepIndex = 0
    }
}

// MARK: - Vista Card Singola (Focus Mode)
struct GuideSingleCardView: View {
    let steps: [GuideStep]
    let orderedSections: [GuideView.SectionGroup]
    @Binding var currentIndex: Int
    var project: KnittingProject? = nil
    var onNavigateToPage: (Int) -> Void
    var onDeleteStep: (GuideStep) -> Void
    var onFinishGuide: () -> Void = {}
    var onOpenGaugeSheet: () -> Void = {}
    var onToggleOutline: () -> Void = {}
    
    @State private var stepToEdit: GuideStep? = nil
    private let haptic = UIImpactFeedbackGenerator(style: .medium)
    private let successHaptic = UINotificationFeedbackGenerator()
    
    var currentStep: GuideStep? {
        guard !steps.isEmpty, currentIndex >= 0, currentIndex < steps.count else { return nil }
        let step = steps[currentIndex]
        guard !step.isDetachedOrDeleted else { return nil }
        return step
    }
    
    // Calcola quale subStep è attivo se siamo in un pattern ripetuto
    private func isSubStepActive(index: Int, for step: GuideStep) -> Bool {
        guard !step.isDetachedOrDeleted else { return false }
        let sub = step.safeSubSteps
        guard let rowsPerRep = step.rowsPerRepeat, rowsPerRep > 1, !sub.isEmpty else {
            return false
        }
        if let target = step.targetStepCount, step.currentStepCount >= target {
            return false
        }
        let currentRowInCycle = (step.currentStepCount % rowsPerRep) + 1 // 1..rowsPerRep
        if sub.count == rowsPerRep {
            return index == (currentRowInCycle - 1)
        } else if rowsPerRep == 6 && sub.count == 2 {
            // Caso tipico maniche: Giri 1-5 (index 0) e Giro 6 di diminuzione (index 1)
            return currentRowInCycle < 6 ? (index == 0) : (index == 1)
        }
        return false
    }
    
    // Calcola quale repeatVariation è attiva
    private func isVariationActive(index: Int, for step: GuideStep) -> Bool {
        guard !step.isDetachedOrDeleted else { return false }
        let variations = step.safeRepeatVariations
        guard let rowsPerRep = step.rowsPerRepeat, rowsPerRep > 0, !variations.isEmpty else {
            return false
        }
        if let target = step.targetStepCount, step.currentStepCount >= target {
            return false
        }
        let currentRepeatZeroBased = step.currentStepCount / rowsPerRep
        return index == currentRepeatZeroBased
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        if let step = currentStep {
            VStack(spacing: 8) {
                // Barra Compatta Sezioni + Progresso
                VStack(spacing: 6) {
                    HStack {
                        ScrollView(.horizontal, showsIndicators: false) {
                            HStack(spacing: 6) {
                                ForEach(orderedSections) { group in
                                    let isCurrentSection = group.title == step.sectionTitle
                                    Button {
                                        if let firstStep = group.steps.first,
                                           let idx = steps.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == firstStep.id }) {
                                            withAnimation(.easeInOut(duration: 0.2)) {
                                                currentIndex = idx
                                            }
                                        }
                                    } label: {
                                        Text(group.title)
                                            .font(.system(size: 13, weight: isCurrentSection ? .bold : .medium))
                                            .padding(.horizontal, 12)
                                            .padding(.vertical, 6)
                                            .background(isCurrentSection ? Color.accentColor.opacity(0.15) : Color(.tertiarySystemFill))
                                            .foregroundColor(isCurrentSection ? Color.accentColor : .secondary)
                                            .clipShape(Capsule())
                                    }
                                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                }
                            }
                            .padding(.leading, 16)
                        }
                        
                        Button {
                            onToggleOutline()
                        } label: {
                            Image(systemName: "list.bullet")
                                .font(.system(size: 13, weight: .semibold))
                                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                .padding(8)
                                .background(Color(.tertiarySystemFill))
                                .clipShape(Circle())
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.plain)
                        .padding(.trailing, 16)
                    }
                    
                    HStack {
                        ProgressView(value: Double(currentIndex + 1), total: Double(steps.count))
                            .tint(Color.accentColor)
                            .scaleEffect(y: 0.8)
                        
                        Text("\(currentIndex + 1)/\(steps.count)")
                            .font(.caption2.monospacedDigit().weight(.medium))
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                    .padding(.horizontal, 16)
                }
                .padding(.top, 4)
                
                // La Card Principale
                ScrollView {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                        // Top Bar Essenziale della Card
                        HStack(alignment: .center, spacing: 8) {
                            if let rows = step.rowsPerRepeat, let reps = step.totalRepeats, rows > 0, reps > 0 {
                                HStack(spacing: 4) {
                                    Image(systemName: "repeat")
                                    Text("\(reps) × \(rows) ferri")
                                }
                                .font(.caption2.bold())
                                .foregroundColor(.indigo)
                                .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                                .padding(.vertical, 4)
                                .background(Color.indigo.opacity(0.12))
                                .clipShape(Capsule())
                            } else if step.stepType == "work_to_measure", let cm = step.targetMeasureCm {
                                HStack(spacing: 4) {
                                    Image(systemName: "ruler")
                                    Text("\(Int(cm)) cm")
                                }
                                .font(.caption2.bold())
                                .foregroundColor(.purple)
                                .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                                .padding(.vertical, 4)
                                .background(Color.purple.opacity(0.12))
                                .clipShape(Capsule())
                            }
                            
                            if let page = step.sourcePage {
                                Button {
                                    onNavigateToPage(page)
                                } label: {
                                    HStack(spacing: 3) {
                                        Image(systemName: "doc.text")
                                        Text("p. \(page)")
                                    }
                                    .font(.caption2.bold())
                                    .foregroundColor(.orange)
                                    .padding(.horizontal, 8)
                                    .padding(.vertical, 4)
                                    .background(Color.orange.opacity(0.12))
                                    .clipShape(Capsule())
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            }
