import SwiftUI
import SwiftData

enum DetailTab: String, CaseIterable {
    case guide = "Guida"
    case counters = "Contatori"
    case pdf = "PDF"
    
    var iconName: String {
        switch self {
        case .guide: return "book.pages"
        case .counters: return "number.circle"
        case .pdf: return "doc.text"
        }
    }
}

struct UnifiedProjectBottomBar: View {
    @Bindable var project: KnittingProject
    @Binding var selectedTab: DetailTab
    @State private var timerManager = TimerManager.shared
    private let haptic = UIImpactFeedbackGenerator(style: .medium)
    
    var body: some View {
        HStack(spacing: 8) {
            // Pillola Timer di Sessione
            Button {
                haptic.impactOccurred()
                if isProjectTimerActive {
                    timerManager.pause()
                } else {
                    timerManager.start(for: project.id)
                }
            } label: {
                HStack(spacing: 6) {
                    Image(systemName: isProjectTimerActive ? "pause.fill" : "play.fill")
                        .font(.system(size: 13, weight: .bold))
                        .foregroundColor(isProjectTimerActive ? .orange : .accentColor)
                    
                    Text(displayTimeString)
                        .font(.system(size: 13, weight: .bold, design: .monospaced))
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                    
                    if isProjectTimerActive {
                        Circle()
                            .fill(Color.green)
                            .frame(width: 6, height: 6)
                            .shadow(color: Color.green.opacity(0.8), radius: 3)
                    }
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, 10)
                .padding(.vertical, 8)
                .background(Color(.tertiarySystemFill))
                .clipShape(Capsule())
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            
            Spacer(minLength: 4)
            
            // Selettore Tab Guida / Contatori / PDF
            HStack(spacing: 2) {
                ForEach(DetailTab.allCases, id: \.self) { tab in
                    let isSelected = (selectedTab == tab)
                    Button {
                        haptic.impactOccurred()
                        withAnimation(.spring(response: 0.3, dampingFraction: 0.82)) {
                            selectedTab = tab
                        }
                    } label: {
                        HStack(spacing: 5) {
                            Image(systemName: tab.iconName)
                                .font(.system(size: 13, weight: isSelected ? .bold : .medium))
                            
                            if isSelected {
                                Text(tab.rawValue)
                                    .font(.system(size: 12, weight: .bold))
                                    .lineLimit(1)
                                    .fixedSize(horizontal: true, vertical: false)
                            }
                        }
                        .padding(.horizontal, isSelected ? 12 : 9)
                        .padding(.vertical, 7)
                        .background(
                            isSelected ? Color.accentColor : Color.clear
                        )
                        .foregroundColor(isSelected ? .white : .secondary)
                        .clipShape(Capsule())
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.plain)
                }
            }
            .padding(3)
            .background(Color(.tertiarySystemFill))
            .clipShape(Capsule())
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, 12)
        .padding(.vertical, 7)
        .background(
            RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 24, style: .continuous)
                .fill(.regularMaterial)
                .shadow(color: Color.black.opacity(0.12), radius: 10, x: 0, y: 4)
        )
        .padding(.horizontal, 16)
        .padding(.bottom, 6)
    }
    
    private var isProjectTimerActive: Bool {
        timerManager.isRunning && timerManager.activeProjectId == project.id
    }
    
    private var displayTimeString: String {
        let total = project.totalSecondsWorked + (isProjectTimerActive ? timerManager.elapsedSecondsInCurrentSession : 0)
        return timerManager.formatTime(total)
    }
}

struct ProjectDetailView: View {
    @Bindable var project: KnittingProject
    var onDeleteProject: (() -> Void)? = nil
    
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
    @Environment(\.modelContext) private var modelContext
    @Environment(\.scenePhase) private var scenePhase
    @State private var selectedTab: DetailTab = .guide
    @State private var targetPdfPage: Int = 1
    @State private var currentGuideStepIndex: Int = 0
    
    @State private var showingEditProject = false
    @State private var showingGaugeSheet = false
    @State private var showingAddStepSheet = false
    @State private var showingRestartAlert = false
    @State private var showingDeleteAlert = false
    @State private var stepToEdit: GuideStep? = nil
    @State private var editedTitle = ""
    @State private var editedDesigner = ""
    @State private var editedSize = ""
    
    private var activeGuideStep: GuideStep? {
        let sorted = project.guideSteps.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex })
        guard currentGuideStepIndex >= 0 && currentGuideStepIndex < sorted.count else { return nil }
        return sorted[currentGuideStepIndex]
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        Group {
            if project.isDeleted || project.modelContext == nil {
                Color.clear
            } else {
                VStack(spacing: 0) {
            // Tab Content
            TabView(selection: $selectedTab) {
                GuideView(
                    project: project,
                    currentStepIndex: $currentGuideStepIndex,
                    onSelectPdfPage: { page in
                        targetPdfPage = page
                        selectedTab = .pdf
                    },
                    onExitProject: {
                        dismiss()
                    }
                )
                .tag(DetailTab.guide)
                
                CountersView(project: project)
                    .tag(DetailTab.counters)
                
                if let pdfData = project.pdfData {
                    PatternPDFView(pdfData: pdfData)
                        .tag(DetailTab.pdf)
                } else {
                    ContentUnavailableView(
                        "Nessun PDF",
                        systemImage: "doc.text",
                        description: Text("Nessun file PDF caricato per questo progetto.")
                    )
                    .tag(DetailTab.pdf)
                }
            }
            .tabViewStyle(.page(indexDisplayMode: .never))
            
            // Barra di Controllo Unificata Inferiore (Timer + Selettore Schede)
            UnifiedProjectBottomBar(project: project, selectedTab: $selectedTab)
        }
        .navigationTitle(project.selectedSize.isEmpty ? project.title : "\(project.title) · \(project.selectedSize)")
        .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
        .navigationBarBackButtonHidden(true)
        .toolbar {
            ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarLeading) {
                Button {
                    dismiss()
                } label: {
                    HStack(spacing: 4) {
                        Image(systemName: "chevron.left")
                            .font(.body.weight(.semibold))
                        Text("Progetti")
                            .font(.body)
                    }
                }
            }
            
            ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarTrailing) {
                HStack(spacing: 12) {
                    Button {
                        showingGaugeSheet = true
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: project.isGaugeRecalculated ? "gauge.with.needle.fill" : "gauge.with.needle")
                            .font(.body)
                            .foregroundColor(project.isGaugeRecalculated ? .indigo : .accentColor)
                    }
                    
                    Menu {
                        if selectedTab == .guide, let active = activeGuideStep {
                            Button {
                                stepToEdit = active
                            } label: {
                                Label("Modifica Scheda Attuale", systemImage: "pencil")
                            }
                        }
                        
                        Button {
                            editedTitle = project.title
                            editedDesigner = project.designer
                            editedSize = project.selectedSize
                            showingEditProject = true
                        } label: {
                            Label("Modifica Info Progetto", systemImage: "info.circle")
                        }
                        
                        Button {
                            showingAddStepSheet = true
                        } label: {
                            Label("Aggiungi Scheda Guida", systemImage: "plus.rectangle.on.rectangle")
                        }
                        
                        Button {
                            showingGaugeSheet = true
                        } label: {
                            Label("Adatta al Campione (AI)...", systemImage: "gauge.with.needle")
                        }
                        
                        Divider()
                        
                        Button(role: .destructive) {
                            showingRestartAlert = true
                        } label: {
                            Label("Ricomincia da capo", systemImage: "arrow.counterclockwise")
                        }
                        
                        Button(role: .destructive) {
                            showingDeleteAlert = true
                        } label: {
                            Label("Elimina Progetto", systemImage: "trash")
                        }
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "ellipsis.circle")
                            .font(.body)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        .sheet(item: $stepToEdit) { step in
            EditGuideStepSheet(step: step, onDelete: {
                if let idx = project.guideSteps.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == step.id }) {
                    project.guideSteps.remove(at: idx)
                    modelContext.delete(step)
                    try? modelContext.save()
                    WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.syncAllProjectsToWatch()
                }
            })
        }
        .sheet(isPresented: $showingAddStepSheet) {
            AddGuideStepSheet(project: project)
        }
        .sheet(isPresented: $showingGaugeSheet) {
            GaugeRecalculationView(project: project)
        }
        .sheet(isPresented: $showingEditProject) {
            NavigationStack {
                Form {
                    Section("Nome e Dettagli") {
                        TextField("Nome del Progetto", text: $editedTitle)
                        TextField("Designer (opzionale)", text: $editedDesigner)
                        TextField("Taglia (es. M, S, L...)", text: $editedSize)
                    }
                    
                    Section {
                        Button(role: .destructive) {
                            showingEditProject = false
                            DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.35) {
                                showingDeleteAlert = true
                            }
                        } label: {
                            HStack {
                                Spacer()
                                Label("Elimina Progetto", systemImage: "trash")
                                Spacer()
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                .navigationTitle("Modifica Progetto")
                .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
                .toolbar {
                    ToolbarItem(placement: .cancellationAction) {
                        Button("Annulla") { showingEditProject = false }
                    }
                    ToolbarItem(placement: .confirmationAction) {
                        Button("Salva") {
                            let clean = editedTitle.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                            if !clean.isEmpty {
                                project.title = clean
                            }
                            project.designer = editedDesigner.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                            project.selectedSize = editedSize.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                            project.updatedAt = Date()
                            showingEditProject = false
                        }
                        .bold()
                        .disabled(editedTitle.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty)
                    }
                }
            }
            .presentationDetents([.medium, .large])
        }
        .alert("Ricomincia da capo", isPresented: $showingRestartAlert) {
            Button("Annulla", role: .cancel) { }
            Button("Ricomincia da capo", role: .destructive) {
                project.resetAllProgress(modelContext: modelContext)
                currentGuideStepIndex = 0
            }
        } message: {
            Text("Sei sicuro di voler ricominciare \"\(project.title)\" da capo? Tutti i progressi, i contatori e il tempo registrato verranno azzerati.")
        }
        .alert("Elimina Progetto", isPresented: $showingDeleteAlert) {
            Button("Annulla", role: .cancel) { }
            Button("Elimina", role: .destructive) {
                if let onDelete = onDeleteProject {
                    onDelete()
                } else {
                    dismiss()
                    DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.35) {
                        modelContext.delete(project)
                        try? modelContext.save()
                        WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.syncAllProjectsToWatch()
                    }
                }
            }
        } message: {
            Text("Sei sicuro di voler eliminare \"\(project.title)\"? L'azione è irreversibile e cancellerà tutte le schede e i contatori associati.")
        }
        .onAppear {
            guard !project.isDeleted, project.modelContext != nil else { return }
            let sorted = project.guideSteps.sorted(by: { $0.orderIndex < $1.orderIndex })
            let savedIdx = UserDefaults.standard.object(forKey: "purl_lab_last_guide_step_\(project.id)") as? Int
            if let saved = savedIdx, saved >= 0 && saved < sorted.count {
                currentGuideStepIndex = saved
            } else if let active = project.activeWorkingStep(),
                      let idx = sorted.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == active.id }) {
                currentGuideStepIndex = idx
            }
            WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.setActiveProject(project)
        }
        .onDisappear {
            // Salva la sessione attiva se si esce dal progetto
            guard !project.isDeleted, project.modelContext != nil else { return }
            if TimerManager.shared.isRunning && TimerManager.shared.activeProjectId == project.id {
                _ = TimerManager.shared.pause()
            }
        }
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
[18/30] FILE: PurlLab/Views/Projects/ProjectListView.swift

import SwiftUI
import SwiftData
import UniformTypeIdentifiers

struct ProjectListView: View {
    @Environment(\.modelContext) private var modelContext
    @Query(sort: \KnittingProject.updatedAt, order: .reverse) private var projects: [KnittingProject]
    
    @State private var showingFilePicker = false
    @State private var showingSettings = false
    @State private var showingManualNewProject = false
    @State private var newProjectTitle = ""
    @State private var newProjectDesigner = ""
    @State private var projectToRename: KnittingProject?
    @State private var renameTitle = ""
    @State private var showingRenameAlert = false
    @State private var projectToDelete: KnittingProject?
    @State private var showingDeleteAlert = false
    @State private var projectToRestart: KnittingProject?
    @State private var showingRestartAlert = false
    @State private var navigationPath: [UUID] = []
    
    private var activeProjectToResume: KnittingProject? {
        projects.first(where: { $0.completedAt == nil }) ?? projects.first
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack(path: $navigationPath) {
            Group {
                if projects.isEmpty {
                    ContentUnavailableView {
                        Label("Nessun Progetto", systemImage: "sparkles.rectangle.stack")
                    } description: {
                        Text("Importa un pattern PDF da File per iniziare a lavorare con la guida AI e i contatori.")
                    } actions: {
                        VStack(spacing: 12) {
                            Button {
                                showingFilePicker = true
                            } label: {
                                Label("Importa Pattern PDF da File", systemImage: "doc.badge.plus")
                                    .bold()
                                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                    .padding(.vertical, 4)
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(.borderedProminent)
                            
                            Button {
                                showingManualNewProject = true
                            } label: {
                                Label("Crea Nuovo Progetto Manuale", systemImage: "plus.rectangle")
                                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                            }
                            .buttonStyle(.bordered)
                        }
                        .padding(.horizontal, 32)
                    }
                } else {
                    List {
                        if let resumeProject = activeProjectToResume {
                            Section {
                                ResumeProjectHeroCard(project: resumeProject) {
                                    navigationPath = [resumeProject.id]
                                }
                                .swipeActions(edge: .trailing, allowsFullSwipe: false) {
                                    Button(role: .destructive) {
                                        projectToDelete = resumeProject
                                        showingDeleteAlert = true
                                    } label: {
                                        Label("Elimina", systemImage: "trash")
                                    }
                                }
                                .swipeActions(edge: .leading) {
                                    Button {
                                        projectToRename = resumeProject
                                        renameTitle = resumeProject.title
                                        showingRenameAlert = true
                                    } label: {
                                        Label("Rinomina", systemImage: "pencil")
                                    }
                                    .tint(.indigo)
                                }
                                .contextMenu {
                                    Button {
                                        projectToRename = resumeProject
                                        renameTitle = resumeProject.title
                                        showingRenameAlert = true
                                    } label: {
                                        Label("Rinomina Progetto", systemImage: "pencil")
                                    }
                                    Button(role: .destructive) {
                                        projectToRestart = resumeProject
                                        showingRestartAlert = true
                                    } label: {
                                        Label("Ricomincia da capo", systemImage: "arrow.counterclockwise")
                                    }
                                    Button(role: .destructive) {
                                        projectToDelete = resumeProject
                                        showingDeleteAlert = true
                                    } label: {
                                        Label("Elimina Progetto", systemImage: "trash")
                                    }
                                }
                                .listRowInsets(EdgeInsets(top: 4, leading: 16, bottom: 8, trailing: 16))
                                .listRowBackground(Color.clear)
                                .listRowSeparator(.hidden)
                            }
                        }
                        
                        Section {
                            HStack(spacing: 12) {
                                Button {
                                    showingFilePicker = true
                                } label: {
                                    HStack(spacing: 6) {
                                        Image(systemName: "doc.badge.plus")
                                            .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                                            .foregroundColor(.accentColor)
                                        Text("Importa PDF")
                                            .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                                            .foregroundColor(.primary)
                                    }
                                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                    .padding(.vertical, 10)
                                    .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
                                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12))
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                                
                                Button {
                                    showingManualNewProject = true
                                } label: {
                                    HStack(spacing: 6) {
                                        Image(systemName: "plus")
                                            .font(.subheadline.weight(.bold))
                                            .foregroundColor(.indigo)
                                        Text("Nuovo Progetto")
                                            .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                                            .foregroundColor(.primary)
                                    }
                                    .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                                    .padding(.vertical, 10)
                                    .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
                                    .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12))
                                }
                                .buttonStyle(.plain)
                            }
                            .listRowInsets(EdgeInsets(top: 0, leading: 16, bottom: 8, trailing: 16))
                            .listRowBackground(Color.clear)
                            .listRowSeparator(.hidden)
                        }
                        
                        let otherProjects = projects.filter { $0.id != activeProjectToResume?.id }
                        if !otherProjects.isEmpty {
                            Section("Altri Progetti (\(otherProjects.count))") {
                                ForEach(otherProjects) { project in
                                    NavigationLink(value: project.id) {
                                        ProjectRowView(project: project)
                                    }
                                    .swipeActions(edge: .trailing, allowsFullSwipe: false) {
                                        Button(role: .destructive) {
                                            projectToDelete = project
                                            showingDeleteAlert = true
                                        } label: {
                                            Label("Elimina", systemImage: "trash")
                                        }
                                    }
                                    .swipeActions(edge: .leading) {
                                        Button {
                                            projectToRename = project
                                            renameTitle = project.title
                                            showingRenameAlert = true
                                        } label: {
                                            Label("Rinomina", systemImage: "pencil")
                                        }
                                        .tint(.indigo)
                                    }
                                    .contextMenu {
                                        Button {
                                            projectToRename = project
                                            renameTitle = project.title
                                            showingRenameAlert = true
                                        } label: {
                                            Label("Rinomina Progetto", systemImage: "pencil")
                                        }
                                        Button(role: .destructive) {
                                            projectToRestart = project
                                            showingRestartAlert = true
                                        } label: {
                                            Label("Ricomincia da capo", systemImage: "arrow.counterclockwise")
                                        }
                                        Button(role: .destructive) {
                                            projectToDelete = project
                                            showingDeleteAlert = true
                                        } label: {
                                            Label("Elimina Progetto", systemImage: "trash")
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                                .onDelete(perform: deleteProjects)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .listStyle(.insetGrouped)
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("I Miei Progetti")
            .navigationDestination(for: UUID.self) { projectId in
                if let project = projects.first(where: { $0.id == projectId && !$0.isDeleted }) {
                    ProjectDetailView(project: project, onDeleteProject: {
                        navigationPath.removeAll()
                        DispatchQueue.main.asyncAfter(deadline: .now() + 0.3) {
                            deleteProject(project)
                        }
                    })
                }
            }
            .onReceive(NotificationCenter.default.publisher(for: .watchDidSelectProject)) { notification in
                if let projectId = notification.userInfo?["projectId"] as? UUID {
                    navigationPath = [projectId]
                }
            }
            .onAppear {
                if ProcessInfo.processInfo.arguments.contains("-openFirstProject") {
                    if let first = projects.first {
                        navigationPath = [first.id]
                    }
                }
                
                // Rimuovi eventuali vecchi contatori del raglan dalla scheda contatori
                for project in projects {
                    let raglanCounters = project.counters.filter { $0.title.localizedCaseInsensitiveContains("raglan") }
                    for rc in raglanCounters {
                        if let idx = project.counters.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == rc.id }) {
                            project.counters.remove(at: idx)
                            modelContext.delete(rc)
                        }
                    }
                    
                    // Aggiorna vecchi nomi nel nuovo nome "Contatore Globale"
                    for counter in project.counters {
                        if counter.title == "Ferri Principali" || counter.title == "Ferro" || counter.title == "Ferri" {
                            counter.title = "Contatore Globale"
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarLeading) {
                    Button {
                        showingSettings = true
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "gearshape")
                    }
                }
                
                ToolbarItem(placement: .topBarTrailing) {
                    Menu {
                        Button {
                            loadSamplePattern()
                        } label: {
                            Label("Carica No Frills Sweater (Test)", systemImage: "sparkles")
                        }
                        
                        Button {
                            showingFilePicker = true
                        } label: {
                            Label("Importa PDF Pattern da File", systemImage: "doc.badge.plus")
                        }
                        
                        Button {
                            showingManualNewProject = true
                        } label: {
                            Label("Nuovo Progetto Vuoto", systemImage: "plus.rectangle")
                        }
                    } label: {
                        Image(systemName: "plus.circle.fill")
                            .font(.title3)
                    }
                }
            }
            .fileImporter(
                isPresented: $showingFilePicker,
                allowedContentTypes: [.pdf],
                allowsMultipleSelection: false
            ) { result in
                handlePdfImport(result)
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $showingSettings) {
                SettingsView()
            }
            .alert("Nuovo Progetto", isPresented: $showingManualNewProject) {
                TextField("Nome Progetto", text: $newProjectTitle)
                TextField("Designer (opzionale)", text: $newProjectDesigner)
                Button("Annulla", role: .cancel) {
                    newProjectTitle = ""
                    newProjectDesigner = ""
                }
                Button("Crea") {
                    let project = KnittingProject(
                        title: newProjectTitle.isEmpty ? "Nuovo Lavoro" : newProjectTitle,
                        designer: newProjectDesigner
                    )
                    let defaultCounter = RowCounter(title: "Contatore Globale")
                    defaultCounter.project = project
                    project.counters.append(defaultCounter)
                    
                    modelContext.insert(project)
                    newProjectTitle = ""
                    newProjectDesigner = ""
                }
            }
            .alert("Rinomina Progetto", isPresented: $showingRenameAlert) {
                TextField("Nome del Progetto", text: $renameTitle)
                Button("Annulla", role: .cancel) {
                    projectToRename = nil
                }
                Button("Salva") {
                    if let p = projectToRename {
                        let clean = renameTitle.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                        if !clean.isEmpty {
                            p.title = clean
                            p.updatedAt = Date()
                        }
                    }
                    projectToRename = nil
                }
            }
            .alert("Ricomincia da capo", isPresented: $showingRestartAlert) {
                Button("Annulla", role: .cancel) {
                    projectToRestart = nil
                }
                Button("Ricomincia da capo", role: .destructive) {
                    if let p = projectToRestart {
                        p.resetAllProgress(modelContext: modelContext)
                    }
                    projectToRestart = nil
                }
            } message: {
                Text("Sei sicuro di voler ricominciare \"\(projectToRestart?.title ?? "")\" da capo? Tutti i progressi, i contatori e il tempo registrato verranno azzerati.")
            }
            .alert("Elimina Progetto", isPresented: $showingDeleteAlert) {
                Button("Annulla", role: .cancel) {
                    projectToDelete = nil
                }
                Button("Elimina", role: .destructive) {
                    if let p = projectToDelete {
                        deleteProject(p)
                    }
                    projectToDelete = nil
                }
            } message: {
                Text("Sei sicuro di voler eliminare \"\(projectToDelete?.title ?? "questo progetto")\"? L'azione è irreversibile e cancellerà tutte le schede e i contatori associati.")
            }
        }
    }
    
    private func deleteProject(_ project: KnittingProject) {
        if navigationPath.contains(project.id) {
            navigationPath.removeAll()
        }
        if let idx = projects.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == project.id }) {
            modelContext.delete(projects[idx])
            try? modelContext.save()
            WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.syncAllProjectsToWatch()
        }
    }
    
    private func deleteProjects(at offsets: IndexSet) {
        for index in offsets {
            modelContext.delete(projects[index])
        }
        try? modelContext.save()
        WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.syncAllProjectsToWatch()
    }
    
    private func loadSamplePattern() {
        guard let sampleUrl = Bundle.main.url(forResource: "NoFrillsSweater", withExtension: "pdf"),
              let pdfData = try? Data(contentsOf: sampleUrl) else {
            print("PDF di test non trovato nel bundle.")
            return
        }
        
        let project = KnittingProject(
            title: "No Frills Sweater",
            designer: "PetiteKnit",
            selectedSize: "M",
            pdfData: pdfData
        )
        
        let c1 = RowCounter(title: "Contatore Globale")
        c1.project = project
        project.counters.append(c1)
        
        if let guideUrl = Bundle.main.url(forResource: "NoFrillsGuide_M", withExtension: "json"),
           let guideData = try? Data(contentsOf: guideUrl),
           let guide = try? JSONDecoder().decode(GeneratedGuideResponse.self, from: guideData) {
            var stepOrder = 0
            for section in guide.sections {
                for stepData in section.steps {
                    let step = GuideStep(
                        sectionTitle: GeminiService.cleanSectionName(section.name),
                        title: stepData.title ?? "",
                        stepType: stepData.stepType ?? "single_action",
                        instructionText: stepData.instruction,
                        subSteps: stepData.subSteps ?? [],
                        repeatVariations: stepData.repeatVariations ?? [],
                        rowsPerRepeat: stepData.rowsPerRepeat,
                        totalRepeats: stepData.totalRepeats,
                        stitchCountBefore: stepData.stitchCountBefore,
                        stitchCountAfter: stepData.stitchCountAfter,
                        targetMeasureCm: stepData.targetMeasureCm,
                        sourcePage: stepData.sourcePage,
                        sourceQuote: stepData.sourceQuote,
                        orderIndex: stepOrder,
                        hasCounter: stepData.hasCounter ?? (stepData.targetStepCount != nil && stepData.targetStepCount! > 0),
                        counterLabel: stepData.counterLabel,
                        targetStepCount: stepData.targetStepCount
                    )
                    step.project = project
                    project.guideSteps.append(step)
                    stepOrder += 1
                }
            }
        }
        
        modelContext.insert(project)
    }
    
    private func handlePdfImport(_ result: Result<[URL], Error>) {
        do {
            guard let selectedUrl = try result.get().first else { return }
            
            let canAccess = selectedUrl.startAccessingSecurityScopedResource()
            defer {
                if canAccess { selectedUrl.stopAccessingSecurityScopedResource() }
            }
            
            let pdfData = try Data(contentsOf: selectedUrl)
            let filename = selectedUrl.deletingPathExtension().lastPathComponent
            
            let project = KnittingProject(
                title: filename.replacingOccurrences(of: "_", with: " ").capitalized,
                pdfData: pdfData
            )
            
            let counter = RowCounter(title: "Contatore Globale")
            counter.project = project
            project.counters.append(counter)
            
            modelContext.insert(project)
        } catch {
            print("Errore importazione PDF: \(error.localizedDescription)")
        }
    }
}

// MARK: - Hero Card per Riprendere il Progetto Attivo
struct ResumeProjectHeroCard: View {
    let project: KnittingProject
    let onResume: () -> Void
    private let haptic = UIImpactFeedbackGenerator(style: .medium)
    
    private var activeStep: GuideStep? {
        project.activeWorkingStep()
    }
    
    private var progressPercentage: Double {
        let total = project.guideSteps.count
        guard total > 0 else { return 0 }
        let completed = project.guideSteps.filter { $0.isCompleted }.count
        return Double(completed) / Double(total)
    }
    

    var body: some View {
        if project.isDeleted || project.modelContext == nil {
            EmptyView()
        } else {
            Button {
                haptic.impactOccurred()
                onResume()
            } label: {
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 14) {
                // Header riga: Stato e tempo
                HStack(alignment: .center) {
                    HStack(spacing: 5) {
                        Circle()
                            .fill(Color.accentColor)
                            .frame(width: 7, height: 7)
                        Text("In Lavorazione")
                            .font(.caption.weight(.semibold))
                            .foregroundColor(.accentColor)
                    }
                    
                    Spacer()
                    
                    if project.totalSecondsWorked > 0 {
                        HStack(spacing: 4) {
                            Image(systemName: "clock")
                            Text(TimerManager.shared.formatTime(project.totalSecondsWorked))
                        }
                        .font(.caption.monospacedDigit())
                        .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                }
                
                // Titolo e dettagli progetto
                HStack(alignment: .bottom) {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 3) {
                        Text(project.title)
                            .font(.title3.bold())
                            .foregroundColor(.primary)
                            .lineLimit(1)
                        
                        HStack(spacing: 6) {
                            if !project.designer.isEmpty {
                                Text(project.designer)
                                    .font(.subheadline)
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            }
                            if !project.selectedSize.isEmpty {
                                Text("• Taglia \(project.selectedSize)")
                                    .font(.subheadline)
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    
                    Spacer()
                    
                    // Callout discreto Riprendi
                    HStack(spacing: 4) {
                        Text("Riprendi")
                            .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                        Image(systemName: "chevron.right")
                            .font(.caption.weight(.bold))
                    }
                    .foregroundColor(.accentColor)
                }
                
                // Sezione / Scheda attiva & avanzamento
                if let step = activeStep {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                        Divider()
                            .opacity(0.6)
                        
                        HStack(alignment: .center) {
                            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
                                Text(step.sectionTitle)
                                    .font(.caption.weight(.semibold))
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                                if !step.title.isEmpty {
                                    Text(step.title)
                                        .font(.subheadline)
                                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                                        .lineLimit(1)
                                }
                            }
                            
                            Spacer()
                            
                            if !project.guideSteps.isEmpty {
                                Text("Scheda \(step.orderIndex + 1)/\(project.guideSteps.count)")
                                    .font(.caption.monospacedDigit())
                                    .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                            }
                        }
                        
                        if !project.guideSteps.isEmpty {
                            ProgressView(value: progressPercentage)
                                .tint(.accentColor)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            .padding(16)
            .background(Color(.secondarySystemGroupedBackground))
            .clipShape(RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 16, style: .continuous))
            .shadow(color: Color.black.opacity(0.04), radius: 8, x: 0, y: 2)
        }
        .buttonStyle(.plain)
    }
}
}

struct ProjectRowView: View {
    let project: KnittingProject
    
    private var progressPercentage: Double {
        let total = project.guideSteps.count
        guard total > 0 else { return 0 }
        let completed = project.guideSteps.filter { $0.isCompleted }.count
        return Double(completed) / Double(total)
    }
    
    var body: some View {
        if project.isDeleted || project.modelContext == nil {
            EmptyView()
        } else {
            HStack(spacing: 14) {
            ZStack {
                Circle()
                    .fill(project.completedAt != nil ? Color.green.opacity(0.12) : Color.accentColor.opacity(0.12))
                    .frame(width: 44, height: 44)
                
                Image(systemName: project.completedAt != nil ? "checkmark.circle.fill" : "circle.grid.cross.fill")
                    .font(.title3)
                    .foregroundColor(project.completedAt != nil ? .green : .accentColor)
            }
            
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 5) {
                HStack {
                    Text(project.title)
                        .font(.headline)
                        .foregroundColor(.primary)
                        .lineLimit(1)
                    
                    Spacer()
                    
                    if project.completedAt != nil {
                        Text("Completato")
                            .font(.caption2.bold())
                            .padding(.horizontal, 6)
                            .padding(.vertical, 2)
                            .background(Color.green.opacity(0.15))
                            .foregroundColor(.green)
                            .clipShape(Capsule())
                    } else if !project.guideSteps.isEmpty {
                        Text(String(format: "%.0f%%", progressPercentage * 100))
                            .font(.caption2.bold().monospacedDigit())
                            .foregroundColor(.accentColor)
                    }
                }
                
                HStack(spacing: 6) {
                    if !project.designer.isEmpty {
                        Text(project.designer)
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                    
                    if !project.selectedSize.isEmpty {
                        Text("• Taglia \(project.selectedSize)")
                            .foregroundColor(.accentColor)
                            .bold()
                    }
                }
                .font(.subheadline)
                
                if !project.guideSteps.isEmpty && project.completedAt == nil {
                    ProgressView(value: progressPercentage)
                        .tint(.accentColor)
                        .padding(.vertical, 2)
                }
                
                HStack(spacing: 12) {
                    Label("\(project.guideSteps.count) schede", systemImage: "rectangle.stack")
                    Label("\(project.counters.count) contatori", systemImage: "number.circle")
                    if project.totalSecondsWorked > 0 {
                        Label(TimerManager.shared.formatTime(project.totalSecondsWorked), systemImage: "clock")
                    }
                }
                .font(.caption2)
                .foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
        }
        .padding(.vertical, 4)
        }
    }
}

import SwiftUI

struct SettingsView: View {
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
    @State private var apiKey: String = ""
    @State private var isKeySaved: Bool = false
    @State private var isTestingConnection: Bool = false
    @State private var testResult: String?
    @State private var isTestSuccessful: Bool = false
    @State private var autoPauseMinutes: Double = 15.0
    
    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                Section {
                    SecureField("Chiave API di Gemini", text: $apiKey)
                        .textContentType(.password)
                        .autocorrectionDisabled()
                        .textInputAutocapitalization(.never)
                    
                    HStack(spacing: 12) {
                        Button {
                            let cleanKey = apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
                            if KeychainHelper.shared.saveGeminiApiKey(cleanKey) {
                                isKeySaved = true
                                testApiKey(cleanKey)
                            }
                        } label: {
                            Label(isKeySaved ? "Chiave Salvata" : "Salva Chiave", systemImage: isKeySaved ? "checkmark.circle.fill" : "square.and.arrow.down")
                        }
                        .disabled(apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty)
                        
                        Spacer()
                        
                        Button {
                            testApiKey(apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines))
                        } label: {
                            if isTestingConnection {
                                ProgressView()
                                    .scaleEffect(0.8)
                            } else {
                                Label("Test Connessione", systemImage: "network")
                            }
                        }
                        .buttonStyle(.bordered)
                        .disabled(apiKey.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines).isEmpty || isTestingConnection)
                    }
                    
                    if let result = testResult {
                        HStack(spacing: 8) {
                            Image(systemName: isTestSuccessful ? "checkmark.seal.fill" : "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
                                .foregroundColor(isTestSuccessful ? .green : .red)
                            Text(result)
                                .font(.caption.bold())
                                .foregroundColor(isTestSuccessful ? .green : .red)
                        }
                        .padding(.vertical, 4)
                    }
                    
                    Button(role: .destructive) {
                        KeychainHelper.shared.deleteGeminiApiKey()
                        apiKey = ""
                        isKeySaved = false
                        testResult = nil
                    } label: {
                        Label("Elimina Chiave Salvata", systemImage: "trash")
                            .font(.caption)
                    }
                    .disabled(!isKeySaved && apiKey.isEmpty)
                    
                    Link(destination: URL(string: "https://aistudio.google.com/app/apikey")!) {
                        HStack {
                            Text("Ottieni una chiave gratuita su Google AI Studio")
                            Spacer()
                            Image(systemName: "arrow.up.right.square")
                        }
                        .font(.footnote)
                    }
                } header: {
                    Text("Google Gemini AI")
                } footer: {
                    Text("La chiave API viene salvata nel Keychain del tuo dispositivo. Puoi testare la connessione direttamente con il pulsante dedicato.")
                }
                
                Section {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 6) {
                        Text("Pausa automatica dopo inattività")
                        HStack {
                            Slider(value: $autoPauseMinutes, in: 5...60, step: 5)
                            Text("\(Int(autoPauseMinutes)) min")
                                .monospacedDigit()
                        }
                    }
                } header: {
                    Text("Timer e Lavoro")
                }
                
                Section {
                    HStack {
                        Text("Versione")
                        Spacer()
                        Text("1.0.0 (PurlLab)")
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                    HStack {
                        Text("Sviluppata per")
                        Spacer()
                        Text("Solo uso personale")
                            .foregroundColor(.secondary)
                    }
                } header: {
                    Text("Informazioni")
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("Impostazioni")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .confirmationAction) {
                    Button("Chiudi") { dismiss() }
                }
            }
            .onAppear {
                if let saved = KeychainHelper.shared.getGeminiApiKey() {
                    apiKey = saved
                    isKeySaved = true
                } else {
                    apiKey = ""
                    isKeySaved = false
                }
                autoPauseMinutes = TimerManager.shared.autoPauseMinutes
            }
            .onChange(of: autoPauseMinutes) { _, newValue in
                TimerManager.shared.autoPauseMinutes = newValue
            }
        }
    }
    
    private func testApiKey(_ key: String) {
        guard key.count >= 20 else {
            testResult = "La chiave inserita è troppo corta o non valida."
            isTestSuccessful = false
            return
        }
        
        isTestingConnection = true
        testResult = nil
        
        Task {
            do {
                _ = try await GeminiService.shared.testConnection(with: key)
                await MainActor.run {
                    isTestingConnection = false
                    isTestSuccessful = true
                    testResult = "Connessione riuscita! API Gemini attiva."
                }
            } catch {
                await MainActor.run {
                    isTestingConnection = false
                    isTestSuccessful = false
                    testResult = "Errore: \(error.localizedDescription)"
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
[20/30] FILE: PurlLabTests/GaugeRecalculationTests.swift

import XCTest
@testable import PurlLab

final class GaugeRecalculationTests: XCTestCase {
    
    func testGaugeRatioCalculation() {
        let patternStitches = 21.0
        let userStitches = 19.0
        
        let stitchRatio = userStitches / patternStitches
        // 19 / 21 = 0.90476... (maglie più larghe -> meno maglie nei 10 cm)
        XCTAssertEqual(stitchRatio, 19.0 / 21.0, accuracy: 0.0001)
        
        let originalCastOn = 114
        // Per mantenere la stessa larghezza in cm: originalCastOn * stitchRatio
        let recalculatedCastOn = Int(round(Double(originalCastOn) * stitchRatio))
        XCTAssertEqual(recalculatedCastOn, 103)
    }
    
    func testRowRatioCalculation() {
        let patternRows = 28.0
        let userRows = 26.0
        
        let rowRatio = userRows / patternRows
        XCTAssertEqual(rowRatio, 26.0 / 28.0, accuracy: 0.0001)
        
        let originalRows = 60
        let recalculatedRows = Int(round(Double(originalRows) * rowRatio))
        XCTAssertEqual(recalculatedRows, 56)
    }
    
    func testReplaceNumberDoesNotMatchInsideLargerNumber() {
        let original = "Avviare 114 maglie sui ferri. Lavorare 4 maglie a coste."
        // Sostituiamo 4 maglie -> 5 maglie: "114 maglie" non deve essere corrotto in "115 maglie"
        let result = GeminiService.replaceNumbersInText(original, stitchMap: [4: 5])
        XCTAssertEqual(result, "Avviare 114 maglie sui ferri. Lavorare 5 maglie a coste.")
    }
    
    func testReplaceNumberDoesNotConfuseUnitMWithMillimeters() {
        let original = "Lavorare con ferri da 4 mm (4 m rimanenti)."
        // Sostituiamo 4 m -> 5 m: "4 mm" deve rimanere intatto, mentre "(4 m" deve diventare "(5 m"
        let result = GeminiService.replaceNumbersInText(original, stitchMap: [4: 5])
        XCTAssertEqual(result, "Lavorare con ferri da 4 mm (5 m rimanenti).")
    }
    
    func testReplaceNumbersSinglePassAvoidsCollisions() {
        let original = "Iniziare con 10 maglie e terminare a 20 maglie."
        // Se eseguisse sostituzioni sequenziali: 10 -> 20, poi 20 -> 30 (portando la prima a 30!)
        // In una singola passata: 10 diventa 20 e 20 diventa 30 contemporaneamente
        let result = GeminiService.replaceNumbersInText(original, stitchMap: [10: 20, 20: 30])
        XCTAssertEqual(result, "Iniziare con 20 maglie e terminare a 30 maglie.")
    }
    
    func testStitchMapDoesNotAffectRows() {
        let text = "Lavorare 28 ferri a maglia rasata."
        // stitchMap non deve toccare "ferri", deve rimanere invariato
        let result = GeminiService.replaceNumbersInText(text, stitchMap: [28: 26])
        XCTAssertEqual(result, "Lavorare 28 ferri a maglia rasata.")
    }
    
    func testRowMapAffectsBothRowsAndStitches() {
        let textRows = "Lavorare 28 ferri."
        let textStitches = "Avviare 28 maglie."
        let resRows = GeminiService.replaceNumbersInText(textRows, rowMap: [28: 24])
        let resStitches = GeminiService.replaceNumbersInText(textStitches, rowMap: [28: 24])
        XCTAssertEqual(resRows, "Lavorare 24 ferri.")
        XCTAssertEqual(resStitches, "Avviare 24 maglie.")
    }
    
    func testRecalculateLocallyReturnsNilForZeroOrNegativeGauge() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Test Zero Gauge")
        let step = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Avvio",
            title: "Avvio Collo",
            instructionText: "Avviare 114 maglie",
            stitchCountBefore: 114,
            stitchCountAfter: 114,
            hasCounter: true,
            currentStepCount: 0
        )
        project.guideSteps.append(step)
        
        // Con input <= 0 deve restituire nil esplicitamente
        let responseZeroPattern = GeminiService.recalculateGuideLocally(
            project: project,
            patternStitches: 0.0,
            patternRows: 28.0,
            userStitches: 19.0,
            userRows: 26.0
        )
        XCTAssertNil(responseZeroPattern)
        
        let responseZeroUser = GeminiService.recalculateGuideLocally(
            project: project,
            patternStitches: 21.0,
            patternRows: 28.0,
            userStitches: 0.0,
            userRows: 26.0
        )
        XCTAssertNil(responseZeroUser)
        
        // Verifica che project.guideSteps non sia stato modificato
        XCTAssertEqual(project.guideSteps.count, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.guideSteps.first?.stitchCountBefore, 114)
    }
    
    func testChainContinuityAcrossNilAfterStep() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Test Catena con Step Info")
        let stepA = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Avvio Collo",
            title: "Avvio",
            instructionText: "Avviare 114 maglie",
            stitchCountBefore: 114,
            stitchCountAfter: 114,
            orderIndex: 0
        )
        let stepInfo = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Avvio Collo",
            title: "Info",
            instructionText: "Posizionare un marcapunto all'inizio del giro.",
            stitchCountBefore: nil,
            stitchCountAfter: nil,
            orderIndex: 1
        )
        let stepB = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Sprone",
            title: "Primo giro raglan",
            instructionText: "Lavorare su 114 maglie",
            stitchCountBefore: 114,
            stitchCountAfter: 122,
            orderIndex: 2
        )
        project.guideSteps.append(contentsOf: [stepA, stepInfo, stepB])
        
        // Ricalcolo locale con ratio 19 / 21:
        // 114 * 19 / 21 = 103.14 -> arrotondato a 103, ma per Collo/Avvio la parità impone 104!
        guard let response = GeminiService.recalculateGuideLocally(
            project: project,
            patternStitches: 21.0,
            patternRows: 28.0,
            userStitches: 19.0,
            userRows: 28.0
        ) else {
            XCTFail("Il ricalcolo locale non dovrebbe restituire nil")
            return
        }
        
        let genSteps = response.sections.flatMap(\.steps)
        XCTAssertEqual(genSteps.count, 3)
        // Step A after ha parità pari: 104
        XCTAssertEqual(genSteps[0].stitchCountAfter, 104)
        // Step info ha nil
        XCTAssertNil(genSteps[1].stitchCountAfter)
        // Step B before deve essere riconciliato con Step A after (104) nonostante il passaggio intermedio nil
        XCTAssertEqual(genSteps[2].stitchCountBefore, genSteps[0].stitchCountAfter)
        XCTAssertEqual(genSteps[2].stitchCountBefore, 104)
    }
    
    func testRecalculateLocallyReconcilesConsecutiveSteps() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Test Continuità Maglie")
        let step1 = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Sprone",
            title: "Passo 1",
            instructionText: "Lavorare 114 maglie fino a 140 maglie",
            stitchCountBefore: 114,
            stitchCountAfter: 140,
            orderIndex: 0
        )
        let step2 = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Sprone",
            title: "Passo 2",
            instructionText: "Lavorare con 140 maglie",
            stitchCountBefore: 140,
            stitchCountAfter: 140,
            orderIndex: 1
        )
        project.guideSteps.append(contentsOf: [step1, step2])
        
        // Ricalcola con ratio 19 / 21
        guard let response = GeminiService.recalculateGuideLocally(
            project: project,
            patternStitches: 21.0,
            patternRows: 28.0,
            userStitches: 19.0,
            userRows: 28.0
        ) else {
            XCTFail("Il ricalcolo locale non dovrebbe restituire nil")
            return
        }
        
        let genSteps = response.sections.flatMap(\.steps)
        XCTAssertEqual(genSteps.count, 2)
        // La continuità topologica garantisce che step2.before == step1.after
        XCTAssertEqual(genSteps[0].stitchCountAfter, genSteps[1].stitchCountBefore)
    }
    
    func testPreservationOfStateAfterInPlaceRecalculation() throws {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Maglione Test")
        let step = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Sprone",
            title: "Giro raglan",
            stepType: "single_action",
            instructionText: "Lavorare 114 maglie",
            orderIndex: 0,
            hasCounter: true,
            counterLabel: "Ferro",
            currentStepCount: 15,
            targetStepCount: 30
        )
        step.isCompleted = false
        step.completedAt = nil
        step.extraCounterValues = [15, 2]
        project.guideSteps.append(step)
        
        let genResponse = try XCTUnwrap(GeminiService.recalculateGuideLocally(
            project: project,
            patternStitches: 21.0,
            patternRows: 28.0,
            userStitches: 19.0,
            userRows: 26.0
        ))
        
        try GaugeGuideManager.applyRecalculatedGuide(to: project, result: genResponse)
        
        XCTAssertEqual(project.guideSteps.count, 1)
        let updatedStep = project.guideSteps[0]
        XCTAssertEqual(updatedStep.id, step.id)
        XCTAssertEqual(updatedStep.currentStepCount, 15)
        XCTAssertEqual(updatedStep.extraCounterValues, [15, 2])
        XCTAssertFalse(updatedStep.isCompleted)
        XCTAssertNil(updatedStep.completedAt)
        XCTAssertNotNil(project.originalGuideJSON)
        XCTAssertTrue(project.isGaugeRecalculated)
    }
    
    func testRestoreFromSnapshot() throws {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Pattern Qualsiasi")
        let step = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Bordo",
            title: "Coste 2x2",
            stepType: "single_action",
            instructionText: "Avviare 114 maglie",
            stitchCountBefore: 114,
            stitchCountAfter: 114,
            orderIndex: 0,
            hasCounter: true,
            counterLabel: "Ferri",
            currentStepCount: 5,
            targetStepCount: 10
        )
        project.guideSteps.append(step)
        
        let genResponse = try XCTUnwrap(GeminiService.recalculateGuideLocally(
            project: project,
            patternStitches: 21.0,
            patternRows: 28.0,
            userStitches: 19.0,
            userRows: 26.0
        ))
        
        try GaugeGuideManager.applyRecalculatedGuide(to: project, result: genResponse)
        XCTAssertTrue(project.isGaugeRecalculated)
        XCTAssertNotNil(project.originalGuideJSON)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.guideSteps[0].stitchCountBefore, 103)
        
        // Esegui restore da snapshot
        try GaugeGuideManager.restoreOriginalGuide(for: project)
        XCTAssertFalse(project.isGaugeRecalculated)
        XCTAssertNil(project.originalGuideJSON)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.guideSteps[0].stitchCountBefore, 114)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.guideSteps[0].currentStepCount, 5)
    }
    
    func testRestoreWithoutSnapshotFailsOnNonNoFrillsProject() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Sciarpa Semplice")
        let step = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Inizio",
            title: "Avvio",
            instructionText: "Avviare 40 maglie",
            stitchCountBefore: 40,
            stitchCountAfter: 40,
            orderIndex: 0
        )
        project.guideSteps.append(step)
        project.originalGuideJSON = nil
        
        XCTAssertThrowsError(try GaugeGuideManager.restoreOriginalGuide(for: project)) { error in
            guard let gaugeError = error as? GaugeRecalculationError,
                  case .noSnapshotAvailable = gaugeError else {
                XCTFail("Atteso errore noSnapshotAvailable, ricevuto: \(error)")
                return
            }
        }
        
        // Verifica che gli step non siano stati toccati
        XCTAssertEqual(project.guideSteps.count, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.guideSteps[0].stitchCountBefore, 40)
    }
    
    func testReorderedStepsWithSameCountDoesNotSwapCounters() throws {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Test Step Riordinati")
        let stepA = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Sezione 1",
            title: "Passo A",
            stepType: "single_action",
            instructionText: "Lavorare 100 maglie",
            orderIndex: 0,
            hasCounter: true,
            currentStepCount: 10,
            targetStepCount: 20
        )
        let stepB = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Sezione 2",
            title: "Passo B",
            stepType: "single_action",
            instructionText: "Lavorare 120 maglie",
            orderIndex: 1,
            hasCounter: true,
            currentStepCount: 50,
            targetStepCount: 60
        )
        project.guideSteps.append(contentsOf: [stepA, stepB])
        
        // Simula una risposta in cui i passaggi sono invertiti nell'array result, ma hanno stepId identici
        let genB = GeneratedStep(
            stepId: stepB.id.uuidString,
            title: "Passo B Ricalcolato",
            stepType: "single_action",
            instruction: "Lavorare 110 maglie",
            subSteps: [],
            repeatVariations: [],
            rowsPerRepeat: nil,
            totalRepeats: nil,
            stitchCountBefore: 110,
            stitchCountAfter: 110,
            targetMeasureCm: nil,
            hasCounter: true,
            counterLabel: "Ferri",
            targetStepCount: 55,
            sourcePage: nil,
            sourceQuote: nil
        )
        let genA = GeneratedStep(
            stepId: stepA.id.uuidString,
            title: "Passo A Ricalcolato",
            stepType: "single_action",
            instruction: "Lavorare 90 maglie",
            subSteps: [],
            repeatVariations: [],
            rowsPerRepeat: nil,
            totalRepeats: nil,
            stitchCountBefore: 90,
            stitchCountAfter: 90,
            targetMeasureCm: nil,
            hasCounter: true,
            counterLabel: "Ferri",
            targetStepCount: 18,
            sourcePage: nil,
            sourceQuote: nil
        )
        
        let invertedResponse = GeneratedGuideResponse(
            patternTitle: project.title,
            designer: project.designer,
            targetSize: project.selectedSize,
            gauge: nil,
            needles: nil,
            recalculationSummary: "Test Inverted",
            changedStepTitles: ["Passo B", "Passo A"],
            sections: [
                GeneratedSection(name: "Sezione 2", steps: [genB]),
                GeneratedSection(name: "Sezione 1", steps: [genA])
            ]
        )
        
        try GaugeGuideManager.applyRecalculatedGuide(to: project, result: invertedResponse)
        
        // Verifica che l'identità per stepId abbia mantenuto i contatori sui rispettivi step
        XCTAssertEqual(stepA.currentStepCount, 10)
        XCTAssertEqual(stepA.targetStepCount, 18)
        XCTAssertEqual(stepA.title, "Passo A Ricalcolato")
        
        XCTAssertEqual(stepB.currentStepCount, 50)
        XCTAssertEqual(stepB.targetStepCount, 55)
        XCTAssertEqual(stepB.title, "Passo B Ricalcolato")
    }
}

================================================================================
[21/30] FILE: PurlLabTests/GuideStepTests.swift
================================================================================
import XCTest
import SwiftData
@testable import PurlLab

final class GuideStepTests: XCTestCase {
    
    func testRepeatPatternTargetCalculation() {
        let step = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Scollo",
            title: "Ferri Accorciati",
            stepType: "repeat_pattern",
            instructionText: "German Short Rows",
            rowsPerRepeat: 4,
            totalRepeats: 5,
            hasCounter: true
        )
        
        let calculatedTarget = (step.rowsPerRepeat ?? 0) * (step.totalRepeats ?? 0)
        XCTAssertEqual(calculatedTarget, 20)
    }
    
    func testExtraCounters() {
        let step = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Manica",
            title: "Corpo Manica",
            instructionText: "Aumenti ogni 6 ferri",
            hasCounter: true,
            targetStepCount: 60
        )
        
        // Aggiungi contatore extra
        step.extraCounterLabels.append("Calature")
        step.extraCounterValues.append(0)
        
        XCTAssertEqual(step.extraCounterLabels.count, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(step.extraCounterValues.first, 0)
        
        // Incrementa
        step.extraCounterValues[0] += 1
        XCTAssertEqual(step.extraCounterValues[0], 1)
        
        // Decrementa con blocco a 0
        step.extraCounterValues[0] = max(0, step.extraCounterValues[0] - 2)
        XCTAssertEqual(step.extraCounterValues[0], 0)
    }
    
    func testCompletionToggle() {
        let step = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Bordo",
            instructionText: "Intreccia",
            isCompleted: false
        )
        XCTAssertFalse(step.isCompleted)
        
        step.isCompleted = true
        step.completedAt = Date()
        XCTAssertTrue(step.isCompleted)
        XCTAssertNotNil(step.completedAt)
    }
    
    func testCounterKindAndIsStitchCounter() {
        // 1. Esplicito counterKind = "stitch"
        let stitchStep = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Avvio",
            instructionText: "Avvia 114 maglie",
            counterLabel: "Avvio",
            counterKind: "stitch",
            currentStepCount: 114,
            targetStepCount: 114
        )
        XCTAssertTrue(stitchStep.isStitchCounter)
        
        // 2. Esplicito counterKind = "maglie" o "punto"
        let maglieStep = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Aumenti",
            instructionText: "Aumenta",
            counterKind: "maglie"
        )
        XCTAssertTrue(maglieStep.isStitchCounter)
        
        // 3. counterKind = "row"
        let rowStep = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Sprone",
            instructionText: "Lavora 20 ferri",
            counterLabel: "Ferri Sprone",
            counterKind: "row"
        )
        XCTAssertFalse(rowStep.isStitchCounter)
        
        // 4. Fallback su counterLabel contenente "maglie" quando counterKind è nil
        let legacyStitchStep = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Avvio",
            instructionText: "Avvia",
            counterLabel: "Maglie Avviate",
            counterKind: nil
        )
        XCTAssertTrue(legacyStitchStep.isStitchCounter)
        
        // 5. Fallback su counterLabel ferri quando counterKind è nil
        let legacyRowStep = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Bordo",
            instructionText: "Lavora",
            counterLabel: "Ferri a Coste",
            counterKind: nil
        )
        XCTAssertFalse(legacyRowStep.isStitchCounter)
    }
    
    func testSafeAccessorsPreventDetachedCrashes() {
        let step = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Test Detached",
            instructionText: "Istruzioni",
            subSteps: ["Passo 1", "Passo 2"],
            repeatVariations: ["Var 1"]
        )
        step.extraCounterLabels = ["Contatore 1"]
        step.extraCounterValues = [3]
        
        // Quando non collegato o eliminato, i safe accessors restituiscono liste vuote senza causare crash
        XCTAssertTrue(step.isDetachedOrDeleted)
        XCTAssertEqual(step.safeSubSteps, [])
        XCTAssertEqual(step.safeRepeatVariations, [])
        XCTAssertEqual(step.safeExtraCounterLabels, [])
        XCTAssertEqual(step.safeExtraCounterValues, [])
    }
}

import XCTest
import SwiftData
@testable import PurlLab

@MainActor
final class KnittingProjectTests: XCTestCase {
    
    private var container: ModelContainer!
    private var context: ModelContext!
    
    override func setUp() async throws {
        let schema = Schema([
            KnittingProject.self,
            GuideStep.self,
            RowCounter.self,
            TimerSession.self
        ])
        let configuration = ModelConfiguration(isStoredInMemoryOnly: true)
        container = try ModelContainer(for: schema, configurations: [configuration])
        context = container.mainContext
    }
    
    override func tearDown() async throws {
        WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.configure(with: nil)
        container = nil
        context = nil
    }
    
    func testTotalGuideRowsWorkedWithSteps() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Test Sciarpa")
        context.insert(project)
        
        let step1 = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Avvio", instructionText: "Avvia 4 m", orderIndex: 0, hasCounter: true, currentStepCount: 6, targetStepCount: 6)
        let step2 = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Aumenti", instructionText: "Aumenta", orderIndex: 1, hasCounter: true, currentStepCount: 24, targetStepCount: 160)
        
        step1.project = project
        step2.project = project
        project.guideSteps = [step1, step2]
        
        XCTAssertEqual(project.totalGuideRowsWorked, 30)
    }
    
    func testTotalGuideRowsWorkedExcludesStitchCounters() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Test Esclusione Maglie da Ferri")
        context.insert(project)
        
        // Step 1: Avvio maglie con counterKind = "stitch" (114 maglie avviate)
        let step1 = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Avvio",
            instructionText: "Avvia 114 maglie",
            orderIndex: 0,
            hasCounter: true,
            counterLabel: "Avvio",
            counterKind: "stitch",
            currentStepCount: 114,
            targetStepCount: 114
        )
        
        // Step 2: Bordo collo con counterKind = "row" (10 ferri lavorati su 20)
        let step2 = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Bordo Collo",
            instructionText: "Lavora a coste 1x1",
            orderIndex: 1,
            hasCounter: true,
            counterLabel: "Ferri",
            counterKind: "row",
            currentStepCount: 10,
            targetStepCount: 20
        )
        
        // Step 3: Ripresa maglie con counterKind nil ma counterLabel "Maglie Riprese"
        let step3 = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Bordo Manica",
            instructionText: "Riprendi 54 maglie",
            orderIndex: 2,
            hasCounter: true,
            counterLabel: "Maglie Riprese",
            counterKind: nil,
            currentStepCount: 54,
            targetStepCount: 54
        )
        
        step1.project = project
        step2.project = project
        step3.project = project
        project.guideSteps = [step1, step2, step3]
        
        // totalGuideRowsWorked deve contare SOLO i ferri (step2 = 10), escludendo step1 (114 maglie) e step3 (54 maglie)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.totalGuideRowsWorked, 10, "I contatori di maglie non devono essere sommati al totale dei ferri lavorati")
        
        // targetTotalRows deve contare SOLO l'obiettivo ferri (step2 = 20), escludendo step1 e step3
        XCTAssertEqual(project.targetTotalRows, 20, "I contatori di maglie non devono essere inclusi nell'obiettivo ferri totale")
    }
    
    func testTotalGuideRowsWorkedFallbackToGlobalCounter() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Test Progetto Manuale")
        context.insert(project)

        project.counters.append(globalCounter)

        XCTAssertEqual(project.totalGuideRowsWorked, 45, "Se non ci sono schede guida, deve fare fallback sul Contatore Globale")
    }
    
    func testTotalStitchesWorkedConstantStitches() {

        project.guideSteps = [step]

    func testTotalStitchesWorkedWithIncreases() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Aumenti Raglan")
        context.insert(project)
        
        // Da 100 a 140 maglie in 20 ferri, lavorati tutti e 20 i ferri
        let step = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Sprone",
            instructionText: "Aumenti raglan",
            stitchCountBefore: 100,
            stitchCountAfter: 140,
            orderIndex: 0,
            hasCounter: true,
            currentStepCount: 20,
            targetStepCount: 20
        )
        step.project = project
        project.guideSteps = [step]
        
        let total = project.totalStitchesWorked
        // Media ~120 maglie per 20 ferri = ~2400 maglie lavorate
        XCTAssertGreaterThan(total, 2300)
        XCTAssertLessThan(total, 2500)
