    func testTargetTotalRowsAndStitches() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Top-Down Sweater")
        context.insert(project)
        
        let step1 = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Collo",
            instructionText: "Coste 1/1",
            stitchCountBefore: 114,
            stitchCountAfter: 114,
            orderIndex: 0,
            hasCounter: true,
            currentStepCount: 0,
            targetStepCount: 20
        )
        let step2 = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Sprone",
            stepType: "repeat_pattern",
            instructionText: "Ripetizioni",
            rowsPerRepeat: 4,
            totalRepeats: 5, // 20 ferri
            stitchCountBefore: 114,
            stitchCountAfter: 154,
            orderIndex: 1,
            hasCounter: true,
            currentStepCount: 0
        )
        
        step1.project = project
        step2.project = project
        project.guideSteps = [step1, step2]
        
        XCTAssertEqual(project.targetTotalRows, 40)
        XCTAssertNotNil(project.targetTotalStitches)
        XCTAssertGreaterThan(project.targetTotalStitches ?? 0, 4000)
    }
    
    func testGlobalProgress() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Test Progresso")
        context.insert(project)
        
        let step = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Lavoro",
            instructionText: "Corpo",
            stitchCountBefore: 100,
            stitchCountAfter: 100,
            orderIndex: 0,
            hasCounter: true,
            currentStepCount: 50,
            targetStepCount: 100
        )
        step.project = project
        project.guideSteps = [step]
        
        // 50 ferri su 100 con maglie costanti -> esattamente 50%
        XCTAssertEqual(project.globalProgress, 0.5, accuracy: 0.01)
    }
    
    func testActiveWorkingStepPriority() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Test Priorità Scheda Attiva")
        context.insert(project)
        
        // Scheda 0: Info (senza contatore, non completata)
        let step0 = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Info", title: "Dettagli", instructionText: "Info testo", orderIndex: 0, hasCounter: false)
        // Scheda 1: Operativa completata
        let step1 = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Avvio", title: "Avvio", instructionText: "Avvia", isCompleted: true, orderIndex: 1, hasCounter: true, currentStepCount: 4, targetStepCount: 4)
        // Scheda 2: Operativa in corso (ha contatore, non completata)
        let step2 = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Lavoro", title: "Aumenti", instructionText: "Aumenta", isCompleted: false, orderIndex: 2, hasCounter: true, currentStepCount: 12, targetStepCount: 64)
        // Scheda 3: Operativa futura
        let step3 = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Chiusura", title: "Bordo", instructionText: "Chiudi", isCompleted: false, orderIndex: 3, hasCounter: true, currentStepCount: 0, targetStepCount: 8)
        
        step0.project = project
        step1.project = project
        step2.project = project
        step3.project = project
        project.guideSteps = [step0, step1, step2, step3]
        
        // 1. Deve privilegiare step2 (prima scheda non completata con contatore) rispetto a step0 (info)
        guard let active = project.activeWorkingStep() else {
            XCTFail("activeWorkingStep() non deve essere nil")
            return
        }
        XCTAssertEqual(active.id, step2.id)
        
        // 2. Se viene specificato un preferredStepId, deve rispettarlo
        guard let preferred = project.activeWorkingStep(preferredStepId: step3.id) else {
            XCTFail("preferred activeWorkingStep() non deve essere nil")
            return
        }
        XCTAssertEqual(preferred.id, step3.id)
    }
    
    func testWatchConnectivityManagerPreservesActiveStepAcrossNavigation() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Maglione Test")
        context.insert(project)
        
        let step1 = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Collo", instructionText: "Lavora il collo", orderIndex: 0, hasCounter: true)
        let step2 = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Sprone", instructionText: "Lavora gli aumenti", orderIndex: 1, hasCounter: true)
        let step3 = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Corpo", instructionText: "Lavora il corpo", orderIndex: 2, hasCounter: true)
        
        step1.project = project
        step2.project = project
        step3.project = project
        project.guideSteps = [step1, step2, step3]
        
        let manager = WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared
        manager.configure(with: context)
        
        // 1. Inizializza sul progetto
        manager.setActiveProject(project)
        XCTAssertEqual(manager.activeProjectId, project.id)
        XCTAssertEqual(manager.activeStepId, step1.id)
        
        // 2. L'utente o Apple Watch imposta lo step 2
        manager.setActiveStep(step2, for: project)
        XCTAssertEqual(manager.activeStepId, step2.id)
        
        // 3. Navigazione nell'app (es. apre tab Contatori o rientra nella schermata)
        manager.setActiveProject(project)
        
        // Deve rimanere rigorosamente su step2, senza azzerarsi a step1
        XCTAssertEqual(manager.activeStepId, step2.id, "setActiveProject non deve sovrascrivere lo step attivo se il progetto non è cambiato")
    }
    
    func testResetAllProgressRestartsProjectFromBeginning() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Maglione Test Restart")
        project.completedAt = Date()
        project.totalSecondsWorked = 3600
        context.insert(project)
        
        let session = TimerSession(startDate: Date().addingTimeInterval(-3600), endDate: Date(), durationSeconds: 3600)
        session.project = project
        project.timerSessions.append(session)
        
        let counter = RowCounter(title: "Contatore Globale", currentCount: 25)
        counter.project = project
        project.counters.append(counter)
        
        let step1 = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Avvio",
            instructionText: "Avvia 100 maglie",
            isCompleted: true,
            orderIndex: 0,
            hasCounter: true,
            counterLabel: "Avvio",
            counterKind: "stitch",
            currentStepCount: 100,
            targetStepCount: 100
        )
        step1.extraCounterLabels = ["Ripetizioni"]
        step1.extraCounterValues = [4]
        step1.completedAt = Date()
        
        let step2 = GuideStep(
            sectionTitle: "Corpo",
            instructionText: "Lavora 50 ferri",
            isCompleted: true,
            orderIndex: 1,
            hasCounter: true,
            currentStepCount: 50,
            targetStepCount: 50
        )
        step2.completedAt = Date()
        
        step1.project = project
        step2.project = project
        project.guideSteps = [step1, step2]
        
        try? context.save()
        
        XCTAssertNotNil(project.completedAt)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.totalSecondsWorked, 3600)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.timerSessions.count, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.counters.first?.currentCount, 25)
        XCTAssertEqual(step1.currentStepCount, 100)
        XCTAssertTrue(step1.isCompleted)
        XCTAssertEqual(step1.extraCounterValues, [4])
        
        // Esegui reset completo dei progressi
        project.resetAllProgress(modelContext: context)
        
        // Verifica azzeramento totale
        XCTAssertNil(project.completedAt, "completedAt deve essere resettato a nil")
        XCTAssertEqual(project.totalSecondsWorked, 0, "totalSecondsWorked deve essere 0")
        XCTAssertTrue(project.timerSessions.isEmpty, "timerSessions deve essere svuotato")
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 0, "I contatori manuali devono essere azzerati")
        
        XCTAssertEqual(step1.currentStepCount, 0, "Il contatore dello step 1 deve essere 0")
        XCTAssertFalse(step1.isCompleted, "Lo step 1 non deve risultare completato")
        XCTAssertNil(step1.completedAt, "completedAt dello step 1 deve essere nil")
        XCTAssertEqual(step1.extraCounterValues, [0], "Gli extra contatori devono essere azzerati a 0")
        
        XCTAssertEqual(step2.currentStepCount, 0, "Il contatore dello step 2 deve essere 0")
        XCTAssertFalse(step2.isCompleted, "Lo step 2 non deve risultare completato")
        XCTAssertNil(step2.completedAt, "completedAt dello step 2 deve essere nil")
        
        XCTAssertEqual(project.totalGuideRowsWorked, 0, "I ferri lavorati totali devono essere 0")
        XCTAssertEqual(project.totalStitchesWorked, 0, "Le maglie lavorate totali devono essere 0")
        XCTAssertEqual(project.globalProgress, 0.0, "Il progresso globale deve essere 0%")
        
        // Lo step attivo calcolato deve ritornare al primo step
        XCTAssertEqual(project.activeWorkingStep()?.id, step1.id, "Lo step attivo deve tornare al primo step")
    }
    
    func testHandleIncomingCommandNegativeOrOutOfBoundsExtraIndexDoesNotCrash() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Test Extra Index Safety")
        context.insert(project)
        
        let step = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Bordo", instructionText: "Ripeti", orderIndex: 0, hasCounter: true)
        step.extraCounterLabels = ["Giri Extra"]
        step.extraCounterValues = [5]
        step.project = project
        project.guideSteps = [step]
        try? context.save()
        
        let manager = WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared
        manager.configure(with: context)
        manager.setActiveProject(project)
        
        // 1. Indice negativo: NON deve mandare in crash l'app
        _ = manager.handleIncomingCommand(
            commandRaw: WatchCommandType.incrementExtraCounter.rawValue,
            projectId: project.id.uuidString,
            stepId: step.id.uuidString,
            newCount: nil,
            index: -1
        )
        XCTAssertEqual(step.extraCounterValues[0], 5, "Un indice negativo non deve alterare i contatori né causare crash")
        
        // 2. Indice fuori limite superiore: NON deve mandare in crash l'app
        _ = manager.handleIncomingCommand(
            commandRaw: WatchCommandType.incrementExtraCounter.rawValue,
            projectId: project.id.uuidString,
            stepId: step.id.uuidString,
            newCount: nil,
            index: 99
        )
        XCTAssertEqual(step.extraCounterValues[0], 5, "Un indice out-of-bounds non deve alterare i contatori")
        
        // 3. Indice valido: incrementa correttamente
        _ = manager.handleIncomingCommand(
            commandRaw: WatchCommandType.incrementExtraCounter.rawValue,
            projectId: project.id.uuidString,
            stepId: step.id.uuidString,
            newCount: nil,
            index: 0
        )
        XCTAssertEqual(step.extraCounterValues[0], 6)
    }
    
    func testHandleIncomingCommandClampsCountersToReasonableBounds() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Test Clamp Safety")
        context.insert(project)
        
        let step = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Corpo", instructionText: "Lavora", orderIndex: 0, hasCounter: true, currentStepCount: 0)
        step.extraCounterLabels = ["Extra"]
        step.extraCounterValues = [100_000]
        step.project = project
        project.guideSteps = [step]
        try? context.save()
        
        let manager = WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared
        manager.configure(with: context)
        manager.setActiveProject(project)
        
        // Imposta valore astronomico oltre 100_000: deve essere limitato a 100_000
        _ = manager.handleIncomingCommand(
            commandRaw: WatchCommandType.setMainCounter.rawValue,
            projectId: project.id.uuidString,
            stepId: step.id.uuidString,
            newCount: 999_999,
            index: nil
        )
        XCTAssertEqual(step.currentStepCount, 100_000)
        
        // Imposta valore negativo: deve essere limitato a 0
        _ = manager.handleIncomingCommand(
            commandRaw: WatchCommandType.setMainCounter.rawValue,
            projectId: project.id.uuidString,
            stepId: step.id.uuidString,
            newCount: -42,
            index: nil
        )
        XCTAssertEqual(step.currentStepCount, 0)
        
        // Incremento su extraCounter già a 100_000: non deve superare 100_000
        _ = manager.handleIncomingCommand(
            commandRaw: WatchCommandType.incrementExtraCounter.rawValue,
            projectId: project.id.uuidString,
            stepId: step.id.uuidString,
            newCount: nil,
            index: 0
        )
        XCTAssertEqual(step.extraCounterValues[0], 100_000)
    }
    
    func testHandleIncomingCommandRejectsNonExistentProjectIdWithoutApplying() {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Progetto Attivo Esistente")
        context.insert(project)
        
        let step = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Avvio", instructionText: "Avvia", orderIndex: 0, hasCounter: true, currentStepCount: 10)
        step.project = project
        project.guideSteps = [step]
        try? context.save()
        
        let manager = WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared
        manager.configure(with: context)
        manager.setActiveProject(project)
        
        let fakeUUID = UUID().uuidString
        
        // Invia comando con un projectId che non corrisponde ad alcun progetto
        let syncData = manager.handleIncomingCommand(
            commandRaw: WatchCommandType.incrementMainCounter.rawValue,
            projectId: fakeUUID,
            stepId: step.id.uuidString,
            newCount: nil,
            index: nil
        )
        
        // Deve ritornare syncData corrente e non applicare nulla
        XCTAssertNotNil(syncData["syncData"])
        XCTAssertEqual(step.currentStepCount, 10, "Il contatore del progetto non deve essere toccato quando il projectId non corrisponde")
        XCTAssertEqual(manager.activeProjectId, project.id, "activeProjectId non deve essere mutato verso un progetto inesistente")
    }
    
    func testHandleIncomingCommandReturnsNotReadyWhenModelContextIsNil() {
        let manager = WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared
        manager.configure(with: nil)
        
        let reply = manager.handleIncomingCommand(
            commandRaw: WatchCommandType.incrementMainCounter.rawValue,
            projectId: "some-project-id",
            stepId: nil,
            newCount: nil,
            index: nil
        )
        
        XCTAssertEqual(reply["error"] as? String, "notReady")
    }
    
    func testDeviceStoreCompatibility() throws {
        let storePath = "/tmp/purl_lab_test_store"
        guard FileManager.default.fileExists(atPath: storePath) else {
            throw XCTSkip("Store reale non trovato in \(storePath). Per ricrearlo da dispositivo collegato eseguire: xcrun devicectl device copy from --device <DEVICE_UUID> --domain-type appDataContainer --domain-identifier com.chiara.PurlLab --source Library/Application\\ Support/PurlLabV4.store --destination /tmp/purl_lab_test_store")
        }
        
        let tempDir = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString)
        try FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: tempDir, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        defer { try? FileManager.default.removeItem(at: tempDir) }
        
        let destinationStore = tempDir.appendingPathComponent("PurlLabV4.store")
        try FileManager.default.copyItem(at: URL(fileURLWithPath: storePath), to: destinationStore)
        
        let schema = Schema(versionedSchema: PurlLabSchemaV1.self)
        let config = ModelConfiguration("PurlLabV4", schema: schema, url: destinationStore)
        
        let testContainer = try ModelContainer(for: schema, configurations: [config])
        let fetchContext = testContainer.mainContext
        let projects = try fetchContext.fetch(FetchDescriptor<KnittingProject>())
        XCTAssertFalse(projects.isEmpty, "Lo store reale dell'iPhone deve caricare i progetti senza errori")
    }
}

================================================================================
[23/30] FILE: PurlLabTests/RowCounterTests.swift
================================================================================
import XCTest
import SwiftData
@testable import PurlLab

final class RowCounterTests: XCTestCase {
    
    func testInitialization() {
        let counter = RowCounter(title: "Contatore Globale")
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.title, "Contatore Globale")
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 0)
        XCTAssertNil(counter.targetCount)
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.stepIncrement, 1)
        XCTAssertFalse(counter.resetOnTarget)
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.orderIndex, 0)
    }
    
    func testIncrementStandard() {
        let counter = RowCounter(title: "Test", currentCount: 5)
        counter.increment()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 6)
        counter.increment()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 7)
    }
    
    func testIncrementCustomStep() {
        let counter = RowCounter(title: "Test", currentCount: 0, stepIncrement: 2)
        counter.increment()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 2)
        counter.increment()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 4)
    }
    
    func testDecrementStopsAtZero() {
        let counter = RowCounter(title: "Test", currentCount: 1)
        counter.decrement()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 0)
        counter.decrement()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 0, "Il contatore non deve diventare negativo")
    }
    
    func testDecrementCustomStep() {
        let counter = RowCounter(title: "Test", currentCount: 5, stepIncrement: 2)
        counter.decrement()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 3)
        counter.decrement()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 1)
        counter.decrement()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 0)
    }
    
    func testReset() {
        let counter = RowCounter(title: "Test", currentCount: 42)
        counter.reset()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 0)
    }
    
    func testTargetCountWithoutReset() {
        let counter = RowCounter(title: "Test", currentCount: 9, targetCount: 10, resetOnTarget: false)
        counter.increment()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 10)
        counter.increment()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 11, "Senza resetOnTarget deve continuare a contare")
    }
    
    func testTargetCountWithResetOnTarget() {
        let counter = RowCounter(title: "Test", currentCount: 9, targetCount: 10, resetOnTarget: true)
        counter.increment()
        XCTAssertEqual(counter.currentCount, 0, "Con resetOnTarget deve azzerarsi una volta raggiunto l'obiettivo")
    }
}

[24/30] FILE: PurlLabTests/TimerManagerTests.swift
================================================================================
import XCTest
import SwiftData
@testable import PurlLab

// MARK: - TestClock per test deterministici senza attese reali
final class TestClock: AppClock, @unchecked Sendable {
    private var internalNow: Date
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var pendingSleeps: [(deadline: Date, continuation: UnsafeContinuation<Void, Never>)] = []
    
    var now: Date {
        get { getNow() }
        set { setNow(newValue) }
    }
    
    private func getNow() -> Date {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        return internalNow
    }
    
    private func setNow(_ date: Date) {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        internalNow = date
    }
    
    init(startDate: Date = Date()) {
        self.internalNow = startDate
    }
    
    func advance(by seconds: TimeInterval) {
        let ready = advanceTimeAndExtractReady(by: seconds)
        for cont in ready {
            cont.resume()
        }
    }
    
    private func advanceTimeAndExtractReady(by seconds: TimeInterval) -> [UnsafeContinuation<Void, Never>] {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        internalNow = internalNow.addingTimeInterval(seconds)
        let current = internalNow
        var remaining: [(deadline: Date, continuation: UnsafeContinuation<Void, Never>)] = []
        var ready: [UnsafeContinuation<Void, Never>] = []
        for item in pendingSleeps {
            if item.deadline <= current {
                ready.append(item.continuation)
            } else {
                remaining.append(item)
            }
        }
        pendingSleeps = remaining
        return ready
    }
    
    private func addSleep(deadline: Date, continuation: UnsafeContinuation<Void, Never>) {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        pendingSleeps.append((deadline: deadline, continuation: continuation))
    }
    
    func sleep(nanoseconds: UInt64) async throws {
        let durationSeconds = Double(nanoseconds) / 1_000_000_000.0
        let currentNow = now
        let deadline = currentNow.addingTimeInterval(durationSeconds)
        
        await withUnsafeContinuation { continuation in
            addSleep(deadline: deadline, continuation: continuation)
        }
        try Task.checkCancellation()
    }
    
    func reset() {
        let all = removeAllPending()
        for cont in all {
            cont.resume()
        }
    }
    
    private func removeAllPending() -> [UnsafeContinuation<Void, Never>] {
        lock.lock()
        defer { lock.unlock() }
        let ready = pendingSleeps.map(\.continuation)
        pendingSleeps.removeAll()
        return ready
    }
}

@MainActor
final class TimerManagerTests: XCTestCase {
    
    private var container: ModelContainer!
    private var context: ModelContext!
    private var testClock: TestClock!
    
    override func setUp() {
        super.setUp()
        let schema = Schema([
            KnittingProject.self,
            TimerSession.self,
            RowCounter.self,
            GuideStep.self
        ])
        let configuration = ModelConfiguration(schema: schema, isStoredInMemoryOnly: true)
        container = try! ModelContainer(for: schema, configurations: [configuration])
        context = ModelContext(container)
        testClock = TestClock()
        TimerManager.shared.clock = testClock
        TimerManager.shared.configure(with: context)
        WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.configure(with: context)
    }
    
    override func tearDown() {
        _ = TimerManager.shared.pause()
        TimerManager.shared.clearPendingSessionsForTesting()
        TimerManager.shared.configure(with: nil)
        WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared.configure(with: nil)
        TimerManager.shared.autoPauseMinutes = 15.0
        TimerManager.shared.clock = SystemClock()
        testClock?.reset()
        testClock = nil
        container = nil
        context = nil
        super.tearDown()
    }
    
    func testFormatTimeUnderOneHour() {
        let manager = TimerManager.shared
        // 5 minuti e 22 secondi = 322s
        let formatted = manager.formatTime(322)
        XCTAssertEqual(formatted, "05m 22s")
    }
    
    func testFormatTimeOverOneHour() {
        let manager = TimerManager.shared
        // 1 ora, 15 minuti e 30 secondi = 4530s
        let formatted = manager.formatTime(4530)
        XCTAssertEqual(formatted, "1h 15m 30s")
    }
    
    func testFormatTimeZero() {
        let manager = TimerManager.shared
        let formatted = manager.formatTime(0)
        XCTAssertEqual(formatted, "00m 00s")
    }
    
    func testPausePersistsTimerSessionAndUpdatesProjectTotalSecondsWorked() async throws {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Timer Test Progetto")
        context.insert(project)
        try context.save()
        
        TimerManager.shared.start(for: project.id)
        XCTAssertTrue(TimerManager.shared.isRunning)
        XCTAssertEqual(TimerManager.shared.activeProjectId, project.id)
        
        testClock.advance(by: 30)
        
        let duration = TimerManager.shared.pause()
        XCTAssertEqual(duration, 30.0, accuracy: 0.001)
        XCTAssertFalse(TimerManager.shared.isRunning)
        XCTAssertNil(TimerManager.shared.activeProjectId)
        
        XCTAssertEqual(project.timerSessions.count, 1)
        let savedSession = try XCTUnwrap(project.timerSessions.first)
        XCTAssertEqual(savedSession.durationSeconds, 30.0, accuracy: 0.001)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.totalSecondsWorked, 30.0, accuracy: 0.001)
        XCTAssertNotNil(savedSession.endDate)
    }
    
    func testStartNewProjectAutoPausesAndPersistsPreviousProject() async throws {
        let projectA = KnittingProject(title: "Progetto A")
        let projectB = KnittingProject(title: "Progetto B")
        context.insert(projectA)
        context.insert(projectB)
        try context.save()
        
        TimerManager.shared.start(for: projectA.id)
        testClock.advance(by: 30)
        
        // Avviare il timer per il progetto B deve mettere in pausa A e persistere la sessione di A
        TimerManager.shared.start(for: projectB.id)
        XCTAssertEqual(TimerManager.shared.activeProjectId, projectB.id)
        XCTAssertEqual(projectA.timerSessions.count, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(projectA.totalSecondsWorked, 30.0, accuracy: 0.001)
        XCTAssertEqual(projectB.timerSessions.count, 0)
        
        testClock.advance(by: 45)
        TimerManager.shared.pause()
        
        XCTAssertEqual(projectB.timerSessions.count, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(projectB.totalSecondsWorked, 45.0, accuracy: 0.001)
    }
    
    func testRecalculateElapsedFromSessionStartDateNotFrozenValue() async throws {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Progetto Test Ricalcolo")
        context.insert(project)
        try context.save()
        
        TimerManager.shared.start(for: project.id)
        // Simula il timer congelato a 0 (es. tick non ancora scattato o app in background)
        TimerManager.shared.elapsedSecondsInCurrentSession = 0
        
        testClock.advance(by: 40)
        
        let duration = TimerManager.shared.pause()
        // Deve ricalcolare da sessionStartDate e non usare il valore 0 congelato
        XCTAssertEqual(duration, 40.0, accuracy: 0.001)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.totalSecondsWorked, 40.0, accuracy: 0.001)
    }
    
    func testAutoPausePersistsSessionWhenInactivityTimeoutExceeded() async throws {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Progetto Auto Pause")
        context.insert(project)
        try context.save()
        
        TimerManager.shared.autoPauseMinutes = 1.0 // 60s
        TimerManager.shared.start(for: project.id)
        
        // Simula sessione iniziata 5 minuti fa, con ultima interazione 2 minuti fa
        let now = testClock.now
        TimerManager.shared.sessionStartDate = now.addingTimeInterval(-300) // 5 min fa
        TimerManager.shared.lastInteractionDate = now.addingTimeInterval(-120) // 2 min fa
        
        // Simula il risveglio foreground che rileva il superamento del timeout (inattivo da 120s > 60s)
        TimerManager.shared.handleAppWillEnterForeground()
        
        XCTAssertFalse(TimerManager.shared.isRunning)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.timerSessions.count, 1)
        let session = try XCTUnwrap(project.timerSessions.first)
        // Lavoro accreditato: da -300s fino a (-120s + 60s) = -60s => 240s
        XCTAssertEqual(session.durationSeconds, 240, accuracy: 1.0)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.totalSecondsWorked, 240, accuracy: 1.0)
        let expectedEnd = now.addingTimeInterval(-60)
        XCTAssertEqual(session.endDate?.timeIntervalSince1970 ?? 0, expectedEnd.timeIntervalSince1970, accuracy: 1.0)
    }
    
    func testPausePreservesSessionAsPendingOnPersistFailureAndRetriesOnStartFlushOrForeground() async throws {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Progetto Test Pending")
        context.insert(project)
        try context.save()
        
        TimerManager.shared.start(for: project.id)
        testClock.advance(by: 30)
        
        // Simula disconnessione / assenza del modelContext durante la pausa
        TimerManager.shared.configure(with: nil)
        
        let duration = TimerManager.shared.pause()
        XCTAssertEqual(duration, 30.0, accuracy: 0.001)
        XCTAssertFalse(TimerManager.shared.isRunning)
        
        // La sessione non deve essere andata perduta: deve trovarsi in pendingSessions
        XCTAssertEqual(TimerManager.shared.pendingSessions.count, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.timerSessions.count, 0)
        
        // 1. Test recupero al foreground
        TimerManager.shared.configure(with: context)
        // Riconfigurando il context senza chiamare foreground la sessione viene già retriata da configure
        XCTAssertEqual(TimerManager.shared.pendingSessions.count, 0)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.timerSessions.count, 1)
        
        // 2. Test recupero al prossimo start
        TimerManager.shared.clearPendingSessionsForTesting()
        TimerManager.shared.start(for: project.id)
        testClock.advance(by: 20)
        TimerManager.shared.configure(with: nil)
        _ = TimerManager.shared.pause()
        XCTAssertEqual(TimerManager.shared.pendingSessions.count, 1)
        
        TimerManager.shared.configure(with: context)
        // Crea nuovo progetto e chiama start: deve svuotare le pendenti
        let project2 = KnittingProject(title: "Progetto 2")
        context.insert(project2)
        try context.save()
        TimerManager.shared.start(for: project2.id)
        XCTAssertEqual(TimerManager.shared.pendingSessions.count, 0)
        _ = TimerManager.shared.pause()
    }
    
    func testCancelTimerStopsTimerWithoutSavingDuration() async throws {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Progetto Test Cancel")
        context.insert(project)
        try context.save()
        
        TimerManager.shared.start(for: project.id)
        XCTAssertTrue(TimerManager.shared.isRunning)
        XCTAssertEqual(TimerManager.shared.activeProjectId, project.id)
        
        testClock.advance(by: 30)
        
        TimerManager.shared.cancelTimer(for: project.id)
        
        XCTAssertFalse(TimerManager.shared.isRunning)
        XCTAssertNil(TimerManager.shared.activeProjectId)
        XCTAssertEqual(TimerManager.shared.elapsedSecondsInCurrentSession, 0)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.totalSecondsWorked, 0)
        XCTAssertTrue(project.timerSessions.isEmpty)
    }
    
    func testFlushElapsedToTotalPreservesStartDateOnSaveFailure() async throws {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Progetto Test Flush Error")
        context.insert(project)
        try context.save()
        
        TimerManager.shared.start(for: project.id)
        guard let originalStart = TimerManager.shared.sessionStartDate else {
            XCTFail("sessionStartDate non impostato")
            return
        }
        
        testClock.advance(by: 20)
        
        // Simula disconnessione / assenza del modelContext (save impossibile)
        TimerManager.shared.configure(with: nil)
        
        let flushed = TimerManager.shared.flushElapsedToTotal()
        XCTAssertEqual(flushed, 0, "Se il save fallisce, flushElapsedToTotal deve restituire 0")
        XCTAssertEqual(TimerManager.shared.sessionStartDate, originalStart, "sessionStartDate non deve essere avanzato se il salvataggio fallisce")
        
        // Ripristina il modelContext: ora il flush deve avere successo e avanzare la data
        TimerManager.shared.configure(with: context)
        testClock.advance(by: 10)
        let successFlush = TimerManager.shared.flushElapsedToTotal()
        XCTAssertEqual(successFlush, 30.0, accuracy: 0.001)
        XCTAssertNotEqual(TimerManager.shared.sessionStartDate, originalStart)
        XCTAssertEqual(project.timerSessions.count, 1)
    }
    
    func testRetryPendingSessionsDiscardsSessionIfProjectDeleted() async throws {
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Progetto Da Eliminare")
        context.insert(project)
        try context.save()
        let projId = project.id
        
        TimerManager.shared.start(for: projId)
        testClock.advance(by: 25)
        
        // Simula disconnessione / assenza del modelContext durante la pausa
        TimerManager.shared.configure(with: nil)
        _ = TimerManager.shared.pause()
        
        // La sessione è trattenuta in pendingSessions per errore temporaneo
        XCTAssertEqual(TimerManager.shared.pendingSessions.count, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(TimerManager.shared.pendingSessions.first?.projectId, projId)
        
        // Ora l'utente elimina il progetto dall'archivio
        context.delete(project)
        try context.save()
        
        // Quando il modelContext torna disponibile, retryPendingSessions deve rilevare che il progetto non esiste più
        // e SCARTARE la sessione invece di trattenerla all'infinito
        TimerManager.shared.configure(with: context)
        
        XCTAssertTrue(TimerManager.shared.pendingSessions.isEmpty, "La sessione pendente per un progetto inesistente deve essere scartata dalla coda")
    }
}

import XCTest
import SwiftData
@testable import PurlLab

final class WatchModelsTests: XCTestCase {
    
    override func tearDown() async throws {
        try await super.tearDown()
    }
    
    func testWatchProjectSummaryCodable() throws {
        let summary = WatchProjectSummary(
            id: UUID().uuidString,
            title: "Sophie Scarf",
            designer: "PetiteKnit",
            selectedSize: "Piccola",
            totalSecondsWorked: 320,
            isTimerRunning: true,
            activeStepId: UUID().uuidString,
            currentSectionTitle: "Sciarpa",
            currentStepTitle: "Aumenti",
            currentStepIndex: 1,
            totalStepsCount: 3,
            isStepCompleted: false,
            counterLabel: "Ferri di Aumento",
            currentCount: 15,
            targetCount: 160,
            currentStitches: 25,
            stitchesBefore: 5,
            stitchesAfter: 25,
            canGoPrevious: true,
            canGoNext: true,
            totalStitchesWorked: 246,
            totalRowsWorked: 36,
            extraCounterLabels: ["Ripetizioni"],
            extraCounterValues: [2]
        )
        
        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        let data = try encoder.encode(summary)
        
        let decoder = JSONDecoder()
        let decoded = try decoder.decode(WatchProjectSummary.self, from: data)
        
        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.id, summary.id)
        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.title, summary.title)
        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.currentCount, 15)
        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.targetCount, 160)
        XCTAssertFalse(decoded.isTargetReached)
        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.stitchesDescription, "5 → 25 m (+20)")
        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.displaySubtitle, "Piccola · PetiteKnit")
    }
    
    func testWatchStitchesDescriptionExcludesZeroToStitches() throws {
        let summary = WatchProjectSummary(
            id: UUID().uuidString,
            title: "Test Cast On",
            designer: "PetiteKnit",
            selectedSize: "M",
            totalSecondsWorked: 0,
            isTimerRunning: false,
            activeStepId: nil,
            currentSectionTitle: "Avvio",
            currentStepTitle: "Avvio Maglie",
            currentStepIndex: 0,
            totalStepsCount: 1,
            isStepCompleted: false,
            counterLabel: "Maglie Avviate",
            currentCount: 114,
            targetCount: 114,
            currentStitches: 114,
            stitchesBefore: 0,
            stitchesAfter: 114,
            canGoPrevious: false,
            canGoNext: false,
            totalStitchesWorked: 114,
            totalRowsWorked: 0,
            extraCounterLabels: [],
            extraCounterValues: []
        )
        
        // Non deve mostrare "0 → 114 m (+114)", ma semplicemente "114 maglie"
        XCTAssertEqual(summary.stitchesDescription, "114 maglie")
    }
    
    func testWatchSyncStateCodable() throws {
        let summary = WatchProjectSummary(
            id: UUID().uuidString,
            title: "No Frills Sweater",
            designer: "PetiteKnit",
            selectedSize: "M",
            totalSecondsWorked: 1200,
            isTimerRunning: false,
            activeStepId: nil,
            currentSectionTitle: "Sprone",
            currentStepTitle: "Raglan",
            currentStepIndex: 0,
            totalStepsCount: 1,
            isStepCompleted: false,
            counterLabel: "Ferri",
            currentCount: 20,
            targetCount: 20,
            currentStitches: 154,
            stitchesBefore: 114,
            stitchesAfter: 154,
            canGoPrevious: false,
            canGoNext: false,
            totalStitchesWorked: 2680,
            totalRowsWorked: 20,
            extraCounterLabels: [],
            extraCounterValues: []
        )
        
        let state = WatchSyncState(
            projects: [summary],
            activeProjectId: summary.id,
            timestamp: Date()
        )
        
        let data = try JSONEncoder().encode(state)
        let decoded = try JSONDecoder().decode(WatchSyncState.self, from: data)
        
        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.projects.count, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(decoded.activeProjectId, summary.id)
        XCTAssertTrue(decoded.projects[0].isTargetReached)
    }
    
    func testWatchCommandTypes() throws {
        let commands: [WatchCommandType] = [
            .incrementMainCounter,
            .decrementMainCounter,
            .setMainCounter,
            .nextStep,
            .previousStep,
            .completeStep,
            .selectStep,
            .incrementExtraCounter,
            .decrementExtraCounter,
            .startTimer,
            .pauseTimer,
            .selectProject,
            .requestFullSync
        ]
        
        for cmd in commands {
            let data = try JSONEncoder().encode(cmd)
            let decoded = try JSONDecoder().decode(WatchCommandType.self, from: data)
            XCTAssertEqual(decoded, cmd)
        }
    }
    
    @MainActor
    func testWatchCommandDeduplication() {
        let manager = WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared
        let commandId = UUID().uuidString
        
        // Primo arrivo del comando: non deve essere duplicato
        let isFirstDuplicate = manager.isDuplicateCommand(commandId)
        XCTAssertFalse(isFirstDuplicate)
        
        // Secondo arrivo con lo stesso commandId (es. da retry transferUserInfo): deve essere identificato come duplicato
        let isSecondDuplicate = manager.isDuplicateCommand(commandId)
        XCTAssertTrue(isSecondDuplicate)
    }
    
    @MainActor
    func testPersistentCommandDeduplicationAcrossManagerRecreation() {
        let suiteName = "test_dedup_\(UUID().uuidString)"
        let testDefaults = UserDefaults(suiteName: suiteName)!
        defer {
            testDefaults.removePersistentDomain(forName: suiteName)
        }
        
        let manager1 = WatchConnectivityIOSManager(userDefaults: testDefaults, setupSession: false)
        let commandId = UUID().uuidString
        
        // Primo arrivo con il primo manager: non deve essere duplicato
        XCTAssertFalse(manager1.isDuplicateCommand(commandId))
        // Stesso manager, stesso commandId: duplicato
        XCTAssertTrue(manager1.isDuplicateCommand(commandId))
        
        // Simula riavvio dell'app ricreando un nuovo manager con le stesse UserDefaults
        let manager2 = WatchConnectivityIOSManager(userDefaults: testDefaults, setupSession: false)
        // Il comando elaborato prima del riavvio deve risultare duplicato
        XCTAssertTrue(manager2.isDuplicateCommand(commandId))
        
        // Un nuovo comando mai visto prima non deve essere duplicato
        let newCommandId = UUID().uuidString
        XCTAssertFalse(manager2.isDuplicateCommand(newCommandId))
    }
    
    @MainActor
    func testPersistentCommandDeduplicationLimitsTo200Commands() {
        let suiteName = "test_dedup_limit_\(UUID().uuidString)"
        let testDefaults = UserDefaults(suiteName: suiteName)!
        defer {
            testDefaults.removePersistentDomain(forName: suiteName)
        }
        
        let manager = WatchConnectivityIOSManager(userDefaults: testDefaults, setupSession: false)
        var ids: [String] = []
        for i in 0..<205 {
            let id = "cmd_\(i)"
            ids.append(id)
            _ = manager.isDuplicateCommand(id)
        }
        
        // Ricrea il manager per verificare cosa è stato salvato
        let managerRecreated = WatchConnectivityIOSManager(userDefaults: testDefaults, setupSession: false)
        // Gli ultimi 200 comandi (cmd_5 .. cmd_204) sono ancora presenti
        XCTAssertTrue(managerRecreated.isDuplicateCommand(ids[5]))
        XCTAssertTrue(managerRecreated.isDuplicateCommand(ids[204]))
        // I primi 5 comandi (cmd_0 .. cmd_4) sono stati scartati dalla finestra scorrevole di 200
        XCTAssertFalse(managerRecreated.hasProcessedCommand(ids[0]))
        XCTAssertFalse(managerRecreated.hasProcessedCommand(ids[4]))
    }
    
    @MainActor
    func testWatchCounterCommandTimestampOrdering() {
        let manager = WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared
        let stepKey = "test_step_\(UUID().uuidString)"
        
        let initialTimestamp: TimeInterval = 1000.0
        manager.recordAppliedTimestamp(stepKey: stepKey, timestamp: initialTimestamp)
        
        // Comando più recente (timestamp 1005 > 1000): NON obsoleto
        let isStaleNewer = manager.isStaleCounterCommand(stepKey: stepKey, timestamp: 1005.0)
        XCTAssertFalse(isStaleNewer)
        
        // Registra il nuovo timestamp
        manager.recordAppliedTimestamp(stepKey: stepKey, timestamp: 1005.0)
        
        // Comando ritardato arrivato fuori ordine (timestamp 1002 < 1005): DEVE essere scartato come obsoleto
        let isStaleOlder = manager.isStaleCounterCommand(stepKey: stepKey, timestamp: 1002.0)
        XCTAssertTrue(isStaleOlder)
    }
    
    @MainActor
    func testThreeInFlightCommandsRealignOnFinalReply() throws {
        let coordinator = WatchCommandCoordinator()
        let fakeTransport = FakeWatchTransport()
        fakeTransport.isReachable = true
        coordinator.transport = fakeTransport
        
        // Simula 3 comandi utente consecutivi in volo (es. tap rapidi +1, +1, +1)
        coordinator.sendCommand(.incrementMainCounter, projectId: "p1", data: ["newCount": 11])
        coordinator.sendCommand(.incrementMainCounter, projectId: "p1", data: ["newCount": 12])
        coordinator.sendCommand(.incrementMainCounter, projectId: "p1", data: ["newCount": 13])
        
        XCTAssertEqual(coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount, 3)
        XCTAssertEqual(fakeTransport.sentMessages.count, 3)
        
        // Simula la ricezione delle reply per ciascun comando
        let cmd1 = fakeTransport.sentMessages[0]["commandId"] as! String
        let cmd2 = fakeTransport.sentMessages[1]["commandId"] as! String
        let cmd3 = fakeTransport.sentMessages[2]["commandId"] as! String
        
        coordinator.completeInFlightCommand(commandId: cmd1)
        XCTAssertEqual(coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount, 2)
        
        coordinator.completeInFlightCommand(commandId: cmd2)
        XCTAssertEqual(coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount, 1)
        
        coordinator.completeInFlightCommand(commandId: cmd3)
        XCTAssertEqual(coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount, 0)
    }
    
    @MainActor
    func testCrownDebounceProtectsLocalCounterDuringRotation() throws {
        let coordinator = WatchCommandCoordinator()
        
        // Utente gira la corona: scatta il debounce
        coordinator.beginCrownDebounce()
        XCTAssertEqual(coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount, 1)
        
        // Durante la rotazione della corona (mentre debounce è attivo), inFlightCommandsCount > 0
        XCTAssertTrue(coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount > 0)
        
        // Fine debounce
        coordinator.endCrownDebounce()
        XCTAssertEqual(coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount, 0)
    }
    
    @MainActor
    func testWatchTransportErrorNotReadyFallbacksToTransferUserInfo() async throws {
        let coordinator = WatchCommandCoordinator()
        let fakeTransport = FakeWatchTransport()
        fakeTransport.isReachable = true
        fakeTransport.replyToReturn = ["error": "notReady"]
        coordinator.transport = fakeTransport
        
        let projId = UUID().uuidString
        coordinator.sendCommand(.incrementMainCounter, projectId: projId, data: ["newCount": 1])
        
        // Cede l'esecuzione al Task asincrono di risposta su MainActor senza attese reali
        for _ in 0..<5 {
            await Task.yield()
        }
        
        // 1. Il messaggio è stato inviato via sendMessage
        XCTAssertEqual(fakeTransport.sentMessages.count, 1)
        // 2. Poiché la risposta ha restituito error: notReady, deve essere scattato il fallback transferUserInfo
        XCTAssertEqual(fakeTransport.transferredUserInfos.count, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(fakeTransport.transferredUserInfos.first?["command"] as? String, WatchCommandType.incrementMainCounter.rawValue)
        // 3. Il contatore in volo deve essere riallineato a 0
        XCTAssertEqual(coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount, 0)
    }
    
    @MainActor
    func testInFlightCommandTimeoutDecrementsCountAndRequestsFullSync() async throws {
        let coordinator = WatchCommandCoordinator()
        let fakeTransport = FakeWatchTransport()
        fakeTransport.isReachable = true
        // Non impostiamo né replyToReturn né errorToReturn: il trasporto simula un blocco / mancata risposta
        fakeTransport.replyToReturn = nil
        fakeTransport.errorToReturn = nil
        coordinator.transport = fakeTransport
        let testClock = TestClock()
        coordinator.clock = testClock
        
        var fullSyncRequested = false
        coordinator.onFullSyncNeeded = {
            fullSyncRequested = true
        }
        
        let projId = UUID().uuidString
        coordinator.sendCommand(.incrementMainCounter, projectId: projId, data: ["newCount": 1])
        
        // Subito dopo l'invio, il comando è in volo
        XCTAssertEqual(coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(fakeTransport.sentMessages.count, 1)
        let originalCommandId = fakeTransport.sentMessages.first?["commandId"] as? String
        XCTAssertNotNil(originalCommandId)
        XCTAssertEqual(fakeTransport.sentMessages.first?["command"] as? String, WatchCommandType.incrementMainCounter.rawValue)
        
        // Cede l'esecuzione per consentire al timeoutTask di avviare l'attesa su clock.sleep
        for _ in 0..<5 {
            await Task.yield()
        }
        
        // Fai avanzare il TestClock di 8 secondi (tempo di timeout): nessuna attesa reale
        testClock.advance(by: 8.0)
        
        // Cede l'esecuzione per consentire l'elaborazione su MainActor del task di timeout
        for _ in 0..<5 {
            await Task.yield()
        }
        
        // Allo scadere:
        // 1. Il comando originale deve risultare reinviato via fallback (transferUserInfoFallback) con lo stesso commandId
        XCTAssertEqual(fakeTransport.transferredUserInfos.count, 1)
        XCTAssertEqual(fakeTransport.transferredUserInfos.first?["commandId"] as? String, originalCommandId)
        XCTAssertEqual(fakeTransport.transferredUserInfos.first?["command"] as? String, WatchCommandType.incrementMainCounter.rawValue)
        
        // 2. Viene poi invocato il callback onFullSyncNeeded per riallineare lo stato
        XCTAssertTrue(fullSyncRequested)
        
        // 3. Il contatore comandi in volo è decrementato
        XCTAssertEqual(coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount, 0)
        
        // Ripristina timeout di default a 8s e cancella comandi pendenti
        coordinator.resetForTesting()
    }
    
    @MainActor
    func testLostUpdateCounterDeltaHandling() {
        let testClock = TestClock()
        let fakeTransport = FakeWatchTransport()
        let coordinator = WatchCommandCoordinator(transport: fakeTransport, clock: testClock)
        
        let schema = Schema([KnittingProject.self, GuideStep.self, RowCounter.self, TimerSession.self])
        let config = ModelConfiguration(isStoredInMemoryOnly: true)
        let container = try! ModelContainer(for: schema, configurations: [config])
        let context = container.mainContext
        
        let project = KnittingProject(title: "Delta Test")
        let step = GuideStep(sectionTitle: "Sezione", instructionText: "Lavora", orderIndex: 0, hasCounter: true, currentStepCount: 6, targetStepCount: 50)
        project.guideSteps.append(step)
        context.insert(project)
        try! context.save()
        
        let manager = WatchConnectivityIOSManager.shared
        manager.configure(with: context)
        manager.setActiveProject(project)
        manager.setActiveStep(step, for: project)
        
        // (a) Watch genera comando incremento quando vedeva 6 (oldCount: 6, newCount: 7)
        // Viene accodato tramite fallback (es. trasporto offline o timeout)
        fakeTransport.isReachable = false
        coordinator.sendCommand(
            .incrementMainCounter,
            projectId: project.id.uuidString,
            data: ["newCount": 7, "oldCount": 6, "stepId": step.id.uuidString]
        )
        
        XCTAssertEqual(fakeTransport.transferredUserInfos.count, 1)
        let queuedMessage = fakeTransport.transferredUserInfos[0]
        let cmdId = queuedMessage["commandId"] as! String
        
        // Nel frattempo l'iPhone passa da 6 a 20
        step.currentStepCount = 20
        try! context.save()
        
        // Il comando in coda viene consegnato a iPhone
        _ = manager.handleIncomingCommand(
            commandRaw: queuedMessage["command"] as? String,
            projectId: queuedMessage["projectId"] as? String,
            stepId: queuedMessage["stepId"] as? String,
            newCount: queuedMessage["newCount"] as? Int,
            oldCount: queuedMessage["oldCount"] as? Int,
            index: queuedMessage["index"] as? Int,
            commandId: cmdId,
            timestamp: queuedMessage["timestamp"] as? TimeInterval
        )
        
        // Risultato deve essere 21 (20 + (7 - 6)), NON 6 o 7
        XCTAssertEqual(step.currentStepCount, 21, "Il comando in coda consegnato dopo che l'iPhone è passato da 6 a 20 deve risultare in 21")
        
        // (b) Stesso comando consegnato due volte -> applicato una sola volta
        _ = manager.handleIncomingCommand(
            commandRaw: queuedMessage["command"] as? String,
            projectId: queuedMessage["projectId"] as? String,
            stepId: queuedMessage["stepId"] as? String,
            newCount: queuedMessage["newCount"] as? Int,
            oldCount: queuedMessage["oldCount"] as? Int,
            index: queuedMessage["index"] as? Int,
            commandId: cmdId,
            timestamp: queuedMessage["timestamp"] as? TimeInterval
        )
        XCTAssertEqual(step.currentStepCount, 21, "Il comando ripetuto con lo stesso commandId deve essere applicato una sola volta")
        
        // (c) Corona 3->8 in ritardo con iPhone a 20 -> 25
        step.currentStepCount = 20
        try! context.save()
        
        let crownCmdId = UUID().uuidString
        _ = manager.handleIncomingCommand(
            commandRaw: WatchCommandType.setMainCounter.rawValue,
            projectId: project.id.uuidString,
            stepId: step.id.uuidString,
            newCount: 8,
            oldCount: 3,
            index: nil,
            commandId: crownCmdId,
            timestamp: testClock.now.timeIntervalSince1970
        )
        // 20 + (8 - 3) = 25
        XCTAssertEqual(step.currentStepCount, 25, "Rotazione corona 3->8 in ritardo con iPhone a 20 deve produrre 25")
    }
}

// MARK: - FakeWatchTransport per testing disaccoppiato da WCSession
final class FakeWatchTransport: @unchecked Sendable, WatchTransport {
    var isReachable: Bool = true
    var sentMessages: [[String: Any]] = []
    var transferredUserInfos: [[String: Any]] = []
    var replyToReturn: [String: Any]?
    var errorToReturn: Error?
    
    func sendMessage(_ message: [String: Any], replyHandler: (([String: Any]) -> Void)?, errorHandler: ((Error) -> Void)?) {
        sentMessages.append(message)
        if let error = errorToReturn {
            errorHandler?(error)
        } else if let reply = replyToReturn {
            replyHandler?(reply)
        }
    }
    
    func transferUserInfoFallback(_ userInfo: [String: Any]) {
        transferredUserInfos.append(userInfo)
    }
}

[26/30] FILE: PurlLabWatch/Models/WatchModels.swift

import Foundation

// MARK: - Clock Abstraction (iniettabile per test deterministici e immediati)
public protocol AppClock: Sendable {
    var now: Date { get }
    func sleep(nanoseconds: UInt64) async throws
}

public struct SystemClock: AppClock {
    public init() {}
    public var now: Date { Date() }
    public func sleep(nanoseconds: UInt64) async throws {
        try await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: nanoseconds)
    }
}

// MARK: - Watch Message Payload
struct WatchMessagePayload: @unchecked Sendable {
    let message: [String: Any]
    init(message: [String: Any]) {
        self.message = message
    }
}

// MARK: - Watch Transport Protocol (per testing e disaccoppiamento)
protocol WatchTransport: AnyObject, Sendable {
    var isReachable: Bool { get }
    func sendMessage(_ message: [String: Any], replyHandler: (([String: Any]) -> Void)?, errorHandler: ((Error) -> Void)?)
    func transferUserInfoFallback(_ userInfo: [String: Any])
}

// MARK: - Watch Command Coordinator
/// Coordinatore puro (senza dipendenze da WatchKit o WCSession) della logica di invio comandi,
/// tracking comandi in volo, deduplicazione, timeout di 8s con fallback e crown debounce.
@MainActor
final class WatchCommandCoordinator {
    private(set) var inFlightCommandsCount: Int = 0
    var transport: (any WatchTransport)?
    var clock: any AppClock = SystemClock()
    var inFlightCommandTimeoutNanoseconds: UInt64 = 8_000_000_000
    
    private var inFlightTimeoutTasks: [String: Task<Void, Never>] = [:]
    private var activeInFlightCommandIds = Set<String>()
    
    var onFullSyncNeeded: (@MainActor () -> Void)?
    var onSyncDataReceived: (@MainActor (Data) -> Void)?
    
    init(transport: (any WatchTransport)? = nil, clock: any AppClock = SystemClock()) {
        self.transport = transport
        self.clock = clock
    }
    
    func beginCrownDebounce() {
        inFlightCommandsCount += 1
    }
    
    func endCrownDebounce() {
        inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
    }
    
    func completeInFlightCommand(commandId: String) {
        guard activeInFlightCommandIds.remove(commandId) != nil else { return }
        if let task = inFlightTimeoutTasks.removeValue(forKey: commandId) {
            task.cancel()
        }
        inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
    }
    
    func resetForTesting() {
        for (_, task) in inFlightTimeoutTasks {
            task.cancel()
        }
        inFlightTimeoutTasks.removeAll()
        activeInFlightCommandIds.removeAll()
        inFlightCommandsCount = 0
        inFlightCommandTimeoutNanoseconds = 8_000_000_000
        clock = SystemClock()
    }
    
    func sendCommand(
        _ command: WatchCommandType,
        projectId: String,
        data: [String: Any] = [:]
    ) {
        guard let transport = self.transport else { return }
        let commandId = UUID().uuidString
        
        var message: [String: Any] = [
            "commandId": commandId,
            "command": command.rawValue,
            "projectId": projectId,
            "timestamp": clock.now.timeIntervalSince1970
        ]
        for (k, v) in data { message[k] = v }
        
        inFlightCommandsCount += 1
        activeInFlightCommandIds.insert(commandId)
        
        let payload = WatchMessagePayload(message: message)
        if transport.isReachable {
            let timeoutTask = Task { @MainActor [weak self] in
                try? await (self?.clock ?? SystemClock()).sleep(nanoseconds: self?.inFlightCommandTimeoutNanoseconds ?? 8_000_000_000)
                guard !Task.isCancelled, let self = self else { return }
                if self.activeInFlightCommandIds.remove(commandId) != nil {
                    self.inFlightTimeoutTasks.removeValue(forKey: commandId)
                    self.inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, self.inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
                    // Fallback transferUserInfo allo scadere del timeout prima di requestFullSync
                    transport.transferUserInfoFallback(message)
                    if command != .requestFullSync {
                        self.onFullSyncNeeded?()
                    }
                }
            }
            inFlightTimeoutTasks[commandId] = timeoutTask
            Self.sendSessionMessageReachable(transport, payload: payload, coordinator: self)
        } else {
            activeInFlightCommandIds.remove(commandId)
            inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
            transport.transferUserInfoFallback(message)
        }
    }
    
    private nonisolated static func sendSessionMessageReachable(
        _ transport: any WatchTransport,
        payload: WatchMessagePayload,
        coordinator: WatchCommandCoordinator?
    ) {
        transport.sendMessage(payload.message, replyHandler: { reply in
            let syncData = reply["syncData"] as? Data
            let error = reply["error"] as? String
            let cmdId = payload.message["commandId"] as? String
            Task { @MainActor in
                guard let coordinator = coordinator else { return }
                if let cmdId = cmdId {
                    coordinator.completeInFlightCommand(commandId: cmdId)
                } else {
                    coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
                }
                
                if let _ = error {
                    // iPhone ha restituito errore (es. notReady): fallback in background per non perdere il comando
                    transport.transferUserInfoFallback(payload.message)
                } else if let syncData = syncData {
                    coordinator.onSyncDataReceived?(syncData)
                }
            }
        }, errorHandler: { _ in
            let cmdId = payload.message["commandId"] as? String
            Task { @MainActor in
                guard let coordinator = coordinator else { return }
                if let cmdId = cmdId {
                    coordinator.completeInFlightCommand(commandId: cmdId)
                } else {
                    coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount = max(0, coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount - 1)
                }
            }
            transport.transferUserInfoFallback(payload.message)
        })
    }
}

/// Modello sintetico di un progetto da visualizzare e controllare su Apple Watch
struct WatchProjectSummary: Codable, Identifiable, Sendable {
    let id: String
    var title: String
    var designer: String
    var selectedSize: String
    var totalSecondsWorked: TimeInterval
    var isTimerRunning: Bool
    
    // Scheda e Sezione Operativa Attualmente Lavorata
    var activeStepId: String?
    var currentSectionTitle: String
    var currentStepTitle: String
    var currentStepIndex: Int
    var totalStepsCount: Int
    var isStepCompleted: Bool
    
    // Contatore specifico della sezione
    var counterLabel: String
    var currentCount: Int
    var targetCount: Int?
    
    // Maglie attuali sul ferro per questa sezione
    var currentStitches: Int?
    var stitchesBefore: Int?
    var stitchesAfter: Int?
    
    // Navigazione tra schede
    var canGoPrevious: Bool
    var canGoNext: Bool
    
    // Statistiche globali di progetto (maglie e ferri totali)
    var totalStitchesWorked: Int
    var totalRowsWorked: Int
    
    // Eventuali contatori aggiuntivi sulla scheda
    var extraCounterLabels: [String]
    var extraCounterValues: [Int]
    
    var isTargetReached: Bool {
        if let target = targetCount, target > 0 {
            return currentCount >= target
        }
        return false
    }
    
    var displaySubtitle: String {
        let size = selectedSize.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
        return size.isEmpty ? designer : (designer.isEmpty ? size : "\(size) · \(designer)")
    }
    
    /// Testo sintetico sulle maglie (es. "114 maglie" o "4 → 8 maglie", evitando la pill "0 → X")
    var stitchesDescription: String? {
        if let before = stitchesBefore, let after = stitchesAfter, before > 0, before != after {
            let diff = after - before
            let diffStr = diff > 0 ? "+\(diff)" : "\(diff)"
            return "\(before) → \(after) m (\(diffStr))"
        } else if let sts = currentStitches ?? stitchesAfter, sts > 0 {
            return "\(sts) maglie"
        }
        return nil
    }
}

/// Contenitore di sincronizzazione trasmesso via WCSession
struct WatchSyncState: Codable, Sendable {
    var projects: [WatchProjectSummary]
    var activeProjectId: String?
    var timestamp: Date
}

/// Tipi di comandi inviati da Apple Watch a iPhone
enum WatchCommandType: String, Codable {
    case incrementMainCounter
    case decrementMainCounter
    case setMainCounter
    case nextStep
    case previousStep
    case completeStep
    case selectStep
    case incrementExtraCounter
    case decrementExtraCounter
    case startTimer
    case pauseTimer
    case selectProject
    case requestFullSync
}

import SwiftUI

@main
struct PurlLabWatchApp: App {
    @Environment(\.scenePhase) private var scenePhase
    
    var body: some Scene {
        WindowGroup {
            WatchCounterView()
        }
        .onChange(of: scenePhase) { _, newPhase in
            if newPhase == .background || newPhase == .inactive {
                WatchConnectivityManager.shared.saveLocalCache()
            }
        }
    }
}

[28/30] FILE: PurlLabWatch/Services/WatchConnectivityManager.swift

import Foundation
import WatchConnectivity
import WatchKit
import SwiftUI

private func playHaptic(_ type: WKHapticType) {
    WKInterfaceDevice.current().play(type)
}

extension WCSession: @retroactive @unchecked Sendable, WatchTransport {
    func transferUserInfoFallback(_ userInfo: [String: Any]) {
        _ = self.transferUserInfo(userInfo)
    }
}

@MainActor
@Observable
final class WatchConnectivityManager: NSObject, WCSessionDelegate {
    static let shared = WatchConnectivityManager()
    
    let coordinator = WatchCommandCoordinator()
    
    var projects: [WatchProjectSummary] = []
    var activeProjectId: String? = nil
    var isConnectedToPhone: Bool = false
    var lastSyncDate: Date? = nil
    
    var inFlightCommandsCount: Int {
        coordinator.inFlightCommandsCount
    }
    
    var transport: (any WatchTransport)? {
        get { coordinator.transport }
        set { coordinator.transport = newValue }
    }
    
    var clock: any AppClock {
        get { coordinator.clock }
        set { coordinator.clock = newValue }
    }
    
    var inFlightCommandTimeoutNanoseconds: UInt64 {
        get { coordinator.inFlightCommandTimeoutNanoseconds }
        set { coordinator.inFlightCommandTimeoutNanoseconds = newValue }
    }
    
    private let userDefaultsKey = "purl_lab_watch_cache_v2"
    
    var activeProject: WatchProjectSummary? {
        if let id = activeProjectId {
            return projects.first(where: { $0.id == id })
        }
        return projects.first
    }
    
    override private init() {
        super.init()
        if WCSession.isSupported() {
            coordinator.transport = WCSession.default
        }
        coordinator.onFullSyncNeeded = { [weak self] in
            self?.requestFullSync()
        }
        coordinator.onSyncDataReceived = { [weak self] data in
            self?.handleSyncData(data)
        }
        loadLocalCache()
        setupWCSession()
    }
    
    // MARK: - WCSession Setup
    private func setupWCSession() {
        guard WCSession.isSupported() else { return }
        let session = WCSession.default
        session.delegate = self
        session.activate()
    }
    
    // MARK: - Azioni Utente (Apple Watch -> iPhone)
    
    /// Incrementa il contatore della sezione (+1) con risposta aptica istantanea
    func incrementMainCounter() {
        guard var current = activeProject else { return }
        let oldCount = current.currentCount
        current.currentCount += 1
        current.totalRowsWorked += 1
        if let sts = current.currentStitches, sts > 0 {
            current.totalStitchesWorked += sts
        }
        updateProjectLocally(current)
        
        // Haptic click piacevole per ogni ferro
        if current.isTargetReached {
            playHaptic(.success)
        } else {
            playHaptic(.click)
        }
        
        var payload: [String: Any] = [
            "newCount": current.currentCount,
            "oldCount": oldCount
        ]
        if let stepId = current.activeStepId {
            payload["stepId"] = stepId
        }
        
        sendCommand(.incrementMainCounter, projectId: current.id, data: payload)
    }
    
    /// Decrementa il contatore della sezione (-1)
    func decrementMainCounter() {
        guard var current = activeProject, current.currentCount > 0 else { return }
        let oldCount = current.currentCount
        current.currentCount -= 1
        if current.totalRowsWorked > 0 { current.totalRowsWorked -= 1 }
        updateProjectLocally(current)
        playHaptic(.directionDown)
        
        var payload: [String: Any] = [
            "newCount": current.currentCount,
            "oldCount": oldCount
        ]
        if let stepId = current.activeStepId {
            payload["stepId"] = stepId
        }
        
        sendCommand(.decrementMainCounter, projectId: current.id, data: payload)
    }
    
    /// Aggiorna il contatore solo in locale (es. per rotazione Digital Crown con aggiornamento immediato UI)
    func updateMainCounterLocally(to value: Int) {
        guard var current = activeProject, value >= 0, current.currentCount != value else { return }
        current.currentCount = value
        updateProjectLocally(current)
    }
    
    /// Invia il comando di impostazione contatore (con oldCount per applicazione delta) a iPhone
    func sendSetMainCounterCommand(to value: Int, oldCount: Int? = nil) {
        guard let current = activeProject, value >= 0 else { return }
        let effectiveOldCount = oldCount ?? current.currentCount
        var payload: [String: Any] = [
            "newCount": value,
            "oldCount": effectiveOldCount
        ]
        if let stepId = current.activeStepId {
            payload["stepId"] = stepId
        }
        sendCommand(.setMainCounter, projectId: current.id, data: payload)
    }
    
    /// Imposta direttamente un valore (es. tramite input manuale)
    func setMainCounter(to value: Int) {
        guard var current = activeProject, value >= 0, current.currentCount != value else { return }
        let oldCount = current.currentCount
        current.currentCount = value
        updateProjectLocally(current)
        playHaptic(.click)
        
        var payload: [String: Any] = [
            "newCount": value,
            "oldCount": oldCount
        ]
        if let stepId = current.activeStepId {
            payload["stepId"] = stepId
        }
        
        sendCommand(.setMainCounter, projectId: current.id, data: payload)
    }
    
    /// Passa alla scheda/sezione successiva della guida
    func nextStep() {
        guard let current = activeProject, current.canGoNext else { return }
        playHaptic(.click)
        var payload: [String: Any] = [:]
        if let stepId = current.activeStepId { payload["stepId"] = stepId }
        sendCommand(.nextStep, projectId: current.id, data: payload)
    }
    
    /// Torna alla scheda/sezione precedente della guida
    func previousStep() {
        guard let current = activeProject, current.canGoPrevious else { return }
        playHaptic(.click)
        var payload: [String: Any] = [:]
        if let stepId = current.activeStepId { payload["stepId"] = stepId }
        sendCommand(.previousStep, projectId: current.id, data: payload)
    }
    
    /// Segna la scheda corrente come completata e avanza alla successiva
    func completeStep() {
        guard let current = activeProject else { return }
        playHaptic(.success)
        var payload: [String: Any] = [:]
        if let stepId = current.activeStepId { payload["stepId"] = stepId }
        sendCommand(.completeStep, projectId: current.id, data: payload)
    }
    
    /// Toggle Timer Sessione (Play / Pausa) sincronizzato con iPhone
    func toggleTimer() {
        guard var current = activeProject else { return }
        let newRunningState = !current.isTimerRunning
        current.isTimerRunning = newRunningState
        updateProjectLocally(current)
        
        playHaptic(newRunningState ? .start : .stop)
        
        sendCommand(
            newRunningState ? .startTimer : .pauseTimer,
            projectId: current.id,
            data: [:]
        )
    }
    
    /// Seleziona un progetto attivo
    func selectProject(id: String) {
        activeProjectId = id
        saveLocalCache()
        playHaptic(.click)
        sendCommand(.selectProject, projectId: id, data: [:])
    }
    
    /// Richiede sincronizzazione completa a iPhone
    func requestFullSync() {
        sendCommand(.requestFullSync, projectId: activeProjectId ?? "", data: [:])
    }
    
    func requestSyncFromPhone() {
        requestFullSync()
    }
    
    /// Segnala l'inizio del debounce della corona contando il periodo come "in volo" (Patch 2b)
    func beginCrownDebounce() {
        coordinator.beginCrownDebounce()
    }
    
    /// Segnala la fine del debounce della corona
    func endCrownDebounce() {
        coordinator.endCrownDebounce()
    }
    
    /// Rimuove il task di timeout e decrementa il contatore in volo
    func completeInFlightCommand(commandId: String) {
        coordinator.completeInFlightCommand(commandId: commandId)
    }
    
    func resetInFlightForTesting() {
        coordinator.resetForTesting()
    }
    
    // MARK: - Invio Comandi a iPhone via WatchConnectivity
    private func sendCommand(_ command: WatchCommandType, projectId: String, data: [String: Any]) {
        coordinator.sendCommand(command, projectId: projectId, data: data)
    }
    
    // MARK: - Aggiornamento Locale & Cache
    func updateProjectLocally(_ updated: WatchProjectSummary) {
        if let idx = projects.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == updated.id }) {
            projects[idx] = updated
        } else {
            projects.append(updated)
        }
        debounceSaveLocalCache()
    }
    
    private var saveCacheTask: Task<Void, Never>?
    
    func debounceSaveLocalCache() {
        saveCacheTask?.cancel()
        saveCacheTask = Task { @MainActor in
            try? await Task.sleep(nanoseconds: 300_000_000)
            guard !Task.isCancelled else { return }
            self.saveLocalCache()
        }
    }
    
    func saveLocalCache() {
        let state = WatchSyncState(
            projects: projects,
            activeProjectId: activeProjectId,
            timestamp: Date()
        )
        if let encoded = try? JSONEncoder().encode(state) {
            UserDefaults.standard.set(encoded, forKey: userDefaultsKey)
        }
    }
    
    private func loadLocalCache() {
        guard let data = UserDefaults.standard.data(forKey: userDefaultsKey),
              let state = try? JSONDecoder().decode(WatchSyncState.self, from: data) else {
            return
        }
        self.projects = state.projects
        self.activeProjectId = state.activeProjectId
    }
    
    func handleSyncData(_ data: Data) {
        guard let state = try? JSONDecoder().decode(WatchSyncState.self, from: data) else {
            return
        }
        // Confronto timestamp (ALTO-05): scarta pacchetti obsoleti arrivati in ritardo
        if let last = self.lastSyncDate, state.timestamp < last {
            return
        }
        
        // Se ci sono comandi in volo residui DOPO il decremento, proteggi il contatore del progetto attivo locale
        // per evitare che risposte intermedie o sync ritardate sovrascrivano i tap rapidi dell'utente (Patch 2a/2b).
        // Se inFlightCommandsCount == 0 (es. reply dell'ultimo comando in volo),
        // accetta integralmente lo stato dell'iPhone riallineando currentCount, totali e step attivo.
        if inFlightCommandsCount > 0, let activeId = self.activeProjectId,
           let localActive = self.projects.first(where: { $0.id == activeId }) {
            var updatedProjects = state.projects
            if let idx = updatedProjects.firstIndex(where: { $0.id == activeId }) {
                updatedProjects[idx].currentCount = localActive.currentCount
                updatedProjects[idx].totalRowsWorked = localActive.totalRowsWorked
                updatedProjects[idx].totalStitchesWorked = localActive.totalStitchesWorked
            }
            self.projects = updatedProjects
        } else {
            self.projects = state.projects
        }
        
        if let newActive = state.activeProjectId {
            self.activeProjectId = newActive
        }
        self.lastSyncDate = state.timestamp
        self.debounceSaveLocalCache()
    }
    
    // MARK: - WCSessionDelegate
    nonisolated func session(_ session: WCSession, activationDidCompleteWith activationState: WCSessionActivationState, error: Error?) {
        let isReachable = session.isReachable
        Task { @MainActor in
            self.isConnectedToPhone = isReachable
            if isReachable {
                self.requestSyncFromPhone()
            }
        }
    }
    
    nonisolated func sessionReachabilityDidChange(_ session: WCSession) {
        let isReachable = session.isReachable
        Task { @MainActor in
            self.isConnectedToPhone = isReachable
            if isReachable {
                self.requestSyncFromPhone()
            }
        }
    }
    
    nonisolated func session(_ session: WCSession, didReceiveApplicationContext applicationContext: [String : Any]) {
        let syncData = applicationContext["syncData"] as? Data
        Task { @MainActor in
            if let syncData {
                self.handleSyncData(syncData)
            }
        }
    }
    
    nonisolated func session(_ session: WCSession, didReceiveMessage message: [String : Any]) {
        let syncData = message["syncData"] as? Data
        Task { @MainActor in
            if let syncData {
                self.handleSyncData(syncData)
            }
        }
    }
    
    nonisolated func session(_ session: WCSession, didReceiveUserInfo userInfo: [String : Any] = [:]) {
        let syncData = userInfo["syncData"] as? Data
        Task { @MainActor in
            if let syncData {
                self.handleSyncData(syncData)
            }
        }
    }
}
